Lanjut ke **Seri 3/12 — Basic Activities & Task Types**. Fokusnya: mengenali jenis task BPMN, kapan dipakai, dan bagaimana mapping-nya ke Java/Jersey/Camunda/PostgreSQL secara praktis.

# Seri 3/12 - Basic Activities & Task Types

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas **activity/task symbols** dalam BPMN secara praktis:

```text id="v3exfx"
Task
User Task
Service Task
Send Task
Receive Task
Business Rule Task
Script Task
Manual Task
Undefined Task
```

Di Camunda 8, task adalah unit kerja dasar dalam process. Dokumentasi Camunda menyebut task sebagai atomic unit of work; ketika token mencapai task tertentu, token berhenti, job dibuat, worker menjalankan pekerjaan, lalu token lanjut setelah handler menyelesaikan pekerjaan. Ini terutama berlaku untuk automated task berbasis job worker. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/tasks/))

Mental model utamanya:

```text id="x7lg4a"
Task = work unit
Task type = semantic intent
Implementation = worker / human task / message wait / DMN / pass-through
```

---

# 2. Diagram Decomposition

```text id="ke4suz"
BPMN Activities / Task Types
│
├── 1. Generic / Undefined Task
│   ├── Conceptual work item
│   ├── Early modeling
│   └── Not yet implementation-specific
│
├── 2. Service Task
│   ├── Automated system work
│   ├── Java worker
│   ├── REST call
│   ├── DB operation
│   ├── External integration
│   └── Retry / incident / idempotency
│
├── 3. User Task
│   ├── Human work assisted by system
│   ├── Tasklist / custom UI
│   ├── Assignment
│   ├── Candidate users/groups
│   ├── Form
│   └── Completion variables
│
├── 4. Manual Task
│   ├── Human work outside engine
│   ├── No UI/task lifecycle
│   └── Documentation-only / pass-through
│
├── 5. Send Task
│   ├── Send message outward
│   ├── Email / Kafka / REST command
│   ├── Notification
│   └── Outbound integration worker
│
├── 6. Receive Task
│   ├── Wait for message
│   ├── Callback
│   ├── Correlation key
│   └── Async response
│
├── 7. Business Rule Task
│   ├── DMN decision
│   ├── Risk score
│   ├── Eligibility
│   ├── Classification
│   └── Rule versioning
│
└── 8. Script Task
    ├── Lightweight expression/transformation
    ├── FEEL / worker-based script
    ├── Variable derivation
    └── Avoid heavy business logic
```

---

# 3. Quick Reference Table

| Task Type | Practical Meaning | Camunda Runtime Behavior | Good Use Case | Avoid When |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Undefined / generic task | Work item belum dispesifikkan | Tergantung implementation/modeling | Early discovery, whiteboarding | Model sudah executable tapi task type belum jelas |
| Service task | Automated work by system | Masuk task → job dibuat → worker complete job | Call API, write DB, validate data, calculate status | Pekerjaan manusia atau menunggu callback async |
| User task | Human work with system support | Task instance dibuat, process menunggu sampai task completed | Approval, verification, manual review | Pekerjaan tidak perlu tracking/UI |
| Manual task | Human work outside engine/tooling | Di Camunda 8 manual task pass-through, process langsung lanjut | Dokumentasi pekerjaan fisik/offline | Butuh claim, assignee, due date, audit completion |
| Send task | Mengirim message ke external system | Behaves like service task; job worker harus mengirim message | Publish Kafka event, send email, send command | Butuh menunggu response langsung tanpa model async |
| Receive task | Menunggu message masuk | Message subscription dibuat, process menunggu correlation | Callback, external approval result, payment received | Perlu race callback vs timeout; lebih cocok event-based gateway + message catch |
| Business rule task | Evaluasi rule/decision | DMN decision dievaluasi, result disimpan ke variable | Risk band, eligibility, routing rule | Rule sangat teknis/imperatif dan bukan decision table |
| Script task | Script/expression kecil | FEEL expression atau worker-based job tergantung config | Derive simple variable, small transformation | Business logic kompleks, DB call, external API call |

Camunda 8 task documentation saat ini mencantumkan task yang didukung seperti service tasks, user tasks, receive tasks, business rule tasks, script tasks, send tasks, manual tasks, dan undefined tasks. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/tasks/))

---

# 4. Core Mental Model: “Task Type = Semantic Contract”

Kesalahan umum adalah memilih task berdasarkan ikon yang terlihat keren. Cara yang lebih benar:

```text id="vnm59c"
Apa nature pekerjaannya?
Siapa/apa yang menjalankan?
Apakah process harus menunggu?
Apakah perlu worker?
Apakah perlu human assignment?
Apakah perlu message correlation?
Apakah output-nya variable?
Apakah task bisa retry?
Apakah task harus idempotent?
```

Contoh:

```text id="sw7j86"
"Validate Case Data"
```

Bisa menjadi beberapa jenis:

| Kemungkinan | Task Type | Kenapa |
|---|---|---|
| Validasi otomatis oleh Java service | Service task | Worker melakukan validation |
| Validasi manual oleh officer | User task | Human perlu review |
| Validasi eksternal via callback | Send task + receive task | Request keluar, response datang belakangan |
| Validasi berdasarkan decision table | Business rule task | DMN mengevaluasi rule |
| Validasi hanya catatan offline | Manual task | Tidak dikontrol engine |

---

# 5. Undefined / Generic Task

## Makna

Generic task adalah task biasa tanpa marker khusus.

```text id="fnc7j6"
[ Validate Request ]
```

Cocok untuk tahap awal modeling ketika tim belum memutuskan apakah pekerjaan dilakukan oleh sistem, manusia, rule engine, atau external participant.

## Practical Use

Gunakan saat:

```text id="nlujmi"
Discovery workshop
High-level process map
Belum ada implementation decision
Belum tahu apakah automated/manual
```

Jangan biarkan terlalu lama di executable BPMN. Saat diagram masuk production design, generic task harus diklasifikasi.

## Review Question

```text id="im85wj"
Task ini akan dijalankan siapa?
- Human?
- Worker?
- External system?
- Rule engine?
- Tidak dikontrol engine?
```

---

# 6. Service Task

## Makna

Service task adalah pekerjaan otomatis oleh sistem. Di Camunda 8, service task merepresentasikan work item dengan task type tertentu; saat token masuk service task, job dibuat dan process instance menunggu sampai job completed. Job worker subscribe ke job type tersebut, memproses job, lalu complete job. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/service-tasks/))

```text id="glg8rp"
[ Validate Case Data ]  <<service task>>
```

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan service task untuk:

```text id="bfyplw"
Call internal REST API
Call external REST API
Persist ke PostgreSQL
Query duplicate case
Generate document
Calculate technical score
Publish audit event
Create investigation record
```

## Camunda Properties yang Perlu Dipikirkan

Service task membutuhkan `taskDefinition`; property `type` dipakai agar worker tahu job apa yang harus diambil, dan `retries` dapat diset untuk menentukan jumlah retry saat worker melaporkan failure. Camunda juga mendukung task headers untuk metadata statis yang dikirim ke worker, serta input/output mappings untuk mengatur variable yang masuk/keluar task. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/service-tasks/))

| Property | Makna Praktis | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Task type | Nama kontrak worker | `validate-case-data` |
| Retries | Retry budget | `3`, `5` |
| Headers | Konfigurasi statis worker | `endpoint=registry`, `operation=check` |
| Input mapping | Variable input untuk worker | `caseId`, `caseType` |
| Output mapping | Variable hasil worker | `validationStatus`, `riskScore` |

## Naming Convention

Gunakan format:

```text id="r3pkg9"
verb-object[-qualifier]
```

Contoh:

```text id="lm4pg9"
validate-case-data
check-duplicate-case
calculate-risk-score
create-investigation
notify-applicant
```

Hindari:

```text id="mbmxey"
task1
service
process
doStuff
caseWorker
```

## Implementation Blueprint

```text id="vy3hfn"
BPMN service task:
  name      = Validate Case Data
  job type  = validate-case-data
  retries   = 3

Worker:
  subscribe validate-case-data
  load case by caseId
  validate domain constraints
  persist validation result
  complete job with variables
```

## Java Worker Sketch

Untuk Camunda 8 versi modern, Camunda Java Client adalah official Java library untuk integrasi process application, termasuk deploy/start process dan implementasi job workers. Dokumentasi Camunda 8.9 juga mencatat bahwa Camunda Java Client menggantikan Zeebe Java Client sejak 8.8, menggunakan REST sebagai default protocol dengan gRPC configurable, dan Zeebe Java Client direncanakan dihapus pada 8.10. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/apis-tools/java-client/getting-started/))

```java id="w4d6n3"
import io.camunda.client.CamundaClient;
import io.camunda.client.api.worker.JobClient;
import io.camunda.client.api.response.ActivatedJob;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.Map;

public final class ValidateCaseDataWorker {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final CaseValidationService validationService;

    public ValidateCaseDataWorker(
            CaseRepository caseRepository,
            CaseValidationService validationService
    ) {
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
        this.validationService = validationService;
    }

    public void register(CamundaClient client) {
        client.newWorker()
                .jobType("validate-case-data")
                .handler(this::handle)
                .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(30))
                .name("validate-case-data-worker")
                .open();
    }

    private void handle(JobClient client, ActivatedJob job) {
        Map<String, Object> vars = job.getVariablesAsMap();
        String caseId = (String) vars.get("caseId");

        try {
            CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findByCaseId(caseId)
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException("Case not found: " + caseId));

            ValidationResult result = validationService.validate(caseRecord);

            caseRepository.saveValidationResult(caseId, result);

            client.newCompleteCommand(job.getKey())
                    .variables(Map.of(
                            "validationStatus", result.status(),
                            "validationErrorCount", result.errors().size()
                    ))
                    .send()
                    .join();

        } catch (RecoverableExternalException ex) {
            client.newFailCommand(job.getKey())
                    .retries(Math.max(job.getRetries() - 1, 0))
                    .errorMessage(ex.getMessage())
                    .send()
                    .join();

        } catch (Exception ex) {
            client.newFailCommand(job.getKey())
                    .retries(0)
                    .errorMessage(ex.getMessage())
                    .send()
                    .join();
        }
    }
}
```

Catatan: detail API bisa berubah antar versi minor. Untuk project baru Camunda 8.8+, gunakan `io.camunda:camunda-client-java`, bukan pola lama `io.camunda.zeebe:zeebe-client-java`, kecuali project perusahaan memang masih berada di versi lama. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/apis-tools/java-client/getting-started/))

## Production Checklist untuk Service Task

| Check | Pertanyaan |
|---|---|
| Idempotency | Aman kalau worker retry atau job diproses ulang? |
| Timeout | Berapa lama worker boleh memegang job? |
| Retry | Failure mana yang retryable vs non-retryable? |
| Error mapping | Kapan fail job, kapan throw BPMN error, kapan incident? |
| DB transaction | Apakah write ke PostgreSQL atomic? |
| External call | Ada HTTP timeout, retry budget, circuit breaker? |
| Observability | Log pakai `caseId`, `processInstanceKey`, `jobKey`? |
| Output variable | Variable hasilnya minimal dan jelas? |
| Security | Jangan expose sensitive data ke process variable sembarangan |

---

# 7. User Task

## Makna

User task adalah pekerjaan yang perlu dilakukan manusia dan dibantu workflow engine/software. Saat process instance masuk user task, Camunda membuat user task instance, process berhenti, lalu lanjut setelah user task completed. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/user-tasks/))

```text id="mjc9qk"
[ Senior Officer Review ]  <<user task>>
```

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan user task untuk:

```text id="v271no"
Approval
Manual verification
Document review
Exception handling
Supervisor escalation
Case assignment
Investigation decision
```

## Jangan Dipakai Untuk

```text id="knjv11"
Automated validation
REST API call
DB update
Fire-and-forget notification
Offline manual work yang tidak perlu tracking
```

## Camunda User Task Properties

Camunda user tasks mendukung assignment, scheduling, task updates, variable mappings, dan form; assignment dapat mencakup `assignee`, `candidateUsers`, dan `candidateGroups`. Mulai Camunda 8.8, kandidat group sebaiknya mereferensikan group ID, bukan group name. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/user-tasks/))

| Concern | Practical Mapping |
|---|---|
| Assignee | User spesifik, misalnya `john.doe` |
| Candidate users | Daftar user yang boleh claim |
| Candidate groups | Role/team seperti `senior-officer` |
| Form | Camunda Form atau custom form |
| Due date/follow-up | SLA/review deadline |
| Priority | Prioritas antrean task |
| Completion variables | `reviewDecision`, `reviewComment`, `approvedBy` |

## User Task Contract

```text id="xwqem7"
Task name:
  Senior Officer Review

Candidate group:
  senior-officer

Input variables:
  caseId
  caseSummary
  riskScore
  duplicateFound
  registryStatus

Output variables:
  reviewDecision
  reviewComment
  reviewedBy
  reviewedAt
```

## Custom UI Completion Pattern

Dalam enterprise app, sering user task tidak diselesaikan langsung dari Tasklist, tapi dari UI internal.

```text id="iwbr7g"
Frontend Vue
  ↓
Jersey REST API
  ↓
Task Application Service
  ↓
Camunda User Task API / client
  ↓
Process continues
```

Contoh API shape:

```java id="dcfdsp"
@Path("/cases/{caseId}/reviews")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CaseReviewResource {

    private final CaseReviewApplicationService reviewService;

    @POST
    public Response completeReview(
            @PathParam("caseId") String caseId,
            CompleteReviewRequest request
    ) {
        CompleteReviewResult result = reviewService.completeReview(caseId, request);

        return Response.ok(Map.of(
                "caseId", caseId,
                "reviewDecision", result.reviewDecision(),
                "taskCompleted", true
        )).build();
    }
}
```

Application service responsibility:

```text id="pnqw4o"
1. Verify user authorization
2. Resolve active task for caseId
3. Validate allowed decision
4. Persist domain review record
5. Complete Camunda user task with variables
6. Return updated case state
```

## Anti-pattern

Buruk:

```text id="phqanj"
User task completed hanya berdasarkan taskId dari frontend tanpa cek caseId/role/status.
```

Lebih aman:

```text id="z9fag3"
Complete task by business context:
  caseId + authenticated user + expected task type/status
```

---

# 8. Manual Task

## Makna

Manual task adalah pekerjaan manusia yang tidak dibantu workflow engine atau UI. Di Camunda 8, manual task diperlakukan sebagai pass-through activity: process langsung lanjut saat instance tiba di manual task. Manual task berguna sebagai informasi bahwa ada pekerjaan di luar engine, tetapi tidak mengaktifkan automation process. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/manual-tasks/))

```text id="imzg5g"
[ Physically Inspect Facility ]  <<manual task>>
```

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan manual task jika:

```text id="cfq24c"
Pekerjaan benar-benar offline
Tidak perlu task assignment di engine
Tidak perlu completion tracking
Hanya ingin dokumentasi proses
```

## Jangan Dipakai Jika

```text id="pnrn7o"
Perlu SLA
Perlu audit siapa yang complete
Perlu approval decision
Perlu form input
Perlu tasklist/custom UI
```

Kalau butuh tracking, gunakan **user task**, bukan manual task.

---

# 9. Send Task

## Makna

Send task digunakan untuk memodelkan publikasi message ke external system, misalnya Kafka topic atau mail server. Di Camunda 8, send task behave seperti service task: job dibuat dan perlu job worker untuk memprosesnya; Zeebe tidak memproses job send task sendiri. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/send-tasks/))

```text id="mzdoxi"
[ Notify Applicant ]  <<send task>>
```

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan send task untuk:

```text id="elqigv"
Send email
Publish Kafka event
Send command to external system
Send notification
Push webhook
Send document to external agency
```

## Send Task vs Service Task

| Situation | Pilihan |
|---|---|
| Fokusnya “melakukan pekerjaan sistem” | Service task |
| Fokusnya “mengirim pesan keluar” | Send task |
| Mengirim email sebagai detail teknis kecil | Service task boleh |
| Message outbound penting di business collaboration | Send task lebih ekspresif |

## Worker Contract

```text id="dtsf6e"
Task name:
  Notify Applicant

Job type:
  send-notification

Headers:
  channel = email
  template = case-submitted

Input variables:
  caseId
  applicantEmail
  notificationLocale

Output variables:
  notificationRequestId
  notificationStatus
```

## Implementation Pattern

```java id="l4qksi"
public final class SendNotificationWorker {

    private final NotificationClient notificationClient;
    private final NotificationRepository notificationRepository;

    public void handle(JobClient client, ActivatedJob job) {
        Map<String, Object> vars = job.getVariablesAsMap();

        String caseId = (String) vars.get("caseId");
        String recipient = (String) vars.get("applicantEmail");

        NotificationCommand command = new NotificationCommand(
                caseId,
                recipient,
                "case-submitted"
        );

        NotificationResult result = notificationClient.send(command);

        notificationRepository.saveOutboundNotification(
                caseId,
                result.requestId(),
                result.status()
        );

        client.newCompleteCommand(job.getKey())
                .variables(Map.of(
                        "notificationRequestId", result.requestId(),
                        "notificationStatus", result.status()
                ))
                .send()
                .join();
    }
}
```

## Production Concern

| Concern | Praktisnya |
|---|---|
| Duplicate send | Gunakan idempotency key: `caseId + template + recipient` |
| Provider timeout | Retry terbatas, jangan infinite |
| Audit | Simpan outbound request dan response |
| Sensitive data | Jangan taruh full email body di variable |
| Delivery status | Jika perlu status delivery async, lanjutkan dengan receive task/message event |

---

# 10. Receive Task

## Makna

Receive task digunakan untuk menunggu message. Saat process masuk receive task, message subscription dibuat dan process berhenti sampai message dikorelasikan. Message harus punya name dan `correlationKey`; correlation key biasanya membaca variable process seperti `orderId` atau `caseId`. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/receive-tasks/))

```text id="dnha1u"
[ Wait Registry Check Completed ]  <<receive task>>
```

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan receive task untuk:

```text id="ir45cv"
External callback
Async approval result
Payment received
Document uploaded
Registry check completed
External investigation status updated
```

## Contract Minimal

```text id="ar4oz9"
Message name:
  RegistryCheckCompleted

Correlation key:
  = caseId

Expected payload:
  registryStatus
  registryReferenceNo
  checkedAt
```

## Jersey Callback Pattern

```java id="a1g31v"
@Path("/registry/callbacks")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class RegistryCallbackResource {

    private final RegistryCallbackService service;

    @POST
    public Response receive(RegistryCallbackRequest request) {
        service.handle(request);

        return Response.accepted(Map.of(
                "caseId", request.caseId(),
                "accepted", true
        )).build();
    }
}
```

```java id="jrmqy1"
public final class RegistryCallbackService {

    private final RegistryResultRepository repository;
    private final CamundaMessageService camundaMessageService;

    public void handle(RegistryCallbackRequest request) {
        repository.upsertResult(
                request.caseId(),
                request.registryStatus(),
                request.registryReferenceNo(),
                request.rawPayload()
        );

        camundaMessageService.publishMessage(
                "RegistryCheckCompleted",
                request.caseId(),
                Map.of(
                        "registryStatus", request.registryStatus(),
                        "registryReferenceNo", request.registryReferenceNo()
                )
        );
    }
}
```

## Receive Task vs Message Intermediate Catch Event

Camunda notes that a message intermediate catch event behaves the same way as receive task, but can be used with event-based gateways. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/receive-tasks/))

| Situation | Prefer |
|---|---|
| Sederhana: tunggu satu callback | Receive task |
| Race: callback vs timeout | Event-based gateway + message catch event |
| Banyak alternative event | Event-based gateway |
| Business-readable “wait step” | Receive task |

## Critical Failure Modes

| Failure | Mitigation |
|---|---|
| Callback datang sebelum subscription aktif | Persist callback; retry correlation; desain ordering |
| Callback duplicate | DB unique key / idempotency |
| Correlation key salah | Contract test dengan external system |
| Message tidak pernah datang | Timer boundary/event-based timeout |
| Message datang setelah timeout | Store and mark as late, jangan crash |
| Payload tidak valid | Persist rejected callback + alert |

---

# 11. Business Rule Task

## Makna

Business rule task digunakan untuk evaluasi business rule, misalnya DMN decision. Di Camunda, ketika process instance tiba di business rule task, decision dievaluasi oleh internal DMN decision engine dan hasilnya disimpan ke variable; jika evaluasi gagal, incident muncul di business rule task. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/business-rule-tasks/))

```text id="rmoxip"
[ Determine Risk Band ]  <<business rule task>>
```

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan untuk:

```text id="vrlrqq"
Risk classification
Eligibility decision
Routing decision
Fee calculation rule
Priority classification
Policy-based approval threshold
```

## Jangan Dipakai Untuk

```text id="db7v4d"
Loop kompleks
External API call
DB-heavy logic
Side effect
Long-running calculation
```

Rule task sebaiknya **pure decision**: input variables → decision result.

## DMN Contract

```text id="zxoobh"
Decision:
  determine_risk_band

Input:
  caseType
  claimAmount
  applicantHistoryScore
  duplicateFound

Output:
  riskBand
  requiredReviewLevel
  slaHours
```

## Practical Pattern

```text id="hnnvcg"
Calculate Risk Features       <<service task>>
  ↓
Determine Risk Band           <<business rule task / DMN>>
  ↓
Gateway: riskBand == "HIGH"?
```

Kenapa dipisah?

```text id="usaeab"
Service task = mengambil/menghitung feature teknis
Business rule task = membuat decision bisnis yang audit-friendly
Gateway = routing berdasarkan decision result
```

## Versioning Concern

Business rule task dapat mengikat decision berdasarkan binding type seperti `latest`, `deployment`, atau `versionTag`; `resultVariable` menentukan nama process variable tempat hasil decision disimpan. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/business-rule-tasks/))

| Binding | Practical Meaning |
|---|---|
| `latest` | Pakai versi decision terbaru saat task aktif |
| `deployment` | Pakai decision yang dideploy bersama process version |
| `versionTag` | Pakai decision dengan tag tertentu |

Untuk proses regulatory/audit-heavy, `deployment` atau `versionTag` sering lebih defensible daripada `latest`, karena hasil lama bisa ditelusuri ke rule version yang jelas.

---

# 12. Script Task

## Makna

Script task digunakan untuk menjalankan script/expression kecil. Di Camunda 8, script task dapat menggunakan FEEL expression atau job-worker implementation. Jika memakai job-worker implementation, script task behave seperti service task: job dibuat, worker harus memprosesnya, dan Zeebe tidak memproses script job sendiri. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/script-tasks/))

```text id="os97jh"
[ Normalize Applicant Name ]  <<script task>>
```

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan script task untuk:

```text id="qv458c"
Derive small variable
Normalize simple value
Build simple label
Calculate trivial expression
Transform small payload
```

Contoh:

```text id="f4ncyz"
riskLabel = if riskScore >= 80 then "HIGH" else "NORMAL"
```

## Jangan Dipakai Untuk

```text id="msjbf7"
Business logic besar
DB access
REST call
Authorization logic
Complex validation
Reusable enterprise rule
```

Rule praktis:

```text id="w617a3"
Kalau logic perlu unit test serius, observability, dependency injection, DB, atau retry policy, jangan taruh di script task.
Pindahkan ke service task atau business rule task.
```

## Script Task vs Service Task vs Business Rule Task

| Need | Prefer |
|---|---|
| Small expression | Script task |
| Decision table / policy rule | Business rule task |
| Call service / DB / API | Service task |
| Reusable business logic | Service task / DMN |
| Audit decision | Business rule task |

---

# 13. Task Granularity

Granularity adalah keputusan arsitektural.

## Terlalu besar

```text id="p7grxx"
Process Case
```

Masalah:

```text id="t5vczi"
Business decision tersembunyi
Sulit observability
Sulit retry per step
Sulit audit
Sulit incident triage
```

## Terlalu kecil

```text id="oj24ia"
Open DB Connection
Validate Field A
Validate Field B
Validate Field C
Call Mapper
Commit Transaction
```

Masalah:

```text id="dhgqxq"
Diagram jadi low-level technical flow
Business stakeholder susah baca
Terlalu banyak job overhead
```

## Granularity yang baik

```text id="e5z7j7"
Validate Case Data
Check Duplicate Case
Calculate Risk Features
Determine Risk Band
Review High Risk Case
Register Case
Notify Applicant
```

Rule praktis:

```text id="t3a0jq"
Satu task sebaiknya punya business outcome yang jelas, bukan detail coding.
```

---

# 14. End-to-End Example: Case Intake

## BPMN Sketch

```text id="wdqq5a"
Start: Case Submitted
  ↓
Validate Case Data             <<service task>>
  ↓
Check Duplicate Case           <<service task>>
  ↓
Determine Risk Band            <<business rule task>>
  ↓
Gateway: riskBand == HIGH?
  ├─ yes →
  │    Senior Officer Review   <<user task>>
  │       ↓
  │    Gateway: approved?
  │       ├─ yes → Register Case
  │       └─ no  → Reject Case
  │
  └─ no →
       Register Case           <<service task>>
          ↓
Notify Applicant               <<send task>>
  ↓
End
```

## Implementation Mapping

| BPMN Element | Type | Implementation |
|---|---|---|
| Validate Case Data | Service task | Java worker + domain validation + PostgreSQL |
| Check Duplicate Case | Service task | MyBatis query + unique/duplicate check |
| Determine Risk Band | Business rule task | DMN decision |
| Senior Officer Review | User task | Tasklist/custom Vue UI + Jersey completion API |
| Register Case | Service task | Insert/update case status in PostgreSQL |
| Reject Case | Service task | Update state + rejection reason |
| Notify Applicant | Send task | Notification worker / email / Kafka |
| End | End event | Process complete |

---

# 15. PostgreSQL/MyBatis Patterns per Task

## A. Service Task: idempotent create

```sql id="gbjhlw"
INSERT INTO investigation (
    case_id,
    status,
    created_at
)
VALUES (
    #{caseId},
    'OPEN',
    now()
)
ON CONFLICT (case_id)
DO UPDATE SET
    status = investigation.status
RETURNING id, case_id, status;
```

Meaning:

```text id="s68ajv"
Jika worker retry, tidak membuat investigation duplicate.
```

## B. User Task: persist review before completing task

```sql id="bqwag3"
INSERT INTO case_review (
    case_id,
    reviewer_id,
    decision,
    comment,
    reviewed_at
)
VALUES (
    #{caseId},
    #{reviewerId},
    #{decision},
    #{comment},
    now()
)
ON CONFLICT (case_id, reviewer_id)
DO UPDATE SET
    decision = EXCLUDED.decision,
    comment = EXCLUDED.comment,
    reviewed_at = now();
```

Meaning:

```text id="pt3si9"
Domain audit tidak bergantung hanya pada history engine.
```

## C. Receive Task: idempotent callback store

```sql id="w3ghul"
INSERT INTO external_callback (
    callback_type,
    correlation_key,
    external_reference_no,
    payload,
    received_at
)
VALUES (
    #{callbackType},
    #{correlationKey},
    #{externalReferenceNo},
    CAST(#{payload} AS jsonb),
    now()
)
ON CONFLICT (callback_type, correlation_key, external_reference_no)
DO NOTHING;
```

Meaning:

```text id="qgtj42"
Callback duplicate tidak membuat side effect ganda.
```

---

# 16. Task Type Decision Matrix

```text id="yhd6u2"
Apakah pekerjaan dilakukan manusia?
  ├─ Ya, perlu tracking/UI/assignment → User Task
  ├─ Ya, tidak perlu engine/UI → Manual Task
  └─ Tidak
      │
      ├─ Apakah hanya menunggu message masuk?
      │    └─ Ya → Receive Task / Message Catch Event
      │
      ├─ Apakah mengirim message keluar?
      │    └─ Ya → Send Task
      │
      ├─ Apakah mengevaluasi business decision/rule?
      │    └─ Ya → Business Rule Task
      │
      ├─ Apakah transformasi kecil/expression?
      │    └─ Ya → Script Task
      │
      └─ Automated system work umum
           └─ Service Task
```

---

# 17. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Contoh | Dampak | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Semua task jadi service task | `Approve Case` sebagai service task | Human decision tersembunyi | User task |
| Semua task jadi user task | `Calculate Risk` sebagai user task | Manualisasi kerja sistem | Service task / business rule task |
| Receive task tanpa timeout | Wait callback selamanya | Hanging instance | Timer boundary / event-based gateway |
| Business rule di service task | Rule eligibility hardcoded Java | Sulit audit/versioning | DMN + business rule task |
| Script task terlalu berat | Script akses DB/API | Tidak maintainable | Service task |
| Manual task untuk approval | Manual task “Manager Approval” | Process langsung lanjut, tidak menunggu approval | User task |
| Send task tanpa idempotency | Email terkirim berkali-kali saat retry | Duplicate notification | Outbox/idempotency key |
| Worker complete sebelum DB commit | Process lanjut tapi data belum aman | Inconsistent state | Commit domain state dulu, baru complete job |
| Output variable terlalu besar | Full payload JSON masuk process variable | Performance/security issue | Simpan di DB, variable hanya reference |
| Task name teknis | `MapperCallTask` | Business unreadable | `Check Duplicate Case` |

---

# 18. Practical Review Checklist

Untuk setiap task di BPMN, cek:

```text id="u1i2yy"
1. Task ini menghasilkan business outcome apa?
2. Siapa/apa executor-nya?
3. Apakah process harus menunggu task ini?
4. Apakah butuh human assignment?
5. Apakah butuh job worker?
6. Apakah task bisa retry?
7. Apakah handler idempotent?
8. Apa input variables-nya?
9. Apa output variables-nya?
10. Apa failure mode-nya?
11. Apakah ada timeout/SLA?
12. Apakah task type-nya sudah tepat?
```

Jika tidak bisa menjawab nomor 2, 8, 9, dan 10, task belum siap untuk production BPMN.

---

# 19. Minimal Production Template per Task

Gunakan format ini saat mendokumentasikan task:

```text id="jwwuf5"
Task name:
Task type:
BPMN id:
Executor:
Input variables:
Output variables:
Business key:
Idempotency key:
Retry policy:
Timeout:
Error behavior:
Domain table touched:
External dependency:
Observability fields:
Security concern:
```

Contoh:

```text id="tcbrb1"
Task name:
  Check Duplicate Case

Task type:
  Service task

BPMN id:
  check-duplicate-case

Executor:
  Java worker: check-duplicate-case

Input variables:
  caseId
  subjectId
  caseType

Output variables:
  duplicateFound
  duplicateCaseId

Business key:
  caseId

Idempotency key:
  caseId + "check-duplicate-case"

Retry policy:
  3 retries for transient DB/connection failure

Timeout:
  30 seconds

Error behavior:
  Technical failure -> job retry / incident
  Duplicate found -> complete normally with duplicateFound=true

Domain table touched:
  case_record
  duplicate_case_check

External dependency:
  PostgreSQL

Observability fields:
  caseId, processInstanceKey, jobKey

Security concern:
  Subject identifier must not be logged in plaintext
```

---

# 20. Ringkasan Seri 3

Inti seri ini:

```text id="qltyuc"
Generic task       = belum jelas implementation
Service task       = automated system work
User task          = human work tracked by engine/application
Manual task        = human/offline work, not tracked, pass-through in Camunda 8
Send task          = outbound message/action via worker
Receive task       = wait for inbound message correlation
Business rule task = evaluate DMN/business decision
Script task        = small expression/transformation
```

Untuk Java/Jersey/MyBatis/Camunda application, cara berpikirnya:

```text id="d74sea"
BPMN task type
→ runtime semantic
→ executor
→ input/output variables
→ domain transaction
→ idempotency
→ retry/failure behavior
→ observability
```

Poin paling penting: **jangan pilih task type dari bentuk ikon saja**. Pilih berdasarkan semantic: manusia vs sistem, kerja aktif vs menunggu event, decision vs side effect, sync vs async, tracked vs untracked.

**Seri 3/12 selesai - Basic Activities & Task Types.**

## Response:
7/16/2026, 9:45:23 AM

Lanjut ke **Seri 4/12 — Subprocess & Reuse Pattern**. Fokusnya: kapan proses besar harus dipecah, kapan pakai embedded subprocess vs call activity, dan bagaimana menjaga versioning/error handling tetap aman di Camunda.

# Seri 4/12 - Subprocess & Reuse Pattern

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas cara memecah BPMN besar menjadi bagian yang lebih kecil, reusable, dan operationally manageable.

Dalam BPMN/Camunda, subprocess bukan sekadar “folder visual”. Subprocess adalah **scope eksekusi**. Artinya, ia bisa punya boundary event, variable scope, error behavior, timeout, dan lifecycle sendiri. Camunda 8 documentation menjelaskan subprocess sebagai container elemen untuk common functionality, dan saat event di boundary subprocess ter-trigger, subprocess dapat diinterupsi terlepas elemen mana yang sedang aktif di dalamnya. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/subprocesses/))

Mental model utama:

```text id="djy0t9"
Subprocess = local scope / grouped flow
Call Activity = reusable external process
Event Subprocess = event-triggered handler inside active scope
Ad-hoc Subprocess = flexible/dynamic set of activities
Transaction Subprocess = BPMN concept for transactional/cancel/compensation modeling
```

Untuk Camunda 8 production modeling, elemen subprocess yang terlihat didokumentasikan secara langsung adalah **embedded subprocess**, **call activity**, **event subprocess**, dan **ad-hoc subprocess**. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/subprocesses/))

---

# 2. Diagram Decomposition

```text id="19pjzv"
BPMN Subprocess & Reuse Pattern
│
├── 1. Embedded Subprocess
│   ├── Group local steps
│   ├── Hide complexity
│   ├── Local variable scope
│   ├── Boundary event target
│   └── Not reusable across processes
│
├── 2. Collapsed vs Expanded Subprocess
│   ├── Expanded = detail visible
│   ├── Collapsed = compact visual
│   ├── Drill-down readability
│   └── Still same process definition
│
├── 3. Call Activity
│   ├── Reusable external BPMN process
│   ├── Parent-child process instance
│   ├── Input/output variable mapping
│   ├── Version binding
│   ├── Process contract
│   └── Shared workflow module
│
├── 4. Event Subprocess
│   ├── Triggered by event while parent scope active
│   ├── Interrupting
│   ├── Non-interrupting
│   ├── Message/timer/error/signal/escalation
│   └── Scope-level exception/reaction handler
│
├── 5. Ad-hoc Subprocess
│   ├── Flexible execution order
│   ├── Optional activities
│   ├── Dynamic case/investigation style
│   ├── Completion condition
│   └── Worker/API-controlled activation
│
├── 6. Transaction / Compensation Thinking
│   ├── Logical rollback
│   ├── Compensation task
│   ├── Cancel behavior
│   ├── Saga-like process design
│   └── Explicit recovery path
│
├── 7. Reuse & Modularity Rules
│   ├── Reuse by call activity
│   ├── Encapsulate domain workflow
│   ├── Avoid over-fragmentation
│   ├── Define input/output contract
│   └── Versioning strategy
│
└── 8. Production Governance
    ├── Naming
    ├── Boundary events
    ├── Variable mapping
    ├── Error propagation
    ├── Observability
    ├── Testing
    └── Migration/versioning
```

---

# 3. Quick Reference Table

| Pattern | Symbol / Type | Praktisnya | Cocok Untuk | Hindari Jika |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Embedded subprocess | Subprocess di dalam process yang sama | Mengelompokkan beberapa task dalam satu scope lokal | Detail proses yang masih milik parent process | Ingin reuse di banyak process |
| Collapsed subprocess | Subprocess disembunyikan detailnya | Menyederhanakan tampilan diagram | Diagram besar yang perlu readability | Pembaca butuh melihat detail kritikal |
| Expanded subprocess | Detail subprocess terlihat | Memperlihatkan flow internal | Review teknis/process design | Diagram terlalu padat |
| Call activity | Memanggil process BPMN lain | Reusable subprocess external | KYC, risk assessment, notification workflow, document verification | Flow hanya dipakai sekali dan sangat tergantung parent |
| Event subprocess | Handler event di dalam scope aktif | Reaksi terhadap timeout, message, error, escalation | Cancel request, emergency stop, escalation, async update | Event hanya berlaku untuk satu activity spesifik; pakai boundary event |
| Ad-hoc subprocess | Set aktivitas fleksibel | Aktivitas bisa dipilih/dijalankan dinamis | Investigation, case handling, knowledge work | Proses harus deterministik dan ketat |
| Transaction/compensation thinking | Transaction subprocess / compensation pattern | Logical rollback/reversal | Payment/refund, booking cancel, undo side effect | Mengharapkan ACID transaction lintas sistem |
| Boundary event on subprocess | Event menempel di boundary subprocess | Timeout/error/cancel seluruh block | SLA block, external failure, cancel batch | Hanya satu task yang perlu timeout/error |

---

# 4. Embedded Subprocess

## Makna

Embedded subprocess adalah subprocess yang berada di dalam process definition yang sama. Camunda 8 docs menyebut embedded subprocess sebagai cara untuk mengelompokkan elemen proses; embedded subprocess harus punya tepat satu **none start event**, dan tidak boleh memakai start event tipe lain. Saat masuk subprocess, start event diaktifkan; subprocess aktif selama ada elemen internal yang aktif, lalu selesai ketika elemen terakhir selesai. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/embedded-subprocesses/))

```text id="r73d68"
Parent Process
  ↓
[ Embedded Subprocess: Validate Case ]
    Start
      ↓
    Validate Mandatory Fields
      ↓
    Validate Applicant Identity
      ↓
    Validate Attached Documents
      ↓
    End
  ↓
Continue Parent Process
```

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan embedded subprocess saat kamu ingin:

```text id="l4rr9r"
Mengelompokkan beberapa step yang masih bagian dari parent process
Menyederhanakan diagram utama
Memberi boundary event ke satu block aktivitas
Membuat local scope untuk exception/timeout
Membedakan fase proses: Intake, Validation, Review, Closure
```

## Contoh Practical

```text id="h22znq"
Case Intake Process
  ↓
Subprocess: Initial Validation
  ├── Validate Request Payload
  ├── Check Mandatory Documents
  ├── Check Duplicate Case
  └── Persist Validation Result
  ↓
Gateway: validationStatus == VALID?
```

Mapping implementasi:

| Subprocess Element | Implementation |
|---|---|
| Validate Request Payload | Service task / Java worker |
| Check Mandatory Documents | Service task + PostgreSQL/MyBatis |
| Check Duplicate Case | Service task + SQL query |
| Persist Validation Result | Service task / domain service |
| Boundary timer on subprocess | SLA timeout untuk seluruh validation phase |
| Boundary error on subprocess | Error handler untuk validation block |

---

# 5. Embedded Subprocess sebagai Scope

Subprocess bukan hanya visual grouping. Ia adalah scope.

Artinya:

```text id="zij9gq"
Boundary event bisa ditempel ke subprocess
Variable bisa dibatasi scope-nya
Error/timeout bisa menangkap seluruh isi subprocess
Subprocess bisa selesai hanya jika semua token di dalamnya selesai
```

Camunda docs menyebut embedded subprocess sering dipakai bersama boundary events; ketika interrupting boundary event ter-trigger, seluruh subprocess, termasuk semua elemen aktif di dalamnya, dihentikan. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/embedded-subprocesses/))

## Contoh: Timeout seluruh validation block

```text id="d0rrq5"
Subprocess: Validate Case
  ├── Check Duplicate
  ├── Check Registry
  └── Validate Documents

Boundary Timer: PT24H
  ↓
Escalate Validation Timeout
```

Makna runtime:

```text id="jex1ax"
Jika validation belum selesai dalam 24 jam,
seluruh aktivitas di dalam Validate Case dihentikan,
lalu proses pindah ke escalation path.
```

Ini berbeda dengan timer di satu task. Timer di subprocess berlaku untuk seluruh block.

---

# 6. Collapsed vs Expanded Subprocess

Camunda docs menyebut collapsed subprocess menyembunyikan detail internal untuk mengurangi kompleksitas visual, sedangkan collapsed subprocess tetap untuk display; untuk reusable process, rekomendasinya gunakan call activity. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/embedded-subprocesses/))

## Expanded

```text id="usgq7m"
Subprocess: Validate Case
  Start
    ↓
  Validate Payload
    ↓
  Check Duplicate
    ↓
  Validate Documents
    ↓
  End
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="c29l85"
Design review
Debugging flow
Onboarding engineer
Process audit detail
```

## Collapsed

```text id="m5syqn"
[ + Validate Case ]
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="ztv4r6"
Executive/process overview
Diagram sangat besar
Detail sudah stabil
Pembaca hanya butuh main flow
```

## Rule Praktis

```text id="ixuazr"
Collapsed subprocess menyembunyikan kompleksitas,
bukan membuat reusable module.

Kalau ingin reuse lintas process,
gunakan call activity.
```

---

# 7. Call Activity

## Makna

Call activity adalah cara memanggil process BPMN lain sebagai bagian dari process saat ini. Camunda docs menyebut call activity sebagai reusable subprocess: mirip embedded subprocess, tetapi process-nya dieksternalisasi sebagai BPMN terpisah dan bisa dipanggil oleh banyak process. Saat call activity dimasuki, Camunda membuat process instance baru dari referenced process; parent menunggu sampai child process selesai, lalu outgoing sequence flow parent dilanjutkan. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/call-activities/))

```text id="v9epje"
Parent Process: Case Intake
  ↓
Call Activity: Run KYC Check
  ↓
Continue Case Intake

Called Process: KYC Check Process
  Start
    ↓
  Validate Identity
    ↓
  Check Watchlist
    ↓
  Determine KYC Result
    ↓
  End
```

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan call activity untuk workflow yang:

```text id="i9u3aj"
Dipakai oleh banyak parent process
Punya lifecycle/versioning sendiri
Punya owner/domain berbeda
Cukup kompleks untuk dipisah
Butuh testing terpisah
Punya reusable input/output contract
```

Contoh enterprise:

| Reusable Process | Parent yang Bisa Memakai |
|---|---|
| KYC Check | Customer onboarding, case intake, account opening |
| Document Verification | Loan application, complaint registration, enforcement case |
| Risk Assessment | Case triage, transaction monitoring, investigation opening |
| Notification Workflow | Case update, approval result, SLA breach |
| Approval Workflow | Procurement, exception approval, policy waiver |
| Payment/Refund Workflow | Order process, claim process, compensation process |

---

# 8. Call Activity Version Binding

Call activity perlu menentukan process ID dari process yang dipanggil. Camunda docs menyebut process ID bisa statis, misalnya `shipping-process`, atau expression; binding type menentukan versi process yang diinstansiasi: `latest`, `deployment`, atau `versionTag`. Jika binding type tidak dispesifikkan, default-nya adalah `latest`. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/call-activities/))

| Binding Type | Makna | Kapan Dipakai |
|---|---|---|
| `latest` | Memanggil versi terbaru saat call activity aktif | Development cepat, process non-regulatory |
| `deployment` | Memanggil versi yang dideploy bersama process pemanggil | Release bundle yang harus konsisten |
| `versionTag` | Memanggil versi terbaru dengan tag tertentu | Production governance, controlled rollout |

## Practical Recommendation

Untuk proses regulatory/case management:

```text id="uebyf7"
Jangan asal pakai latest untuk proses kritikal.
```

Kenapa?

```text id="o29a5r"
Parent process v1 bisa tiba-tiba memanggil child process v3.
Input/output contract bisa berubah.
Audit defensibility melemah.
Testing parent-child combination jadi tidak stabil.
```

Lebih aman:

```text id="vy334g"
Gunakan deployment binding untuk release yang tightly-coupled.
Gunakan versionTag untuk contract-based reuse.
```

Contoh:

```text id="usrytw"
Parent:
  case-intake-process:2026.07

Child:
  risk-assessment-process:2026.07
```

---

# 9. Input/Output Contract untuk Call Activity

Call activity tidak boleh dianggap “langsung tahu semua variable parent”. Untuk production design, selalu buat contract eksplisit.

## Buruk

```text id="uaj7i0"
Parent mengirim semua variables ke child process.
Child bebas membaca apa saja.
Child mengubah variable parent tanpa dokumentasi.
```

Masalah:

```text id="lzvyav"
Tight coupling
Variable collision
Security risk
Sulit versioning
Sulit test
Sulit audit
```

## Baik

```text id="6sub78"
Input mapping:
  caseId
  applicantId
  caseType
  submittedAt

Output mapping:
  riskBand
  riskScore
  riskReasonCodes
  assessmentReferenceNo
```

## Contract Template

```text id="be2n0v"
Called process:
  risk-assessment-process

Version binding:
  versionTag = 2026.07

Input variables:
  caseId: string
  applicantId: string
  caseType: string
  submittedAt: datetime

Output variables:
  riskScore: number
  riskBand: LOW | MEDIUM | HIGH
  riskReasonCodes: list<string>
  assessmentReferenceNo: string

Error outputs:
  riskAssessmentStatus: FAILED
  failureReasonCode: string
```

---

# 10. Embedded Subprocess vs Call Activity

| Concern | Embedded Subprocess | Call Activity |
|---|---|---|
| File/process definition | Sama dengan parent | BPMN process terpisah |
| Reuse | Tidak reusable lintas process | Reusable |
| Instance | Scope dalam parent instance | Child process instance baru |
| Versioning | Ikut parent process version | Punya versioning sendiri |
| Owner | Biasanya parent process owner | Bisa owner/domain berbeda |
| Coupling | Tightly coupled ke parent | Contract-based |
| Testing | Test bersama parent | Bisa test child terpisah |
| Migration | Ikut parent migration | Perlu pikir parent-child version compatibility |
| Best for | Local grouping/fase proses | Shared workflow module |

## Decision Rule

```text id="6d28p9"
Kalau hanya merapikan diagram parent → embedded subprocess.
Kalau ingin reuse/ownership/versioning terpisah → call activity.
```

---

# 11. Event Subprocess

## Makna

Event subprocess adalah subprocess yang dipicu oleh event selama containing scope masih aktif. Camunda docs menjelaskan event subprocess bisa ditambahkan global ke process atau lokal di embedded subprocess; ia harus punya tepat satu start event dari tipe timer, message, error, signal, atau escalation. Event subprocess bisa interrupting atau non-interrupting, mirip boundary event, tetapi berada di dalam scope. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/event-subprocesses/))

```text id="p1qjg8"
Process: Case Handling
  Main flow:
    Review Case
      ↓
    Decide Outcome

  Event Subprocess:
    Start Message: CancelCaseRequested
      ↓
    Cancel Case
      ↓
    Notify Parties
```

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan event subprocess untuk event yang:

```text id="z9sc56"
Bisa terjadi kapan saja selama scope aktif
Berlaku untuk seluruh process/subprocess, bukan hanya satu task
Butuh handler flow lebih dari satu step
Butuh akses ke variable scope
Bisa interrupting atau non-interrupting
```

Contoh:

| Event | Event Subprocess Use Case |
|---|---|
| Message | Customer cancels request while process active |
| Timer | SLA breach at process level |
| Error | Error handler for a whole subprocess |
| Signal | Global operational event |
| Escalation | Business escalation handler |
| Timer non-interrupting | Periodic reminder while process continues |

---

# 12. Interrupting vs Non-Interrupting Event Subprocess

Camunda docs menjelaskan non-interrupting event subprocess dapat ter-trigger beberapa kali; interrupting event subprocess hanya dapat ter-trigger sekali. Jika interrupting event subprocess ter-trigger, semua instance aktif dalam containing scope dihentikan, termasuk non-interrupting event subprocess lain yang sedang aktif. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/event-subprocesses/))

## Interrupting

```text id="j7hzcl"
Message: CancelCaseRequested
  ↓
Terminate active work
  ↓
Mark case as CANCELLED
  ↓
Notify applicant
```

Makna:

```text id="eii3yb"
Begitu cancel request masuk,
main flow dihentikan.
```

## Non-interrupting

```text id="rdifdc"
Timer: every 24 hours while review active
  ↓
Send reminder
```

Makna:

```text id="gk24zh"
Main flow tetap berjalan,
reminder berjalan paralel.
```

## Practical Rule

```text id="x1oxfk"
Interrupting = event mengganti jalan hidup process.
Non-interrupting = event menambahkan side-flow tanpa menghentikan main flow.
```

---

# 13. Event Subprocess vs Boundary Event

| Concern | Boundary Event | Event Subprocess |
|---|---|---|
| Posisi | Menempel di activity/subprocess boundary | Di dalam scope process/subprocess |
| Target | Satu activity/scope spesifik | Seluruh containing scope |
| Handler complexity | Cocok untuk handler pendek | Cocok untuk handler multi-step |
| Variable access | Boundary berada di luar scope tertentu | Event subprocess berada dalam scope dan bisa access/modify local variables |
| Use case | Timeout satu user task, error satu service task | Cancel request kapan saja, global escalation, process-level SLA |

Camunda docs mencatat event subprocess berada di dalam scope, sehingga bisa mengakses dan memodifikasi local variables dari containing scope; boundary event berada di luar scope sehingga tidak punya kemampuan yang sama. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/event-subprocesses/))

## Decision Rule

```text id="96qii2"
Event hanya relevan ke satu task/subprocess → boundary event.
Event berlaku selama seluruh process/subprocess aktif → event subprocess.
```

---

# 14. Ad-hoc Subprocess

## Makna

Ad-hoc subprocess adalah subprocess fleksibel. Camunda docs menjelaskan ad-hoc subprocess sebagai jenis embedded subprocess dengan marker tilde `~`; elemen internalnya tidak terhubung ke start/end event, dapat dieksekusi beberapa kali, dalam urutan apa pun, atau dilewati. Constraint-nya: minimal punya satu activity dan tidak boleh punya start/end event. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/ad-hoc-subprocesses/))

```text id="u71qgc"
Ad-hoc Subprocess: Investigation Actions
  ~
  [ Request Additional Document ]
  [ Interview Applicant ]
  [ Check External Registry ]
  [ Assign Field Inspection ]
  [ Consult Legal Team ]
```

## Kapan Dipakai

Ad-hoc subprocess cocok untuk:

```text id="u71l4t"
Case management
Investigation
Complaint handling
Exception handling
Knowledge work
AI/human-assisted decision flow
Dynamic work item selection
```

Misalnya dalam enforcement lifecycle:

```text id="rpexvb"
Investigating officer dapat memilih:
- Request additional evidence
- Interview party
- Check previous violation
- Consult legal
- Run site inspection
- Close investigation
```

Tidak semua langkah wajib, dan urutannya bisa berbeda per kasus.

---

# 15. Ad-hoc Subprocess Execution di Camunda

Camunda docs menyebut ad-hoc subprocess bisa ditangani secara internal oleh Zeebe atau melalui job worker. Dengan internal handling, model bisa menentukan elemen mana yang diaktifkan dan kapan subprocess selesai. Dengan job worker, worker dapat mengontrol subprocess dengan mengaktifkan elemen internal dan memutuskan kapan selesai. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/ad-hoc-subprocesses/))

## Internal style

```text id="xky12y"
Process variable:
  selectedActions = [
    "request-additional-document",
    "consult-legal-team"
  ]

Ad-hoc subprocess:
  activeElementsCollection = selectedActions
```

Camunda docs menjelaskan `activeElementsCollection` harus menghasilkan list of strings yang cocok dengan ID elemen internal; jika expression tidak menghasilkan list string atau berisi nilai yang bukan ID elemen internal, process instance membuat incident. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/ad-hoc-subprocesses/))

## Worker-controlled style

```text id="qhhm16"
Ad-hoc subprocess creates job
Worker decides:
  activate request-additional-document
  activate consult-legal-team
  wait for completion
  activate close-investigation
```

Camunda docs menjelaskan ketika ad-hoc subprocess memakai job worker, job dibuat saat aktivasi; worker memilih elemen yang diaktifkan dan menyelesaikan job dengan ad-hoc subprocess job result, lalu Zeebe mengaktifkan elemen-elemen tersebut. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/ad-hoc-subprocesses/))

---

# 16. Ad-hoc Subprocess: Practical Example

## Scenario

Dalam investigation case, officer memilih aktivitas berdasarkan kondisi kasus.

```text id="nufiep"
Start Investigation
  ↓
Ad-hoc Subprocess: Perform Investigation Actions
  ~
  [ Request Additional Document ]
  [ Conduct Interview ]
  [ Check External Registry ]
  [ Consult Legal ]
  [ Site Inspection ]
  ↓
Determine Investigation Outcome
```

## Decision input

```json id="rwz0xa"
{
  "caseId": "CASE-2026-001",
  "caseType": "ENFORCEMENT",
  "riskBand": "HIGH",
  "selectedInvestigationActions": [
    "request-additional-document",
    "check-external-registry",
    "consult-legal"
  ]
}
```

## Completion condition idea

```text id="h96rc6"
completedActions contains all requiredActions
```

Camunda docs menyebut ad-hoc subprocess dapat mendefinisikan optional `completionCondition` boolean expression yang dievaluasi setiap kali inner element selesai; jika true, subprocess selesai dan outgoing sequence flow diambil. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/ad-hoc-subprocesses/))

---

# 17. Transaction Subprocess & Compensation Thinking

BPMN punya konsep transaction/cancel/compensation. Namun untuk Camunda 8 practical production design, jangan mengandalkan “distributed ACID transaction” lintas microservice. Lebih realistis gunakan **compensation/saga-style thinking**:

```text id="jwdnom"
Do action
Persist state
If later failure/cancel happens
Run explicit compensating action
```

Camunda symbol reference menjelaskan cancel event digunakan hanya dalam konteks transaction. ([Camunda](https://camunda.com/en/bpmn/reference/)) Di sisi Camunda 8 docs yang dibuka, subprocess docs yang aktif/didokumentasikan secara langsung adalah embedded subprocess, call activity, event subprocess, dan ad-hoc subprocess. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/subprocesses/))

## Practical Model

```text id="qyd9va"
Reserve Resource
  ↓
Create Case Record
  ↓
Send External Notification
  ↓
Gateway: later rejected?
  ├── yes → Compensate Notification / Release Resource / Mark Case Rejected
  └── no  → Continue
```

## Compensation Checklist

| Original Action | Compensating Action |
|---|---|
| Reserve budget | Release budget |
| Create investigation | Close/cancel investigation |
| Send payment instruction | Send reversal/refund instruction |
| Register case | Mark as cancelled/retracted |
| Send external notification | Send correction/cancellation notice |
| Lock entity | Unlock entity |

## Rule Praktis

```text id="857khz"
Jangan pikir rollback database transaction.
Pikir business compensation.
```

---

# 18. Practical Modularization Strategy

## A. Jangan langsung pakai call activity untuk semua hal

Buruk:

```text id="q9861l"
Parent process:
  Call Validate Payload
  Call Check Duplicate
  Call Calculate Risk
  Call Register Case
  Call Notify Applicant
```

Masalah:

```text id="e33it5"
Terlalu banyak child process
Debugging terfragmentasi
Overhead operational
Versioning jadi rumit
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="dwk0eb"
Embedded subprocess: Validate Case
  ├── Validate Payload
  ├── Check Duplicate
  └── Validate Documents

Call activity: Run Risk Assessment
```

Kenapa?

```text id="zpeepd"
Validation lokal hanya milik parent.
Risk assessment reusable lintas proses.
```

---

## B. Gunakan embedded subprocess untuk fase

Contoh phase-based modeling:

```text id="4l6a9k"
Case Intake Process
  ↓
Subprocess: Intake
  ↓
Subprocess: Validation
  ↓
Subprocess: Risk Assessment
  ↓
Subprocess: Review
  ↓
Subprocess: Registration
  ↓
Subprocess: Notification
```

Jika satu fase reusable lintas proses, ubah menjadi call activity.

---

## C. Gunakan call activity untuk domain workflow reusable

Contoh:

```text id="c23jua"
Call Activity: Document Verification Process
Call Activity: Risk Assessment Process
Call Activity: KYC Process
Call Activity: Notification Process
```

Setiap called process harus punya:

```text id="4z6r3w"
Owner
Process ID
Version strategy
Input contract
Output contract
Error contract
SLA
Operational dashboard
Test suite
```

---

# 19. Implementation Pattern: Parent Process + Call Activity

## Parent process

```text id="mysmqu"
Case Intake Process
  ↓
Validate Case Locally
  ↓
Call Activity: Risk Assessment Process
  ↓
Gateway: riskBand == HIGH?
```

## Called process

```text id="vc0hk4"
Risk Assessment Process
  Start
    ↓
  Load Risk Features
    ↓
  Evaluate Risk Rule
    ↓
  Persist Assessment Result
    ↓
  End
```

## Input/output mapping

```text id="mql55f"
Parent → Child:
  caseId
  applicantId
  caseType

Child → Parent:
  riskScore
  riskBand
  riskAssessmentId
```

## PostgreSQL table

```sql id="hlqa7o"
CREATE TABLE risk_assessment (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    assessment_version VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    risk_score NUMERIC(5,2) NOT NULL,
    risk_band VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL,
    reason_codes JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT uq_risk_assessment_case_version UNIQUE (case_id, assessment_version)
);
```

## MyBatis upsert sketch

```xml id="y8b9np"
<insert id="upsertRiskAssessment">
  INSERT INTO risk_assessment (
      case_id,
      assessment_version,
      risk_score,
      risk_band,
      reason_codes
  )
  VALUES (
      #{caseId},
      #{assessmentVersion},
      #{riskScore},
      #{riskBand},
      CAST(#{reasonCodesJson} AS jsonb)
  )
  ON CONFLICT (case_id, assessment_version)
  DO UPDATE SET
      risk_score = EXCLUDED.risk_score,
      risk_band = EXCLUDED.risk_band,
      reason_codes = EXCLUDED.reason_codes,
      created_at = now()
</insert>
```

---

# 20. Implementation Pattern: Cancel Request via Event Subprocess

## BPMN

```text id="lalie3"
Case Handling Process

Main flow:
  Review Case
    ↓
  Decide Outcome
    ↓
  Notify Result

Event subprocess:
  Message Start: CancelCaseRequested
    ↓
  Validate Cancellation Allowed
    ↓
  Mark Case Cancelled
    ↓
  Notify Applicant
```

## Jersey endpoint

```java id="i5pw7l"
@Path("/cases/{caseId}/cancellation-requests")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CaseCancellationResource {

    private final CaseCancellationApplicationService service;

    @POST
    public Response requestCancellation(
            @PathParam("caseId") String caseId,
            CancellationRequest request
    ) {
        service.requestCancellation(caseId, request);

        return Response.accepted(Map.of(
                "caseId", caseId,
                "status", "CANCELLATION_REQUEST_ACCEPTED"
        )).build();
    }
}
```

## Application service

```java id="vl8blk"
public final class CaseCancellationApplicationService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final CamundaMessageService camundaMessageService;

    public void requestCancellation(String caseId, CancellationRequest request) {
        caseRepository.insertCancellationRequest(
                caseId,
                request.reasonCode(),
                request.requestedBy()
        );

        camundaMessageService.publishMessage(
                "CancelCaseRequested",
                caseId,
                Map.of(
                        "cancellationReasonCode", request.reasonCode(),
                        "cancellationRequestedBy", request.requestedBy()
                )
        );
    }
}
```

Practical invariant:

```text id="jif9i7"
Persist domain request first.
Then correlate BPMN message.
```

---

# 21. Implementation Pattern: Subprocess Timeout

## BPMN

```text id="x0dywl"
Subprocess: External Verification
  ├── Send Verification Request
  └── Wait Verification Response

Boundary Timer: PT48H
  ↓
Escalate Verification Timeout
```

## Runtime behavior

```text id="wa59ak"
Jika external verification belum selesai dalam 48 jam,
semua active work di subprocess dihentikan,
lalu proses masuk escalation path.
```

## Practical handling

| Concern | Implementation |
|---|---|
| External callback setelah timeout | Simpan sebagai late callback, jangan correlate blindly |
| External request sudah dikirim | Mark request as timed out |
| Officer escalation | Create user task |
| Audit | Record timeout event |
| Notification | Inform applicant/internal team |

## DB table idea

```sql id="p5zpwt"
CREATE TABLE external_verification_request (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    provider VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    request_reference_no VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    requested_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    timed_out_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    CONSTRAINT uq_ext_verification_request UNIQUE (provider, request_reference_no)
);
```

---

# 22. Error Propagation Strategy

Subprocess design harus menjawab:

```text id="e1puqg"
Kalau child process gagal, parent harus apa?
Kalau subprocess timeout, semua task di dalamnya dibatalkan atau tidak?
Kalau event subprocess interrupting jalan, status domain apa yang berubah?
Kalau call activity child menghasilkan error, parent catch atau incident?
```

## Pattern

| Failure Type | BPMN Pattern | Implementation |
|---|---|---|
| Technical transient failure | Job retry / incident | Worker fail job with retries |
| Business validation failure | Normal output + gateway | `validationStatus = INVALID` |
| Fatal business error | BPMN error boundary | Parent catches error path |
| SLA timeout | Boundary timer on subprocess | Escalation path |
| Cancel request | Interrupting event subprocess | Cancel active work and update domain state |
| Reminder | Non-interrupting event subprocess/timer boundary | Send reminder, main flow continues |
| External callback missing | Event-based gateway + timer | Timeout branch |
| Need rollback | Compensation process | Explicit reversal tasks |

---

# 23. Variable Scope & Mapping Rules

## Rule 1 - Jangan passing semua variable

Buruk:

```text id="kmg6i6"
Propagate all parent variables to called process.
Propagate all child variables back to parent.
```

Risiko:

```text id="w2q8z1"
Variable name collision
Security issue
Contract tidak jelas
Parent-child coupling terlalu kuat
```

## Rule 2 - Gunakan contract eksplisit

Baik:

```text id="z7dxky"
Input:
  caseId
  applicantId
  caseType

Output:
  riskBand
  riskScore
  assessmentReferenceNo
```

## Rule 3 - Pakai prefix bila perlu

Untuk mencegah collision:

```text id="q284op"
risk.assessmentId
risk.score
risk.band
kyc.status
kyc.reasonCodes
```

Atau gunakan object variable kecil:

```json id="jrv8s9"
{
  "riskAssessment": {
    "assessmentId": "RA-001",
    "score": 87,
    "band": "HIGH",
    "reasonCodes": ["DUPLICATE_HISTORY", "HIGH_VALUE"]
  }
}
```

---

# 24. Subprocess Naming Convention

## Embedded subprocess

Gunakan noun phrase atau phase name:

```text id="iuord7"
Initial Validation
External Verification
Manual Review
Case Registration
Notification Handling
```

## Call activity

Gunakan nama reusable process:

```text id="aaovmw"
Run Risk Assessment
Perform KYC Check
Verify Documents
Execute Notification Workflow
```

## Event subprocess

Gunakan event-triggered handler:

```text id="i27icl"
Handle Cancellation Request
Handle SLA Breach
Handle Emergency Stop
Handle External Update
Handle Escalation
```

## Ad-hoc subprocess

Gunakan flexible action container:

```text id="yq2tu8"
Perform Investigation Actions
Resolve Case Exceptions
Collect Additional Evidence
Handle Remediation Actions
```

---

# 25. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Contoh | Dampak | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Subprocess sebagai folder kosmetik | Semua task dimasukkan ke subprocess tanpa semantic | Tidak membantu execution/review | Gunakan subprocess untuk scope/fase/error boundary |
| Call activity untuk task kecil | `Call Validate Email` | Over-fragmented process | Service task atau embedded subprocess |
| Embedded subprocess untuk reusable workflow | KYC copy-paste di banyak process | Duplication/version drift | Call activity |
| Passing semua variables | Parent-child saling tergantung | Contract rapuh | Explicit input/output mapping |
| Binding `latest` sembarangan | Child process berubah tanpa parent aware | Regression/audit risk | `deployment` atau `versionTag` |
| Event subprocess interrupting tanpa audit | Cancel menghentikan process tanpa status jelas | Domain state inconsistent | Persist cancellation/termination reason |
| Non-interrupting event tanpa limit | Reminder/handler bisa berulang berlebihan | Notification spam/job explosion | Rate limit, condition, max count |
| Ad-hoc subprocess untuk proses ketat | Semua langkah harus deterministik tapi dibuat bebas | Sulit audit/test | Gunakan structured subprocess |
| Compensation dianggap DB rollback | External side effects tidak bisa rollback | Data/business inconsistency | Explicit compensating actions |
| Subprocess terlalu dalam | Nested 5 level | Sulit debug/onboard | Maksimal 2-3 level, sisanya call activity/documentation |

---

# 26. Practical Review Checklist

Untuk setiap subprocess/call activity, cek:

```text id="wzskrf"
1. Apakah ini grouping lokal atau reusable workflow?
2. Kalau reusable, kenapa bukan call activity?
3. Kalau hanya lokal, kenapa bukan embedded subprocess?
4. Apa input variables-nya?
5. Apa output variables-nya?
6. Apa error behavior-nya?
7. Apakah ada boundary timer/error/escalation?
8. Apakah subprocess bisa di-cancel?
9. Apa yang terjadi jika child process gagal?
10. Apa version binding untuk call activity?
11. Apakah variable propagation eksplisit?
12. Apakah subprocess punya owner?
13. Apakah ada test scenario untuk happy path, timeout, error, cancel?
14. Apakah observability cukup: caseId, parent instance, child instance?
15. Apakah diagram masih readable?
```

---

# 27. Testing Scenarios

Minimal test scenario untuk subprocess/call activity:

| Scenario | Expected |
|---|---|
| Happy path embedded subprocess selesai | Parent lanjut |
| Salah satu task dalam subprocess gagal transient | Retry/incident sesuai policy |
| Boundary timer subprocess ter-trigger | Semua active task dalam subprocess dihentikan |
| Boundary error subprocess ter-trigger | Error path parent berjalan |
| Call activity child selesai sukses | Output variables mapped ke parent |
| Call activity child gagal business error | Parent menangkap error atau masuk rejection path |
| Call activity process ID salah | Deployment/test gagal atau incident saat runtime |
| Version binding berubah | Parent-child compatibility tetap valid |
| Event subprocess interrupting ter-trigger | Main flow dihentikan dan handler berjalan |
| Event subprocess non-interrupting ter-trigger | Main flow tetap berjalan |
| Ad-hoc selected elements valid | Elemen terpilih aktif |
| Ad-hoc selected elements invalid | Incident/validation failure sesuai expectation |

---

# 28. Production Governance

## A. Versioning

```text id="d2kjcu"
Parent process version:
  case-intake:v2026.07

Called process version:
  risk-assessment:v2026.07

Decision/rule version:
  risk-band-dmn:v2026.07
```

Gunakan naming/tagging konsisten supaya audit bisa menjawab:

```text id="o36x7h"
Case ini diproses oleh versi BPMN apa?
Child process apa yang dipanggil?
Decision/rule version apa yang dipakai?
Input/output contract waktu itu seperti apa?
```

## B. Observability

Log minimal:

```text id="mhag93"
caseId
parentProcessInstanceKey
childProcessInstanceKey
bpmnProcessId
elementId
elementName
jobType
correlationKey
versionTag
```

## C. Migration Consideration

Jika parent process punya call activity ke child process:

```text id="v8d8az"
Ubah child process contract = bisa merusak parent process lama.
```

Karena itu, breaking change harus diperlakukan seperti API contract change:

```text id="p2q0dp"
Tambah field optional → aman
Rename field → breaking
Ubah enum output → breaking
Ubah semantic riskBand → breaking
Ubah error behavior → breaking
```

---

# 29. Mini Exercise

Ambil satu proses kantor, lalu klasifikasikan:

```text id="yivyov"
1. Step mana yang hanya perlu grouping lokal?
2. Step mana yang reusable lintas proses?
3. Step mana yang butuh timeout seluruh block?
4. Event apa yang bisa terjadi kapan saja?
5. Apakah ada aktivitas fleksibel/ad-hoc?
6. Apakah ada side effect yang butuh compensation?
7. Apa input/output contract untuk setiap called process?
8. Apa version binding yang aman?
```

Contoh hasil:

| Flow | Pattern |
|---|---|
| Initial case validation | Embedded subprocess |
| Risk assessment | Call activity |
| Cancellation while case active | Interrupting event subprocess |
| Reminder while review pending | Non-interrupting timer event subprocess/boundary |
| Investigation actions | Ad-hoc subprocess |
| Refund/reversal | Compensation process |

---

# 30. Ringkasan Seri 4

Inti seri ini:

```text id="nqvgu9"
Embedded subprocess = local grouping + scope
Collapsed subprocess = readability, bukan reuse
Call activity = reusable external BPMN process
Event subprocess = event-triggered handler while scope active
Ad-hoc subprocess = flexible/dynamic activity set
Transaction/compensation thinking = explicit business rollback/reversal
```

Decision rule paling penting:

```text id="c95rno"
Local complexity → embedded subprocess
Reusable workflow → call activity
Event can happen anytime in scope → event subprocess
Dynamic/optional case work → ad-hoc subprocess
External side effect rollback → compensation pattern
```

Untuk production Camunda modeling:

```text id="nhj4cp"
Subprocess harus punya semantic reason.
Call activity harus punya contract dan version strategy.
Event subprocess harus jelas interrupting atau non-interrupting.
Ad-hoc subprocess harus punya activation/completion rule.
Compensation harus explicit, bukan asumsi rollback teknis.
```

**Seri 4/12 selesai - Subprocess & Reuse Pattern.**

Rekomendasi berikutnya: **Seri 5/12 - Gateway Reference**, fokus pada Exclusive/XOR, Parallel/AND, Inclusive/OR, Event-based Gateway, condition/default flow, deadlock risk, race condition, dan pattern routing praktis untuk Camunda production workflow.
