**Sources:**

- [Overview | Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/subprocesses/)
- [BPMN 2.0 Symbols - A complete guide with examples. | Camunda](https://camunda.com/en/bpmn/reference/)

## Response:
7/16/2026, 9:47:30 AM

Lanjut ke **Seri 5/12 — Gateway Reference**. Seri ini fokus pada routing token: XOR, AND, OR, event-based gateway, default flow, serta risiko deadlock/race condition dalam workflow Camunda production.

# Seri 5/12 - Gateway Reference

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas **gateway BPMN** sebagai mekanisme routing token:

```text id="geq53p"
Exclusive Gateway / XOR
Parallel Gateway / AND
Inclusive Gateway / OR
Event-Based Gateway
Default Flow
Conditional Flow
Gateway Join
Gateway Anti-Pattern
```

Gateway bukan “task”. Gateway tidak melakukan pekerjaan bisnis, tidak call API, tidak update database, dan tidak menjalankan logic berat. Gateway hanya menentukan **ke mana token process bergerak** berdasarkan data, event, atau kebutuhan sinkronisasi.

Camunda 8.9 mendokumentasikan gateway sebagai elemen yang merutekan token dalam pola lebih kompleks daripada sequence flow biasa. Elemen gateway yang didukung meliputi **exclusive**, **parallel**, **event-based**, dan **inclusive gateway**. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/gateways/))

---

# 2. Mental Model Utama

```text id="fy7abv"
Gateway = routing control
Task    = work execution
Event   = something happens
```

Kesalahan umum:

```text id="m1mzhv"
Gateway: Validate Risk
```

Itu salah secara mental model. “Validate Risk” adalah pekerjaan, jadi harus menjadi task.

Lebih benar:

```text id="ouc1r1"
Service Task: Calculate Risk Score
  ↓
Gateway: riskScore >= 80?
  ├── yes → Senior Review
  └── no  → Auto Register
```

Gateway hanya membaca hasil kerja sebelumnya, misalnya process variable:

```json id="wfim2u"
{
  "riskScore": 87,
  "duplicateFound": false,
  "validationStatus": "VALID"
}
```

---

# 3. Diagram Decomposition

```text id="c0la3h"
BPMN Gateway Reference
│
├── 1. Exclusive Gateway / XOR
│   ├── One path only
│   ├── Data-based decision
│   ├── Condition expression
│   ├── Default flow
│   ├── First matching condition
│   └── Incident if no match and no default
│
├── 2. Parallel Gateway / AND
│   ├── Split all outgoing paths
│   ├── Concurrent execution
│   ├── Join waits for all incoming paths
│   ├── No condition
│   └── Deadlock risk if modeled incorrectly
│
├── 3. Inclusive Gateway / OR
│   ├── One or more paths
│   ├── Data-based multi-decision
│   ├── All matching conditions are taken
│   ├── Optional default flow
│   ├── Join waits for selected paths
│   └── Higher testing complexity
│
├── 4. Event-Based Gateway
│   ├── First event wins
│   ├── Message/timer/signal catch event
│   ├── Callback vs timeout
│   ├── External race handling
│   └── No data condition
│
├── 5. Conditional & Default Flow
│   ├── FEEL expression
│   ├── Boolean result
│   ├── Fallback route
│   └── Incident prevention
│
├── 6. Gateway Join Semantics
│   ├── XOR join = pass-through readability
│   ├── AND join = waits all incoming
│   ├── OR join = waits selected incoming
│   └── Event-based gateway = branching only
│
└── 7. Production Modeling
    ├── Naming
    ├── Variable contract
    ├── Test matrix
    ├── Deadlock avoidance
    ├── Race handling
    ├── Observability
    └── Anti-pattern cleanup
```

---

# 4. Quick Reference Table

| Gateway | Alias | Routing Meaning | Kapan Dipakai | Hindari Jika |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Exclusive Gateway | XOR | Pilih tepat satu path berdasarkan data | Approve/reject, valid/invalid, high/low risk | Lebih dari satu path harus jalan |
| Parallel Gateway | AND | Jalankan semua outgoing path | Audit + notify + enrichment berjalan bersamaan | Ada condition berbeda per path |
| Inclusive Gateway | OR | Jalankan satu atau lebih path berdasarkan condition | Multiple optional review: legal/compliance/finance | Bisa dimodelkan lebih jelas dengan beberapa XOR/AND |
| Event-Based Gateway | Event race | Tunggu event pertama yang terjadi | Callback vs timeout, payment received vs canceled | Keputusan berbasis variable, bukan event |
| Default Flow | Fallback | Path jika tidak ada condition match | Else branch / safe fallback | Dipakai untuk menyembunyikan rule utama |
| Conditional Flow | Guard | Sequence flow dengan boolean condition | Route berdasarkan variable | Condition kompleks dan tidak testable |

---

# 5. Exclusive Gateway / XOR

## Makna

Exclusive gateway memilih **satu** outgoing sequence flow berdasarkan data/process variables. Dalam Camunda 8.9, semua outgoing flow kecuali satu harus punya `conditionExpression`; flow yang tidak punya condition harus didefinisikan sebagai default flow. Camunda mengevaluasi condition berdasarkan urutan BPMN XML dan mengambil flow pertama yang bernilai true. Jika tidak ada condition yang true dan tidak ada default flow, incident dibuat. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/exclusive-gateways/))

```text id="fkoazj"
Gateway: riskScore >= 80?
  ├── yes → Senior Officer Review
  └── no  → Auto Register
```

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan XOR saat:

```text id="thkoi6"
Hanya satu path boleh jalan
Keputusan berbasis data/process variable
Path saling eksklusif secara bisnis
Ada fallback/default route
```

Contoh:

| Decision | Variable | Path |
|---|---|---|
| Case valid? | `validationStatus` | valid / invalid |
| High risk? | `riskScore` | senior review / auto register |
| Approved? | `reviewDecision` | approve / reject / revise |
| Duplicate found? | `duplicateFound` | merge / continue |
| Payment status? | `paymentStatus` | paid / failed / pending |

---

## Practical BPMN Example

```text id="kqg2nw"
Validate Case Data
  ↓
Gateway: validationStatus?
  ├── validationStatus = "VALID"
  │       ↓
  │   Check Duplicate Case
  │
  ├── validationStatus = "INVALID"
  │       ↓
  │   Reject Case
  │
  └── default
          ↓
      Manual Validation Review
```

## FEEL Condition Examples

Camunda gateway condition expression harus menghasilkan boolean dan dapat membaca process variables. Dokumentasi Camunda memberi contoh seperti `= totalPrice > 100`, `= order.customer = "Paul"`, dan kombinasi `or` / `and`. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/exclusive-gateways/))

```text id="devy8d"
= validationStatus = "VALID"
= validationStatus = "INVALID"
= riskScore >= 80
= duplicateFound = true
= reviewDecision = "APPROVED"
= reviewDecision = "REJECTED"
= riskScore >= 80 and caseType = "ENFORCEMENT"
```

## Default Flow Rule

Default flow bukan sekadar flow tanpa condition. Di Camunda Modeler, default flow harus diset di property gateway. Camunda secara eksplisit menyebut empty condition tidak otomatis membuat flow menjadi default. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/exclusive-gateways/))

Praktisnya:

```text id="tkc9i9"
Selalu set default flow untuk XOR gateway production.
Default path sebaiknya aman:
  - manual review
  - technical fallback
  - reject with controlled reason
  - incident-handling route
```

## Anti-Pattern XOR

| Anti-Pattern | Dampak | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Tidak ada default flow | Incident jika tidak ada condition match | Tambahkan default |
| Condition overlap | Camunda mengambil first match berdasarkan XML order | Buat condition mutually exclusive |
| Gateway melakukan kerja | Semantik salah | Pindahkan kerja ke service/user task |
| Condition membaca variable yang belum ada | Incident / routing salah | Pastikan variable dibuat sebelum gateway |
| Terlalu banyak branch | Diagram sulit dibaca | Gunakan DMN/business rule task sebelum gateway |
| Default flow berisi rule penting | Rule tersembunyi | Buat explicit condition |

---

# 6. Parallel Gateway / AND

## Makna

Parallel gateway menjalankan **semua** outgoing sequence flow. Camunda 8.9 mendokumentasikan bahwa ketika parallel gateway dengan multiple outgoing sequence flow dimasuki, semua flow diambil; path berjalan concurrently dan independently. Parallel join menunggu sampai setiap incoming sequence diambil. Catatan penting: “concurrent” di sini bukan parallel thread; record process instance ditulis ke partition/stream processor yang sama. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/parallel-gateways/))

```text id="cwsput"
After Case Registered
  ↓
Parallel Gateway
  ├── Send Applicant Notification
  ├── Write Audit Event
  └── Start Background Enrichment
  ↓
Parallel Join
  ↓
Continue
```

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan AND saat:

```text id="ed1sz6"
Semua path harus dijalankan
Tidak ada conditional branch
Path bisa berjalan independen
Join harus menunggu semua selesai
```

Contoh:

| Scenario | Parallel Paths |
|---|---|
| Case registered | Notify applicant + write audit + update dashboard |
| Start investigation | Assign officer + create checklist + notify supervisor |
| Close case | Archive document + notify parties + update reporting mart |
| Post-approval | Generate letter + update status + send integration event |

## Practical Pattern

```text id="4voblt"
Register Case
  ↓
AND Split
  ├── Send Notification
  ├── Persist Audit Trail
  └── Publish CaseRegistered Event
  ↓
AND Join
  ↓
End
```

## Kapan Tidak Perlu Join?

Kalau semua branch adalah side-effect final dan tidak ada step berikutnya yang membutuhkan semuanya selesai, join bisa tidak diperlukan secara model. Tapi secara production, hati-hati: tanpa join, kamu harus jelas apakah proses boleh selesai saat sebagian branch masih aktif atau branch punya end event sendiri.

Practical rule:

```text id="kjjm8x"
Jika ada step setelah parallel block yang membutuhkan semua hasil branch, pakai AND join.
Jika branch adalah independent terminal side-effect, boleh desain tiap branch menuju end sendiri, tapi pastikan audit/observability jelas.
```

## Deadlock Risk

Parallel join menunggu semua incoming path. Jika model tidak simetris, token bisa menunggu path yang tidak pernah aktif.

Buruk:

```text id="64z9o8"
XOR:
  ├── Path A ──────┐
  └── Path B       │
                   ▼
              AND Join
```

Masalah:

```text id="imczti"
AND join menunggu semua incoming flow,
padahal XOR hanya mengaktifkan satu path.
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="efk2e4"
XOR split harus join dengan XOR/pass-through merge,
bukan AND join.
```

## Anti-Pattern AND

| Anti-Pattern | Dampak | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| AND join setelah XOR split | Token stuck/deadlock | Gunakan XOR merge |
| Condition di parallel outgoing | Semantik kacau | Gunakan inclusive/exclusive gateway |
| Parallel task update row sama tanpa locking | Race/data conflict | Optimistic locking/idempotency |
| Branch tidak idempotent | Retry duplicate side effect | Idempotency key per branch |
| Join tidak perlu tapi dipasang | Proses tertahan oleh side-effect minor | Evaluasi apakah branch terminal |
| Semua branch call external API tanpa timeout | Instance lama/stuck | Timeout/retry/incident policy |

---

# 7. Inclusive Gateway / OR

## Makna

Inclusive gateway menjalankan **satu atau lebih** outgoing path berdasarkan condition. Camunda 8.9 menjelaskan inclusive gateway sebagai OR-gateway untuk membuat multiple decisions berbasis data/process variables; semua outgoing sequence flow harus punya condition jika ada multiple outgoing, dan optional default flow dapat dipakai. Saat gateway dimasuki, semua condition dievaluasi dan semua flow yang fulfilled diambil. Jika tidak ada condition true dan tidak ada default flow, incident dibuat. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/inclusive-gateways/))

```text id="viqhz3"
Gateway: Required Reviews?
  ├── legalReviewRequired = true       → Legal Review
  ├── complianceReviewRequired = true  → Compliance Review
  ├── financeReviewRequired = true     → Finance Review
  └── default                          → No Additional Review
```

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan OR saat:

```text id="etkywb"
Satu atau lebih path bisa aktif
Path dipilih berdasarkan condition
Jumlah path aktif bergantung data
Join harus menunggu hanya path yang memang aktif
```

Contoh:

| Scenario | Possible Paths |
|---|---|
| Case review | Legal review, compliance review, finance review |
| Document validation | ID doc, company doc, tax doc |
| Investigation action | Interview, site visit, registry check |
| Notification | Email, SMS, in-app notification |
| Risk remediation | Freeze account, manual review, external report |

## Practical BPMN Example

```text id="qne7mg"
Determine Required Reviews
  ↓
OR Gateway: Required Reviews?
  ├── = legalReviewRequired       → Legal Review
  ├── = complianceReviewRequired  → Compliance Review
  ├── = financeReviewRequired     → Finance Review
  └── default                     → Skip Additional Review
  ↓
OR Join
  ↓
Consolidate Review Result
```

## Inclusive Join Semantics

Converging inclusive gateway menunggu incoming path yang memang diambil. Camunda menjelaskan bahwa converging inclusive gateway selesai jika semua incoming sequence flow telah diambil setidaknya sekali, atau tidak ada path dari active flow node yang bisa mencapai gateway kecuali incoming path yang sudah diambil. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/inclusive-gateways/))

Practical interpretation:

```text id="st6olr"
OR join tidak menunggu semua possible branch.
OR join menunggu branch yang dipilih/aktif.
```

## Kenapa OR Gateway Perlu Hati-Hati

Inclusive gateway sangat powerful, tapi lebih sulit diuji daripada XOR dan AND.

Dengan 3 condition:

```text id="vffmfr"
legalReviewRequired
complianceReviewRequired
financeReviewRequired
```

Kombinasi test minimal:

```text id="c33zii"
none
legal only
compliance only
finance only
legal + compliance
legal + finance
compliance + finance
all three
```

Jumlah kombinasi = `2^n`.

## Anti-Pattern OR

| Anti-Pattern | Dampak | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| OR dipakai karena bingung XOR/AND | Model sulit dipahami | Gunakan gateway yang lebih sederhana |
| Banyak branch tanpa test matrix | Bug kombinasi | Buat combinatorial test |
| Default flow masih punya condition | Semantik salah | Default flow tanpa condition |
| OR join ditempatkan tidak tepat | Token stuck atau merge terlalu cepat | Review selected-path behavior |
| Branch update domain state yang sama | Conflict/race | State transition table + lock/idempotency |
| Condition terlalu kompleks | Tidak maintainable | Pindahkan ke DMN/service task |

---

# 8. Event-Based Gateway

## Makna

Event-based gateway membuat keputusan berdasarkan **event pertama yang terjadi**, bukan berdasarkan data variable. Camunda 8.9 mensyaratkan event-based gateway punya minimal dua outgoing sequence flow; setiap flow harus menuju intermediate catch event bertipe timer, message, atau signal. Saat gateway dimasuki, process instance menunggu sampai salah satu event ter-trigger; event pertama menang dan event lain tidak bisa trigger setelah itu. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/event-based-gateways/))

```text id="jeen96"
Event-Based Gateway
  ├── Message: PaymentReceived
  └── Timer: PT24H
```

Makna:

```text id="a3xi6z"
Kalau PaymentReceived datang duluan → lanjut payment success.
Kalau timer 24 jam terjadi duluan → lanjut timeout path.
```

## Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan event-based gateway saat:

```text id="x2i676"
Process menunggu salah satu dari beberapa event
Ada race callback vs timeout
Ada race approve vs cancel
Ada race external response vs SLA breach
Keputusan tidak bisa dibuat sekarang karena event belum terjadi
```

Contoh:

| Race | Event 1 | Event 2 |
|---|---|---|
| Payment | PaymentReceived | PaymentTimeout |
| Document | AdditionalDocumentSubmitted | SubmissionDeadlineExpired |
| External registry | RegistryCheckCompleted | RegistryCheckTimedOut |
| Approval | ManagerApproved | ApprovalSlaExpired |
| Cancellation | ExternalCompleted | CustomerCancelled |

## Practical BPMN Example

```text id="x6iml7"
Send Registry Check Request
  ↓
Event-Based Gateway
  ├── Message Catch: RegistryCheckCompleted
  │       ↓
  │   Continue Case Processing
  │
  └── Timer Catch: PT48H
          ↓
      Escalate Registry Timeout
```

## Message Correlation Mapping

```text id="bp9flf"
Message name:
  RegistryCheckCompleted

Correlation key:
  caseId

Payload:
  registryStatus
  registryReferenceNo
  checkedAt
```

## Jersey Callback Sketch

```java id="pqxtag"
@Path("/registry/callbacks")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class RegistryCallbackResource {

    private final RegistryCallbackService service;

    @POST
    public Response receive(RegistryCallbackRequest request) {
        service.handle(request);

        return Response.accepted(Map.of(
                "caseId", request.caseId(),
                "accepted", true
        )).build();
    }
}
```

```java id="wf9yv8"
public final class RegistryCallbackService {

    private final RegistryResultRepository repository;
    private final CamundaMessageService camundaMessageService;

    public void handle(RegistryCallbackRequest request) {
        repository.upsertCallback(
                request.caseId(),
                request.registryReferenceNo(),
                request.registryStatus(),
                request.rawPayload()
        );

        camundaMessageService.publishMessage(
                "RegistryCheckCompleted",
                request.caseId(),
                Map.of(
                        "registryStatus", request.registryStatus(),
                        "registryReferenceNo", request.registryReferenceNo()
                )
        );
    }
}
```

Critical rule:

```text id="p2wrv5"
Persist callback first, then publish/correlate message.
```

## Late Callback Handling

Event-based gateway creates a race. Kalau timer menang duluan, message event tidak lagi menunggu. Jika callback datang setelah timeout:

```text id="tg4cvq"
Jangan crash.
Jangan blindly reopen process.
Simpan sebagai late callback.
Evaluasi apakah perlu manual reconciliation.
```

DB pattern:

```sql id="e11zex"
CREATE TABLE external_callback_log (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    callback_type VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    correlation_key VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    external_reference_no VARCHAR(128),
    callback_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    correlated BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    late_callback BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    raw_payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT uq_external_callback UNIQUE (
        callback_type,
        correlation_key,
        external_reference_no
    )
);
```

---

# 9. Conditional Flow dan FEEL Expression

Gateway routing di Camunda biasanya memakai expression.

Contoh practical:

```text id="d9lo1r"
= riskScore >= 80
= validationStatus = "VALID"
= duplicateFound = false
= reviewDecision = "APPROVED"
= caseType = "ENFORCEMENT" and riskScore >= 70
= list contains(requiredReviews, "LEGAL")
```

## Rule Expression Production

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Boolean only | Condition harus menghasilkan true/false |
| Variable tersedia | Jangan baca variable yang belum dibuat |
| Condition sederhana | Logic kompleks pindahkan ke DMN/service task |
| Mutually exclusive untuk XOR | Hindari overlap |
| Exhaustive dengan default | Jangan biarkan no-match |
| Testable | Setiap branch harus punya test case |
| Business-readable | Nama sequence flow harus jelas |

## Buruk

```text id="gp9w8e"
= riskScore > 80 or (caseType = "A" and amount > 100000 and applicant.segment = "X" and flags[1] = true)
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="xjj8zs"
Business Rule Task: Determine Review Route
  output: reviewRoute = "SENIOR_REVIEW"

Gateway:
  = reviewRoute = "SENIOR_REVIEW"
```

---

# 10. Gateway Join Semantics

## XOR Join

Exclusive gateway bisa dipakai sebagai join untuk readability. Camunda menyebut joining exclusive gateway punya pass-through semantics dan tidak menggabungkan concurrent flows seperti parallel gateway. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/exclusive-gateways/))

```text id="xcjbh3"
Approve Path ─┐
Reject Path  ─┼→ XOR Join → Continue
Revise Path  ─┘
```

Makna:

```text id="jq0fro"
Token mana pun yang datang langsung lewat.
Tidak menunggu path lain.
```

## AND Join

```text id="l56o1h"
Branch A ─┐
Branch B ─┼→ AND Join → Continue
Branch C ─┘
```

Makna:

```text id="u3du57"
Menunggu semua incoming path.
```

## OR Join

```text id="qnn2c4"
Selected Branch A ─┐
Selected Branch B ─┼→ OR Join → Continue
Possible Branch C ─┘
```

Makna:

```text id="u5rh37"
Menunggu branch yang memang dipilih.
Tidak menunggu branch yang tidak aktif.
```

## Practical Join Rule

```text id="nq6eit"
XOR split → XOR/pass-through join
AND split → AND join
OR split  → OR join
Event-based split → biasanya tidak perlu join; hanya satu event menang
```

---

# 11. Gateway Decision Matrix

```text id="rhc9ei"
Apakah keputusan berdasarkan event yang belum terjadi?
  ├─ Ya → Event-Based Gateway
  └─ Tidak
      │
      ├─ Apakah semua path harus jalan?
      │    └─ Ya → Parallel Gateway
      │
      ├─ Apakah tepat satu path harus jalan?
      │    └─ Ya → Exclusive Gateway
      │
      ├─ Apakah satu atau lebih path bisa jalan?
      │    └─ Ya → Inclusive Gateway
      │
      └─ Jika logic rumit
           → Business Rule Task / DMN dulu, gateway setelahnya
```

---

# 12. Practical Patterns

## Pattern 1 - Simple approval

```text id="vccos0"
User Task: Review Case
  ↓
XOR Gateway: reviewDecision?
  ├── APPROVED → Register Case
  ├── REJECTED → Reject Case
  └── default  → Request Clarification
```

Variables:

```json id="duc2db"
{
  "reviewDecision": "APPROVED",
  "reviewComment": "Sufficient evidence"
}
```

---

## Pattern 2 - Risk-based routing

```text id="ca19ts"
Business Rule Task: Determine Risk Band
  ↓
XOR Gateway: riskBand?
  ├── HIGH   → Senior Review
  ├── MEDIUM → Officer Review
  ├── LOW    → Auto Register
  └── default → Manual Triage
```

Practical rule:

```text id="qdyh20"
Gateway tidak menghitung risk.
Gateway hanya membaca riskBand.
```

---

## Pattern 3 - Parallel post-registration actions

```text id="q4iutd"
Register Case
  ↓
AND Split
  ├── Send Applicant Notification
  ├── Publish CaseRegistered Event
  └── Write Audit Record
  ↓
AND Join
  ↓
End
```

Implementation concern:

```text id="ftztib"
Setiap branch harus idempotent.
Notification duplicate harus dicegah.
Audit insert harus punya idempotency key.
Event publish sebaiknya pakai outbox.
```

---

## Pattern 4 - Optional multiple reviews

```text id="awz59p"
Determine Required Reviews
  ↓
OR Gateway
  ├── LEGAL required      → Legal Review
  ├── COMPLIANCE required → Compliance Review
  ├── FINANCE required    → Finance Review
  └── default             → Skip Reviews
  ↓
OR Join
  ↓
Consolidate Review Result
```

Implementation concern:

```text id="wmcx8y"
Review result harus disimpan per review type.
Join consolidation harus tahu review mana yang required.
```

Table:

```sql id="cm7lsp"
CREATE TABLE case_review_requirement (
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    review_type VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    required BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    completed BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    decision VARCHAR(32),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    PRIMARY KEY (case_id, review_type)
);
```

---

## Pattern 5 - Callback vs timeout

```text id="l0hkph"
Send External Verification Request
  ↓
Event-Based Gateway
  ├── Message: VerificationCompleted
  │       ↓
  │   Continue Processing
  │
  └── Timer: PT24H
          ↓
      Escalate Timeout
```

Implementation concern:

```text id="r8g4nb"
Callback endpoint must be idempotent.
Timeout path must mark request timed out.
Late callback must be stored and reconciled.
```

---

# 13. Java/Jersey/MyBatis Implementation Concerns

## A. Gateway condition depends on worker output

Gateway condition should depend on variables produced by previous tasks.

Example service task output:

```java id="x9g39x"
client.newCompleteCommand(job.getKey())
        .variables(Map.of(
                "validationStatus", "VALID",
                "duplicateFound", false,
                "riskScore", 87,
                "riskBand", "HIGH"
        ))
        .send()
        .join();
```

Gateway after that:

```text id="fmop4j"
= validationStatus = "VALID"
= riskBand = "HIGH"
= duplicateFound = false
```

## B. Persist decision result before completing worker

Bad pattern:

```text id="hv6x5d"
Complete Camunda job
Then persist DB state
```

Risk:

```text id="evlh96"
Process moves forward, but domain DB state fails.
```

Better:

```text id="k6p03o"
Persist DB state
Commit transaction
Complete Camunda job with variables
```

## C. Gateway variable should reflect domain state

Example:

```sql id="wib1sx"
UPDATE case_record
SET
    risk_score = #{riskScore},
    risk_band = #{riskBand},
    updated_at = now()
WHERE case_id = #{caseId};
```

Then complete job:

```java id="kgwmd8"
client.newCompleteCommand(job.getKey())
        .variables(Map.of(
                "riskScore", result.riskScore(),
                "riskBand", result.riskBand()
        ))
        .send()
        .join();
```

## D. Avoid variable-only truth

For regulatory/case management:

```text id="h1v91p"
Process variable = routing context
Domain DB = authoritative state
```

Jangan hanya mengandalkan variable untuk audit final. Persist decision penting ke PostgreSQL.

---

# 14. Gateway Test Matrix

## XOR Test Matrix

Contoh gateway:

```text id="wv60su"
Gateway: riskBand?
  HIGH → Senior Review
  MEDIUM → Officer Review
  LOW → Auto Register
  default → Manual Triage
```

Test:

| Case | Input | Expected Path |
|---|---|---|
| High risk | `riskBand=HIGH` | Senior Review |
| Medium risk | `riskBand=MEDIUM` | Officer Review |
| Low risk | `riskBand=LOW` | Auto Register |
| Unknown | `riskBand=null` / `UNKNOWN` | Manual Triage |
| Missing variable | no `riskBand` | Controlled fallback or incident expected |

## AND Test Matrix

| Case | Expected |
|---|---|
| All branches complete | Join passes |
| One branch fails transient | Retry/incident |
| One branch long-running | Join waits |
| Branch duplicate retry | No duplicate side effect |
| Branch updates same row | No lost update |

## OR Test Matrix

Untuk `n` optional branch, minimal uji:

```text id="tjkydl"
No branch true
Each single branch true
All branches true
Important combinations
Conflict combinations
```

## Event-Based Test Matrix

| Case | Expected |
|---|---|
| Message arrives before timer | Message path taken |
| Timer fires before message | Timeout path taken |
| Duplicate message | Idempotent no duplicate side effect |
| Late message after timeout | Stored as late/reconciled |
| Wrong correlation key | Not correlated, logged/rejected |
| Missing message payload | Callback stored as invalid / not correlated |

---

# 15. Naming Convention

## Gateway Name

Gateway name sebaiknya berbentuk pertanyaan singkat:

```text id="qgrv3e"
Case valid?
High risk?
Duplicate found?
Review approved?
Additional reviews required?
External response received before timeout?
```

## Sequence Flow Name

Sequence flow keluar gateway harus menjawab pertanyaan:

```text id="w8hjy4"
yes
no
valid
invalid
approved
rejected
high
medium
low
timeout
received
```

## Bad Naming

```text id="nh8vvu"
Gateway1
Check
Process decision
If okay
Route
```

## Better Naming

```text id="dwrh2c"
Gateway: Case valid?
Flow: valid
Flow: invalid
Flow: default manual review
```

---

# 16. Production Checklist

Untuk setiap gateway, cek:

```text id="mjmu4t"
1. Gateway ini routing, bukan melakukan pekerjaan?
2. Gateway type sudah tepat: XOR, AND, OR, event-based?
3. Jika XOR/OR, semua condition jelas?
4. Jika XOR, condition mutually exclusive?
5. Jika XOR/OR, ada default flow?
6. Jika AND, semua branch memang wajib jalan?
7. Jika AND join, semua incoming path pasti aktif?
8. Jika OR, kombinasi branch sudah dites?
9. Jika event-based, outgoing menuju catch event yang valid?
10. Jika message event, correlation key jelas?
11. Jika timer event, timeout business-reasonable?
12. Jika join, join type cocok dengan split type?
13. Variable yang dibaca tersedia sebelum gateway?
14. Path failure/unknown tidak tersembunyi?
15. Nama gateway dan sequence flow readable?
```

---

# 17. Common Gateway Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Gejala | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|---|
| Gateway sebagai task | “Gateway: Validate Data” | Semantik salah | Pakai service task lalu gateway |
| XOR tanpa default | No condition match → incident | Fragile runtime | Tambahkan default |
| Overlapping XOR condition | Multiple true, first XML order wins | Routing tidak terlihat dari diagram | Buat mutually exclusive |
| AND join setelah XOR | Token stuck | Deadlock | XOR merge |
| OR gateway untuk logic sederhana | Sulit test | Complexity naik | XOR/AND biasa |
| Event-based untuk data decision | Menunggu event padahal data sudah ada | Model salah | XOR/OR |
| Gateway condition terlalu kompleks | Sulit audit/test | Hidden business logic | DMN/business rule task |
| Message race tanpa timeout | Instance menggantung | Operational debt | Event-based gateway + timer |
| Parallel branch non-idempotent | Retry duplicate side effects | Data inconsistency | Idempotency key/outbox |
| Join hilang saat hasil dibutuhkan | Step berikutnya jalan terlalu cepat | Incomplete data | Tambahkan join |

---

# 18. Practical Review Example

Misal proses:

```text id="ofgmb0"
Submit Case
  ↓
Validate Case
  ↓
Calculate Risk
  ↓
Gateway
```

Pertanyaan review:

```text id="eq7xmt"
Gateway ini membaca variable apa?
Apakah variable itu dipersist?
Apakah variable itu selalu tersedia?
Apa semua possible value sudah ditangani?
Apa default path?
Kalau riskScore null, lari ke mana?
Kalau riskBand UNKNOWN, lari ke mana?
Apakah route ini perlu audit?
Apakah route ini perlu DMN?
```

Better model:

```text id="rxql0c"
Validate Case Data
  ↓
Gateway: case valid?
  ├── valid
  │     ↓
  │   Determine Risk Band
  │     ↓
  │   Gateway: riskBand?
  │     ├── HIGH   → Senior Review
  │     ├── MEDIUM → Officer Review
  │     ├── LOW    → Auto Register
  │     └── default → Manual Triage
  │
  └── invalid
        ↓
      Reject Case
```

---

# 19. Mini Exercise

Ambil satu BPMN di kantor dan cari semua gateway. Untuk setiap gateway, isi tabel ini:

| Gateway Name | Type | Input Variables / Events | Outgoing Paths | Default? | Join Pair | Risk |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Case valid? | XOR | `validationStatus` | valid/invalid/default | Ya | XOR merge | Missing variable |
| Run post-registration actions | AND | none | notify/audit/event | N/A | AND join | Branch retry |
| Required reviews? | OR | `requiredReviews` | legal/compliance/finance/default | Ya | OR join | Combination complexity |
| Registry response? | Event-based | message/timer | completed/timeout | N/A | N/A | Late callback |

Jika tabel ini tidak bisa diisi, gateway belum cukup jelas untuk production review.

---

# 20. Ringkasan Seri 5

Inti seri ini:

```text id="vvpyss"
Exclusive/XOR      = pilih tepat satu path berdasarkan data
Parallel/AND       = jalankan semua path
Inclusive/OR       = jalankan satu atau lebih path berdasarkan data
Event-Based        = event pertama yang terjadi menang
Default Flow       = fallback jika tidak ada condition match
Conditional Flow   = boolean guard berbasis variable
```

Rule paling penting:

```text id="nuzew6"
Gateway tidak melakukan pekerjaan.
Gateway hanya merutekan token.
```

Decision rule praktis:

```text id="z6o9cm"
Data decision, one path only      → XOR
Data decision, multiple paths     → OR
All paths must run                → AND
First external event wins         → Event-Based
Logic too complex                 → DMN/service task first, gateway after
```

Untuk production Camunda:

```text id="iwr9yj"
XOR/OR wajib punya condition yang testable.
XOR/OR sebaiknya punya default flow.
AND join harus cocok dengan AND split.
Event-based gateway harus punya timeout/correlation strategy.
Gateway condition tidak boleh menyembunyikan business logic kompleks.
```

---

**Seri 5/12 selesai - Gateway Reference.**

Rekomendasi berikutnya: **Seri 6/12 - Start / Intermediate / End Events**, fokus pada event lifecycle, catching vs throwing, start event types, intermediate event behavior, end event semantics, dan mapping praktis ke REST trigger, message correlation, timer schedule, error, escalation, serta completion state di Camunda.

**Sources:**

- [Overview | Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/gateways/)

## Response:
7/16/2026, 9:49:18 AM

Lanjut ke **Seri 6/12 — Start / Intermediate / End Events**. Seri ini akan menata event sebagai trigger, wait state, signal bisnis/teknis, dan completion semantics—bukan sekadar lingkaran di diagram.

# Seri 6/12 - Start / Intermediate / End Events

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas **event lifecycle** dalam BPMN:

```text
Start Event        = bagaimana process dimulai
Intermediate Event = sesuatu yang terjadi di tengah process
End Event          = bagaimana execution path/process selesai
```

Dalam BPMN, event adalah “sesuatu yang terjadi”. Camunda menjelaskan event sebagai elemen yang bisa **ditangkap / catching** atau **dilempar / throwing**. Contoh: catching message event membuat token lanjut setelah message yang sesuai diterima. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Mental model praktis:

```text
Event bukan pekerjaan.
Event adalah trigger, wait state, signal, notification, timeout, atau completion marker.
```

---

# 2. Diagram Decomposition

```text
BPMN Events
│
├── 1. Start Events
│   ├── None Start Event
│   ├── Message Start Event
│   ├── Timer Start Event
│   ├── Signal Start Event
│   ├── Conditional Start Event
│   └── Multiple / advanced start thinking
│
├── 2. Intermediate Events
│   ├── Intermediate Catch Event
│   │   ├── Message Catch
│   │   ├── Timer Catch
│   │   ├── Signal Catch
│   │   ├── Conditional Catch
│   │   ├── Link Catch
│   │   └── Compensation Catch
│   │
│   └── Intermediate Throw Event
│       ├── Message Throw
│       ├── Signal Throw
│       ├── Escalation Throw
│       ├── Link Throw
│       └── Compensation Throw
│
├── 3. End Events
│   ├── None End Event
│   ├── Terminate End Event
│   ├── Message End Event
│   ├── Error End Event
│   ├── Escalation End Event
│   ├── Signal End Event
│   └── Compensation End Thinking
│
├── 4. Runtime Semantics
│   ├── Process start
│   ├── Token wait state
│   ├── Message subscription
│   ├── Timer scheduling
│   ├── Event propagation
│   ├── Scope termination
│   └── Path completion
│
└── 5. Implementation Mapping
    ├── REST endpoint starts process
    ├── Callback endpoint correlates message
    ├── Timer handled by engine
    ├── Worker throws error/escalation
    ├── DB stores domain state
    └── Audit records final outcome
```

---

# 3. Quick Reference Table

| Event Category | Event Type | Practical Meaning | Common Implementation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Start | None start | Process dimulai secara eksplisit oleh API/command | Jersey endpoint start process |
| Start | Message start | Process dimulai saat message tertentu diterima | External event/callback starts new instance |
| Start | Timer start | Process dimulai oleh jadwal | Daily batch, SLA sweep, periodic job |
| Start | Signal start | Process dimulai oleh broadcast signal | Global operational event |
| Start | Conditional start | Process dimulai saat condition terpenuhi | Variable/state-based trigger |
| Intermediate catch | Message catch | Process menunggu message tertentu | Callback correlation by `caseId` |
| Intermediate catch | Timer catch | Process menunggu waktu tertentu | Delay, timeout, reminder |
| Intermediate catch | Signal catch | Process menunggu broadcast signal | System-wide trigger |
| Intermediate catch | Conditional catch | Process menunggu condition menjadi true | Variable update triggers continuation |
| Intermediate throw | Message throw | Process mengirim message keluar | Publish event / send command |
| Intermediate throw | Signal throw | Process broadcast signal | Notify many process instances |
| Intermediate throw | Escalation throw | Process menaikkan kondisi bisnis | Escalate to parent/scope handler |
| End | None end | Path selesai normal | No special side effect |
| End | Terminate end | Menghentikan seluruh scope/process | Cancel all active branches |
| End | Message end | Process selesai sambil mengirim message | Publish final result |
| End | Error end | Process/path selesai dengan BPMN error | Caught by boundary/event subprocess |
| End | Escalation end | Process/path selesai sambil escalate | Business escalation |
| End | Signal end | Process selesai sambil broadcast signal | Inform many listeners |

---

# 4. Mental Model: Catching vs Throwing

## Catching event

Catching event berarti process **menunggu atau bereaksi terhadap sesuatu**.

```text
Process waits until something happens.
```

Contoh:

```text
Wait Payment Received
Wait External Registry Response
Wait 24 Hours
Wait Cancellation Request
```

Implementation consequence:

```text
Token berhenti.
Engine membuat subscription/timer/wait state.
Process lanjut hanya jika event terpenuhi.
```

---

## Throwing event

Throwing event berarti process **menghasilkan atau melempar sesuatu**.

```text
Process emits something.
```

Contoh:

```text
Throw Message: CaseRegistered
Throw Signal: EmergencyStop
Throw Escalation: SLA Breached
Throw Error: ValidationFailed
```

Implementation consequence:

```text
Process tidak menunggu event.
Process mengirim/memancarkan event atau propagasi kondisi ke handler.
```

---

# 5. Start Events

## 5.1 None Start Event

None start event adalah start paling dasar.

```text
Start
  ↓
Validate Request
```

Dipakai jika process dimulai secara eksplisit dari application/API.

Contoh implementation:

```text
POST /cases
  ↓
Create case record
  ↓
Start process instance: case-intake-process
```

Jersey resource:

```java
@Path("/cases")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CaseResource {

    private final CaseApplicationService caseApplicationService;

    @POST
    public Response submitCase(SubmitCaseRequest request) {
        SubmitCaseResult result = caseApplicationService.submitCase(request);

        return Response.accepted(Map.of(
                "caseId", result.caseId(),
                "processInstanceKey", result.processInstanceKey()
        )).build();
    }
}
```

Practical rule:

```text
None start cocok jika application service yang mengontrol kapan process dimulai.
```

Use case:

| Scenario | Start Type |
|---|---|
| User submit case dari UI | None start via REST |
| Internal service memulai workflow | None start via application command |
| Admin manually starts process | None start |
| Test automation starts process | None start |

---

## 5.2 Message Start Event

Message start event memulai process ketika message tertentu diterima.

```text
Message Start: CaseSubmitted
  ↓
Validate Case
```

Camunda menjelaskan message event digunakan untuk menunggu message yang tepat; message event cocok untuk hubungan satu sender ke satu recipient / process instance. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/message-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Cocok untuk:

```text
External system mengirim event bisnis.
Process belum ada.
Message tersebut membuat process instance baru.
```

Contoh:

```text
External Complaint Portal
  → Message: ComplaintSubmitted
  → Start Complaint Handling Process
```

Practical payload:

```json
{
  "complaintId": "CMP-2026-0001",
  "submittedBy": "USR-100",
  "complaintType": "REGULATORY",
  "submittedAt": "2026-07-16T09:00:00+07:00"
}
```

### Message start vs REST start

| Situation | Prefer |
|---|---|
| UI/API internal submit request | None start via REST command |
| External event creates new process | Message start |
| Need validation/idempotency before starting process | REST/application service first |
| Event broker is primary integration mechanism | Message start |
| Need synchronous API response with domain object | REST start |

Practical rule:

```text
Kalau message berasal dari luar dan process belum ada, message start cocok.
Kalau perlu domain validation/idempotency kompleks sebelum process start, gunakan REST/application service lalu start process eksplisit.
```

---

## 5.3 Timer Start Event

Timer start event memulai process berdasarkan jadwal.

```text
Timer Start: every day 02:00
  ↓
Run SLA Sweep
```

Camunda timer events mendukung date, duration, dan cycle untuk menentukan kapan timer terjadi. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/timer-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Use case:

| Scenario | Timer Start |
|---|---|
| Daily SLA check | Setiap hari jam tertentu |
| Monthly compliance report | Monthly cycle |
| Periodic reconciliation | Every N hours |
| Scheduled batch import | Cron/cycle-like schedule |

Contoh process:

```text
Timer Start: every 1 hour
  ↓
Find Overdue Cases
  ↓
Create Escalation Tasks
  ↓
End
```

Practical caution:

```text
Timer start harus jelas timezone, frequency, idempotency, dan overlap behavior.
```

Kalau job berjalan tiap jam, pastikan:

```text
1. Tidak memproses case yang sama berkali-kali tanpa kontrol.
2. Ada lock atau status marker.
3. Ada audit kapan sweep berjalan.
4. Ada metric jumlah item diproses.
```

PostgreSQL pattern:

```sql
CREATE TABLE sla_sweep_run (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    sweep_name VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    window_start TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    window_end TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    processed_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    CONSTRAINT uq_sla_sweep_window UNIQUE (sweep_name, window_start, window_end)
);
```

---

## 5.4 Signal Start Event

Signal start event bereaksi terhadap broadcast signal.

```text
Signal Start: EmergencyPolicyUpdate
  ↓
Re-evaluate Active Cases
```

Signal berbeda dari message.

```text
Message = targeted, biasanya 1 penerima / correlation-specific
Signal  = broadcast, bisa diterima banyak process
```

Use case:

```text
Global policy change
Emergency stop
System-wide recalculation trigger
Mass re-evaluation
```

Caution:

```text
Jangan pakai signal untuk callback yang harus masuk ke satu case tertentu.
Untuk callback targeted, gunakan message + correlation key.
```

---

## 5.5 Conditional Start Event

Conditional event dipakai untuk memicu behavior saat FEEL condition menjadi true berdasarkan variable/scope/perubahan variable. Camunda docs menjelaskan intermediate conditional catch event menunggu sampai condition menjadi true, dan Camunda 8.9 release notes menyebut dukungan BPMN conditional events untuk start/continue/interrupt process execution berdasarkan evaluated conditions. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/conditional-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Use case:

```text
Start handler ketika caseScore > threshold
Start subprocess ketika flag cancellationRequested = true
Start event subprocess ketika status menjadi OVERDUE
```

Practical caution:

```text
Conditional event powerful, tapi bisa sulit di-debug jika condition tersembunyi.
Untuk production, condition harus sederhana dan observable.
```

---

# 6. Intermediate Events

Intermediate event terjadi **di tengah process**.

```text
Start
  ↓
Task A
  ↓
Intermediate Event
  ↓
Task B
  ↓
End
```

Intermediate event terbagi menjadi:

```text
Intermediate Catch = menunggu sesuatu
Intermediate Throw = mengirim/melempar sesuatu
```

---

# 7. Intermediate Message Catch Event

## Makna

Process menunggu message tertentu.

```text
Send Registry Check Request
  ↓
Wait Message: RegistryCheckCompleted
  ↓
Continue Case Processing
```

Camunda concepts menjelaskan message subscription dibuka saat process instance menunggu message, misalnya saat masuk message catch event. Subscription menggunakan message name dan correlation key, yang dievaluasi saat message catch event aktif. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/messages/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Practical contract

```text
Message name:
  RegistryCheckCompleted

Correlation key:
  caseId

Payload:
  registryStatus
  registryReferenceNo
  checkedAt
```

## Jersey callback

```java
@Path("/registry/callbacks")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class RegistryCallbackResource {

    private final RegistryCallbackApplicationService service;

    @POST
    public Response receive(RegistryCallbackRequest request) {
        service.handle(request);

        return Response.accepted(Map.of(
                "caseId", request.caseId(),
                "accepted", true
        )).build();
    }
}
```

Service:

```java
public final class RegistryCallbackApplicationService {

    private final RegistryCallbackRepository callbackRepository;
    private final CamundaMessageService camundaMessageService;

    public void handle(RegistryCallbackRequest request) {
        callbackRepository.upsertCallback(
                request.caseId(),
                request.registryReferenceNo(),
                request.registryStatus(),
                request.rawPayload()
        );

        camundaMessageService.publishMessage(
                "RegistryCheckCompleted",
                request.caseId(),
                Map.of(
                        "registryStatus", request.registryStatus(),
                        "registryReferenceNo", request.registryReferenceNo()
                )
        );
    }
}
```

Practical invariant:

```text
Persist callback first.
Correlate/publish message second.
```

---

# 8. Intermediate Timer Catch Event

## Makna

Process menunggu waktu tertentu sebelum lanjut.

```text
Wait 3 days
  ↓
Send Reminder
```

Use case:

```text
Delay before reminder
Cooling-off period
SLA timeout
Wait until due date
Retry later by process design
```

Timer forms:

| Timer Style | Meaning | Example |
|---|---|---|
| Duration | Tunggu selama durasi tertentu | `PT24H`, `P3D` |
| Date | Tunggu sampai waktu tertentu | `2026-07-20T09:00:00+07:00` |
| Cycle | Berulang | reminder setiap hari |

Practical caution:

```text
Timer adalah business time, bukan Thread.sleep().
Jangan implement delay panjang di Java worker.
```

Buruk:

```java
Thread.sleep(Duration.ofDays(3).toMillis());
```

Lebih benar:

```text
Modelkan timer event di BPMN.
Worker selesai.
Engine mengatur wait state.
```

---

# 9. Intermediate Conditional Catch Event

## Makna

Process menunggu sampai condition menjadi true.

```text
Wait until caseReadyForReview = true
  ↓
Assign Review Task
```

Camunda docs menjelaskan intermediate conditional catch event menunggu sampai condition true, lalu process melanjutkan outgoing sequence flow. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/conditional-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Use case:

```text
Menunggu variable/state berubah
Menunggu flag external readiness
Menunggu prerequisite internal selesai
```

Caution:

```text
Condition harus observable.
Jangan buat condition rumit yang bergantung pada data tersembunyi.
```

Lebih aman untuk banyak kasus enterprise:

```text
External state berubah
  ↓
Callback/API menerima update
  ↓
Persist state
  ↓
Correlate message
```

Daripada:

```text
Process diam menunggu condition kompleks tanpa event eksplisit.
```

---

# 10. Intermediate Signal Catch / Throw Event

## Signal Catch

```text
Wait Signal: EmergencyStop
  ↓
Stop Current Processing
```

## Signal Throw

```text
Throw Signal: PolicyUpdated
```

Signal cocok untuk broadcast.

Use case:

```text
Emergency stop semua process tertentu
Policy update memicu banyak process
Mass reprocessing trigger
```

Caution:

```text
Signal bukan callback targeted.
Kalau event harus masuk ke satu caseId tertentu, gunakan message.
```

---

# 11. Link Events

Link event berguna untuk menyambung bagian diagram yang besar agar readable. Camunda docs menjelaskan link event pair setara dengan intermediate none events yang dihubungkan; ini berguna untuk diagram process yang komprehensif. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/link-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```text
... flow section A
  ↓
Link Throw: ContinueToReview

Link Catch: ContinueToReview
  ↓
Review Case
```

Use case:

```text
Diagram terlalu panjang
Ingin menghindari garis crossing
Ingin split visual section
```

Caution:

```text
Link event adalah alat readability.
Jangan gunakan untuk menyembunyikan flow penting.
```

---

# 12. Intermediate Message Throw Event

## Makna

Process mengirim message keluar di tengah flow.

```text
Case Registered
  ↓
Throw Message: CaseRegistered
  ↓
Continue Internal Flow
```

Dalam implementasi, ini sering menjadi:

```text
Publish Kafka event
Send command to another system
Call notification service
Write outbox event
```

Practical recommendation untuk enterprise:

```text
Gunakan outbox pattern untuk message/event penting.
```

PostgreSQL outbox:

```sql
CREATE TABLE integration_outbox (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    aggregate_type VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    event_type VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(256) NOT NULL,
    payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    published_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    CONSTRAINT uq_integration_outbox_idempotency UNIQUE (idempotency_key)
);
```

Worker flow:

```text
Service task persists domain state + outbox event
Outbox publisher sends event
Publisher marks event published
```

---

# 13. Intermediate Escalation / Error / Compensation Overview

Detail penuh untuk error, escalation, compensation akan masuk **Seri 8/12**, tapi mental model awalnya:

| Event | Meaning | Practical Use |
|---|---|---|
| Error | Kondisi error yang harus ditangkap handler tertentu | Business/technical failure that interrupts scope |
| Escalation | Kondisi bisnis naik level, tidak selalu fatal | SLA breach, supervisor attention |
| Compensation | Undo/reversal untuk action yang sudah selesai | Refund, cancel registration, release reservation |

Camunda docs menjelaskan escalation yang tidak tertangkap tidak membuat incident; process dapat terus berjalan tanpa escalation ditangkap. Ini berbeda dari error event behavior. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/escalation-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical consequence:

```text
Jangan treat error dan escalation sebagai sinonim.
Error = failure path yang harus ditangani.
Escalation = business signal untuk perhatian/handler tambahan.
```

---

# 14. End Events

End event menandai akhir execution path.

```text
Task A
  ↓
End
```

Tapi “end” tidak selalu berarti seluruh process selesai. Kalau ada parallel branch lain yang masih aktif, path ini selesai tetapi process instance bisa tetap berjalan.

Camunda none end event docs menjelaskan bahwa ketika none end event dimasuki, current execution path berakhir; process/subprocess selesai jika tidak ada execution path aktif lain. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/none-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 15. None End Event

## Makna

Path selesai normal tanpa semantic tambahan.

```text
Register Case
  ↓
End
```

Use case:

```text
Happy path complete
Rejection path complete
Notification branch complete
Audit branch complete
```

Practical caution:

```text
Beri nama end event jika ada banyak outcome.
```

Contoh:

```text
End: Case Registered
End: Case Rejected
End: Case Cancelled
End: Duplicate Case Merged
```

Lebih baik daripada:

```text
End
End
End
End
```

---

# 16. Terminate End Event

## Makna

Terminate end event menghentikan seluruh scope/process yang relevan, bukan hanya current path.

Use case:

```text
Cancel whole process immediately
Fraud detected, stop all active work
Duplicate case confirmed, terminate remaining branches
Emergency stop
```

Caution:

```text
Terminate end event sangat kuat.
Jangan dipakai hanya karena ingin “end cepat”.
```

Contoh:

```text
Parallel:
  ├── Long Manual Review
  ├── External Verification
  └── Duplicate Check → Duplicate Confirmed → Terminate End
```

Makna:

```text
Jika duplicate confirmed dan terminate end tercapai,
active review/external verification ikut dihentikan.
```

Production checklist:

```text
Apakah domain state sudah diupdate sebelum terminate?
Apakah active human task perlu dibatalkan?
Apakah external request perlu diberi cancellation signal?
Apakah audit mencatat alasan terminate?
```

---

# 17. Message End Event

## Makna

Process/path selesai sambil mengirim message.

```text
Approve Case
  ↓
Message End: CaseApproved
```

Implementation mapping:

```text
Publish event: CaseApproved
Notify external system
Send callback response asynchronously
```

Practical caution:

```text
Untuk message/event penting, jangan hanya rely pada visual end event.
Pastikan ada reliable publish mechanism, misalnya outbox.
```

Alternative production modeling:

```text
Service Task: Publish CaseApproved Event
  ↓
End: Case Approved
```

Ini sering lebih eksplisit untuk engineering team karena publish event punya retry/idempotency/observability.

---

# 18. Error End Event

## Makna

Path selesai dengan BPMN error.

```text
Validate Case
  ↓
Error End: InvalidCaseData
```

Biasanya ditangkap oleh:

```text
Error boundary event
Error event subprocess
Parent process handler
```

Use case:

```text
Fatal validation failure
Child process cannot complete business requirement
External verification failed in controlled way
```

Caution:

```text
Jangan gunakan error end untuk normal rejection.
```

Normal business rejection lebih baik:

```text
Gateway: approved?
  ├── yes → Register
  └── no  → Reject Case → End: Case Rejected
```

Error lebih cocok untuk kondisi exceptional yang perlu handler khusus.

---

# 19. Escalation End Event

## Makna

Path selesai sambil melempar escalation.

```text
Review Overdue
  ↓
Escalation End: SupervisorAttentionRequired
```

Use case:

```text
SLA breached
Manual review overdue
Risk threshold exceeded
Policy exception found
```

Practical distinction:

```text
Escalation bukan selalu failure.
Escalation adalah business attention signal.
```

---

# 20. Signal End Event

## Makna

Path selesai sambil broadcast signal.

```text
Policy Updated
  ↓
Signal End: PolicyUpdatePublished
```

Use case:

```text
Banyak process perlu tahu bahwa sesuatu terjadi.
```

Caution:

```text
Signal bisa berdampak luas.
Gunakan dengan governance ketat.
```

---

# 21. End Event Outcome Modeling

Untuk case management/regulatory flow, jangan hanya punya satu generic end.

Lebih baik modelkan outcome eksplisit:

```text
End: Case Registered
End: Case Rejected
End: Case Cancelled
End: Duplicate Merged
End: Timed Out
End: Escalated
```

Lalu mapping ke domain status:

| End Event | Domain Status |
|---|---|
| Case Registered | `REGISTERED` |
| Case Rejected | `REJECTED` |
| Case Cancelled | `CANCELLED` |
| Duplicate Merged | `MERGED_DUPLICATE` |
| Timed Out | `TIMED_OUT` |
| Escalated | `ESCALATED` atau process lanjut ke escalation process |

Practical rule:

```text
End event should match business outcome, not just diagram closure.
```

---

# 22. Practical Event Patterns

## Pattern 1 - REST starts process

```text
POST /cases
  ↓
Start Process: case-intake
  ↓
None Start Event
  ↓
Validate Case
```

Use when:

```text
Application service owns validation, idempotency, and start command.
```

---

## Pattern 2 - External event starts process

```text
External Portal
  ↓ Message: ComplaintSubmitted
Message Start Event
  ↓
Create Complaint Case
```

Use when:

```text
External message itself is the process trigger.
```

---

## Pattern 3 - Wait for callback

```text
Send Verification Request
  ↓
Message Catch: VerificationCompleted
  ↓
Continue
```

Use when:

```text
Process exists.
External response comes later.
Need correlation key.
```

---

## Pattern 4 - Callback vs timeout

```text
Send Registry Request
  ↓
Event-Based Gateway
  ├── Message: RegistryCompleted
  └── Timer: PT48H
```

Use when:

```text
First event wins.
Late event must be handled outside the main waiting state.
```

---

## Pattern 5 - Reminder while user task active

```text
User Task: Senior Officer Review
  boundary non-interrupting timer: every 24h
    ↓
  Send Reminder
```

Use when:

```text
Main task continues.
Reminder branch runs in parallel.
```

Detailed boundary events will be covered in **Seri 9/12**.

---

## Pattern 6 - Process-level cancellation

```text
Main flow active

Event Subprocess:
  Message Start: CancelCaseRequested
    ↓
  Mark Case Cancelled
    ↓
  Terminate / End
```

Use when:

```text
Cancellation can happen anytime while process is active.
```

---

# 23. Java/Jersey/Camunda Implementation Mapping

## Start process from REST

```java
public final class CaseApplicationService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final CamundaProcessService camundaProcessService;

    public SubmitCaseResult submitCase(SubmitCaseRequest request) {
        String caseId = caseRepository.createCaseIfAbsent(request);

        long processInstanceKey = camundaProcessService.startProcess(
                "case-intake-process",
                caseId,
                Map.of(
                        "caseId", caseId,
                        "caseType", request.caseType(),
                        "submitterId", request.submitterId()
                )
        );

        return new SubmitCaseResult(caseId, processInstanceKey);
    }
}
```

Design note:

```text
Create/persist domain aggregate first.
Then start BPMN process with business key/correlation key.
```

---

## Correlate message from callback

```java
public final class ExternalVerificationCallbackService {

    private final VerificationCallbackRepository callbackRepository;
    private final CamundaMessageService camundaMessageService;

    public void handle(VerificationCallbackRequest request) {
        callbackRepository.upsert(
                request.caseId(),
                request.externalReferenceNo(),
                request.status(),
                request.rawPayload()
        );

        camundaMessageService.publishMessage(
                "VerificationCompleted",
                request.caseId(),
                Map.of(
                        "verificationStatus", request.status(),
                        "externalReferenceNo", request.externalReferenceNo()
                )
        );
    }
}
```

Design note:

```text
Callback idempotency belongs in DB/application layer.
Message correlation belongs in orchestration layer.
```

---

## Persist outcome before end

Before reaching meaningful business end state, persist domain status.

```java
public final class RegisterCaseWorker {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;

    public void handle(JobClient client, ActivatedJob job) {
        String caseId = (String) job.getVariablesAsMap().get("caseId");

        caseRepository.markRegistered(caseId);

        client.newCompleteCommand(job.getKey())
                .variables(Map.of(
                        "caseStatus", "REGISTERED"
                ))
                .send()
                .join();
    }
}
```

Practical rule:

```text
End event should reflect already-persisted domain state.
```

---

# 24. PostgreSQL Support Tables

## A. Idempotent process start

```sql
CREATE TABLE case_submission_request (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    request_payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT uq_case_submission_idempotency UNIQUE (idempotency_key),
    CONSTRAINT uq_case_submission_case UNIQUE (case_id)
);
```

Use case:

```text
Client retries POST /cases.
System should not start duplicate process.
```

---

## B. Callback log

```sql
CREATE TABLE external_message_callback (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    message_name VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    correlation_key VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    external_reference_no VARCHAR(128),
    payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    correlation_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    correlated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    late_callback BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    error_message TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT uq_external_message_callback UNIQUE (
        message_name,
        correlation_key,
        external_reference_no
    )
);
```

Use case:

```text
Track received callbacks, duplicate callbacks, late callbacks, and failed correlation.
```

---

## C. Business outcome audit

```sql
CREATE TABLE case_process_outcome (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    process_id VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    process_instance_key VARCHAR(128),
    outcome_code VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    outcome_reason TEXT,
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT uq_case_process_outcome UNIQUE (case_id, process_id)
);
```

Use case:

```text
End event outcome should be queryable without depending only on process engine history.
```

---

# 25. Event Selection Decision Matrix

```text
Apakah event memulai process?
  ├─ Ya
  │   ├─ API/application command → None Start
  │   ├─ External targeted message → Message Start
  │   ├─ Schedule/time → Timer Start
  │   ├─ Broadcast signal → Signal Start
  │   └─ Variable condition → Conditional Start
  │
  └─ Tidak, event terjadi di tengah process
      │
      ├─ Process menunggu external targeted response → Message Catch
      ├─ Process menunggu waktu → Timer Catch
      ├─ Process menunggu broadcast → Signal Catch
      ├─ Process menunggu condition true → Conditional Catch
      ├─ Process mengirim targeted message → Message Throw
      ├─ Process broadcast event → Signal Throw
      ├─ Process menaikkan business condition → Escalation Throw
      └─ Process selesai → End Event
```

---

# 26. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Contoh | Dampak | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Treat event as task | “Message event validates request” | Semantik salah | Pakai service task sebelum/sesudah event |
| Message tanpa correlation key | Callback tidak tahu instance mana | Failed correlation | Gunakan `caseId`, `orderId`, `applicationId` |
| Timer di Java worker | `Thread.sleep(3 days)` | Worker stuck, resource waste | BPMN timer event |
| Generic end event semua path | Semua end bernama `End` | Outcome tidak jelas | Beri nama business outcome |
| Error end untuk rejection normal | Rejected case dianggap error | Failure semantics kacau | Normal rejection path + none end |
| Signal untuk targeted callback | Banyak process bisa menerima | Salah recipient | Message event |
| Message start tanpa idempotency | Duplicate process instance | Data/process duplicate | Idempotency key + unique constraint |
| Timer start tanpa overlap control | Batch run tumpang tindih | Duplicate processing | Sweep run table + lock |
| Late callback tidak ditangani | Callback setelah timeout crash/hilang | Audit/reconciliation issue | Store as late callback |
| End before DB commit | Process selesai tapi state belum persist | Inconsistent audit | Persist domain state before completing task |

---

# 27. Practical Review Checklist

Untuk setiap event di BPMN, cek:

```text
1. Event ini start, intermediate, atau end?
2. Event ini catching atau throwing?
3. Kalau message, apa message name-nya?
4. Kalau message, apa correlation key-nya?
5. Kalau timer, apa timezone/duration/date/cycle-nya?
6. Kalau signal, apakah broadcast memang diinginkan?
7. Kalau conditional, condition-nya sederhana dan observable?
8. Kalau end event, outcome bisnisnya apa?
9. Kalau terminate, apa saja active branch yang dihentikan?
10. Kalau callback datang duplicate, sistem aman?
11. Kalau callback datang terlambat, sistem aman?
12. Kalau process start di-retry, apakah duplicate dicegah?
13. Apakah domain DB menyimpan state penting?
14. Apakah process history cukup untuk audit atau perlu domain audit table?
15. Apakah event semantics terbaca dari nama event?
```

---

# 28. Naming Convention

## Start Event

```text
Case Submitted
Complaint Received
Daily SLA Sweep Started
Payment Notification Received
```

## Intermediate Catch Event

```text
Registry Check Completed
Payment Received
Additional Document Submitted
Review SLA Reached
Cancellation Requested
```

## Intermediate Throw Event

```text
Case Registered Published
Supervisor Escalation Raised
Policy Update Signaled
```

## End Event

```text
Case Registered
Case Rejected
Case Cancelled
Duplicate Case Merged
Verification Timed Out
```

Bad names:

```text
Start
Wait
Message
Timer
End
Done
```

Better names:

```text
Start: Case Submitted
Message: Registry Check Completed
Timer: Review SLA Reached
End: Case Rejected
```

---

# 29. Mini Exercise

Ambil satu workflow nyata, misalnya:

```text
Case Intake
External Verification
Manual Review
Case Closure
```

Isi tabel ini:

| Event | Type | Catch/Throw | Message/Timer/Signal? | Correlation Key | Business Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Case Submitted | Start | Catch | None/message | `caseId` | New case starts |
| Registry Response Received | Intermediate | Catch | Message | `caseId` | External verification completed |
| Review Deadline Reached | Intermediate | Catch | Timer | N/A | Review overdue |
| Case Registered | End | N/A | None/message | `caseId` | Successful completion |
| Case Cancelled | End | N/A | Terminate/none | `caseId` | Process cancelled |

Kalau kolom `Correlation Key` untuk message event kosong, event design belum siap production.

---

# 30. Ringkasan Seri 6

Inti seri ini:

```text
Start Event        = trigger process
Intermediate Event = wait / throw / react during process
End Event          = complete execution path with specific semantics
```

Rule paling penting:

```text
Event bukan work unit.
Work unit = task.
Event = sesuatu yang terjadi, ditunggu, dilempar, atau menandai akhir.
```

Decision rule praktis:

```text
API starts process             → None start via application service
External event starts process  → Message start
Schedule starts process        → Timer start
Process waits callback         → Message catch
Process waits time             → Timer catch
Process broadcasts             → Signal throw
Process completes normally     → None end
Process cancels all active work→ Terminate end
Process fails to parent scope  → Error end
Business attention needed      → Escalation
```

Untuk production Camunda/Jersey/MyBatis/PostgreSQL:

```text
Message event wajib punya message name + correlation key.
Timer event harus punya business time semantics.
Signal event harus dipakai hanya untuk broadcast.
End event harus merepresentasikan business outcome.
Domain state penting harus dipersist sebelum process dianggap selesai.
Callback/start event harus idempotent.
```
