# Seri 7/12 - Message, Timer, Signal, Conditional Events

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini mendalami 4 event yang paling sering menentukan kualitas workflow production:

```text
Message Event      = targeted communication / callback / correlation
Timer Event        = business time / SLA / delay / schedule
Signal Event       = broadcast event ke banyak listener
Conditional Event  = react ketika variable condition menjadi true
```

Dalam BPMN, event adalah sesuatu yang terjadi; process bisa **catch** event atau **throw** event. Camunda menjelaskan bahwa catching event membuat token lanjut ketika event yang sesuai diterima, sedangkan throwing event memancarkan/menghasilkan event tertentu. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Diagram Decomposition

```text
Message / Timer / Signal / Conditional Events
│
├── 1. Message Events
│   ├── Message Start Event
│   ├── Intermediate Message Catch Event
│   ├── Intermediate Message Throw Event
│   ├── Message End Event
│   ├── Message name
│   ├── Correlation key
│   ├── Message subscription
│   ├── TTL / buffering
│   ├── Duplicate message handling
│   └── Late message handling
│
├── 2. Timer Events
│   ├── Timer Start Event
│   ├── Intermediate Timer Catch Event
│   ├── Timer Boundary Event
│   ├── Timer Event Subprocess
│   ├── Date
│   ├── Duration
│   ├── Cycle
│   ├── SLA
│   ├── Reminder
│   └── Timeout
│
├── 3. Signal Events
│   ├── Signal Start Event
│   ├── Intermediate Signal Catch Event
│   ├── Intermediate Signal Throw Event
│   ├── Signal End Event
│   ├── Broadcast semantics
│   ├── Multiple receivers
│   └── Global operational trigger
│
├── 4. Conditional Events
│   ├── Conditional Start Event
│   ├── Intermediate Conditional Catch Event
│   ├── Boundary Conditional Event
│   ├── Event Subprocess Conditional Start
│   ├── FEEL condition
│   ├── Variable-driven trigger
│   └── Observable state change
│
└── 5. Production Design
    ├── Idempotency
    ├── Correlation contract
    ├── Timeout strategy
    ├── Late/duplicate event policy
    ├── Domain state persistence
    ├── Auditability
    └── Operational runbook
```

---

# 3. Quick Reference Table

| Event | Semantik | Cocok Untuk | Jangan Dipakai Untuk |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Message** | Komunikasi targeted ke process/instance tertentu | Callback, external response, payment received, document uploaded | Broadcast ke banyak process |
| **Timer** | Trigger berbasis waktu | SLA, reminder, timeout, scheduled start, delay | Menunggu external callback tanpa batas |
| **Signal** | Broadcast event ke semua listener yang cocok | Emergency stop, policy update, global refresh | Event yang harus masuk ke satu `caseId` tertentu |
| **Conditional** | Trigger saat FEEL condition atas variable menjadi true | State-driven continuation, variable-based reaction | External integration yang lebih jelas sebagai message |

---

# 4. Message Events

## 4.1 Mental Model

Message event adalah event untuk komunikasi **targeted**. Dalam Camunda, message dikorelasikan dengan kombinasi:

```text
messageName + correlationKey
```

Camunda menjelaskan bahwa message dipublish dengan defined name dan value dari `correlationKey`; misalnya process punya variable `orderId = "order-123"`, maka message harus dipublish dengan correlation key `"order-123"`. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/message-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```text
Process instance menunggu:
  messageName    = RegistryCheckCompleted
  correlationKey = CASE-2026-001

Callback datang:
  messageName    = RegistryCheckCompleted
  correlationKey = CASE-2026-001

Jika match:
  process lanjut
```

---

## 4.2 Message Event Types

| Type | Makna Praktis | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Message Start Event | Message membuat process instance baru | `ComplaintSubmitted` |
| Intermediate Message Catch | Process menunggu message | `RegistryCheckCompleted` |
| Intermediate Message Throw | Process mengirim message di tengah flow | `CaseRegisteredPublished` |
| Message End Event | Process selesai sambil mengirim message | `CaseRejectedPublished` |
| Message Boundary Event | Activity disela/ditanggapi oleh message | `CancellationRequested` saat user task aktif |

---

## 4.3 Message Subscription Behavior

Saat process instance masuk ke message catch event, engine membuka subscription. Jika message name dan correlation key cocok, message dikorelasikan ke process instance. Camunda juga mencatat bahwa jika tidak ada subscription yang sesuai, message dapat dibuang; subscription ditutup ketika element atau scope terkait ditinggalkan; dan setelah subscription dibuka, correlation key tidak ikut berubah walaupun variable referensinya berubah. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/messages/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical implication:

```text
1. Pastikan correlation key sudah tersedia sebelum process masuk message catch.
2. Jangan ubah variable correlation key setelah subscription aktif dan berharap subscription ikut berubah.
3. Callback bisa datang sebelum subscription aktif; butuh buffering/TTL atau application-level retry/reconciliation.
4. Callback bisa datang setelah scope sudah timeout/selesai; butuh late callback policy.
```

---

## 4.4 Message Naming Convention

Gunakan nama event bisnis, bukan nama API teknis.

Baik:

```text
CaseSubmitted
RegistryCheckCompleted
PaymentReceived
AdditionalDocumentSubmitted
CancellationRequested
ExternalVerificationFailed
```

Buruk:

```text
POST_CALLBACK
receive_message
message1
registry_api_response
event_done
```

Rule:

```text
Message name = business event yang terjadi
Correlation key = business identifier stabil
Payload = data minimal untuk melanjutkan process
```

---

## 4.5 Correlation Key Design

| Candidate Key | Kualitas | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| `caseId` | Sangat baik | Stabil, unik secara domain |
| `orderId` | Sangat baik | Cocok untuk order/payment |
| `applicationNo` | Baik | Cocok jika immutable |
| `paymentInstructionId` | Sangat baik | Cocok untuk payment callback |
| `email` | Buruk | Bisa berubah dan tidak selalu unik |
| `name` | Buruk | Tidak unik |
| internal DB surrogate id | Tergantung | Aman internal, tapi external party belum tentu tahu |
| process instance key | Tergantung | Terlalu engine-specific untuk external contract |

Practical invariant:

```text
External system harus tahu correlation key yang sama dengan process.
```

---

## 4.6 Pattern: Async External Registry Check

```text
Send Registry Check Request
  ↓
Event-Based Gateway
  ├── Message: RegistryCheckCompleted
  │       ↓
  │   Continue Case Processing
  │
  └── Timer: PT48H
          ↓
      Escalate Timeout
```

Message contract:

```text
messageName:
  RegistryCheckCompleted

correlationKey:
  caseId

payload:
  registryStatus
  registryReferenceNo
  checkedAt
```

Callback payload:

```json
{
  "caseId": "CASE-2026-001",
  "registryReferenceNo": "REG-2026-9981",
  "registryStatus": "MATCHED",
  "checkedAt": "2026-07-16T09:30:00+07:00"
}
```

---

## 4.7 Jersey Callback Implementation Pattern

```java
@Path("/external/registry/callbacks")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class RegistryCallbackResource {

    private final RegistryCallbackApplicationService service;

    @POST
    public Response receive(RegistryCallbackRequest request) {
        service.handle(request);

        return Response.accepted(Map.of(
                "caseId", request.caseId(),
                "accepted", true
        )).build();
    }
}
```

Application service:

```java
public final class RegistryCallbackApplicationService {

    private final RegistryCallbackRepository callbackRepository;
    private final CamundaMessageService camundaMessageService;

    public void handle(RegistryCallbackRequest request) {
        callbackRepository.upsertCallback(
                request.caseId(),
                request.registryReferenceNo(),
                request.registryStatus(),
                request.rawPayload()
        );

        camundaMessageService.publishMessage(
                "RegistryCheckCompleted",
                request.caseId(),
                Map.of(
                        "registryStatus", request.registryStatus(),
                        "registryReferenceNo", request.registryReferenceNo(),
                        "checkedAt", request.checkedAt().toString()
                )
        );
    }
}
```

Rule production:

```text
Persist callback dulu.
Baru publish/correlate BPMN message.
```

Kenapa? Kalau correlation gagal, data callback tetap aman untuk retry/reconciliation.

---

## 4.8 PostgreSQL Callback Table

```sql
CREATE TABLE external_callback_log (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    message_name VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    correlation_key VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    external_reference_no VARCHAR(128),
    callback_status VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    correlation_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    correlated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    late_callback BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    error_message TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT uq_external_callback UNIQUE (
        message_name,
        correlation_key,
        external_reference_no
    )
);
```

MyBatis upsert:

```xml
<insert id="upsertCallback">
  INSERT INTO external_callback_log (
      message_name,
      correlation_key,
      external_reference_no,
      callback_status,
      payload
  )
  VALUES (
      #{messageName},
      #{correlationKey},
      #{externalReferenceNo},
      #{callbackStatus},
      CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb)
  )
  ON CONFLICT (
      message_name,
      correlation_key,
      external_reference_no
  )
  DO UPDATE SET
      callback_status = EXCLUDED.callback_status,
      payload = EXCLUDED.payload,
      received_at = now()
</insert>
```

---

## 4.9 Duplicate / Late Message Policy

| Case | Risiko | Policy |
|---|---|---|
| Duplicate callback | Side effect ganda | Unique constraint + idempotent handler |
| Message datang sebelum subscription aktif | Message tidak correlate | TTL/buffering atau application-level retry |
| Message datang setelah timeout | Process sudah pindah path | Mark `late_callback = true`, manual reconciliation |
| Wrong correlation key | Tidak ada matching instance | Store as rejected/unmatched callback |
| Payload invalid | Process variable rusak | Validate before correlate |
| Multiple messages match | Ambiguity | External reference + idempotency key |

Camunda message concept docs menyebut jika subscription terbuka dan ada buffered message yang cocok, message dapat dikorelasikan; jika beberapa message cocok, first published message yang dikorelasikan seperti FIFO. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/messages/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. Timer Events

## 5.1 Mental Model

Timer event adalah event berbasis waktu. Timer dipakai untuk:

```text
delay
deadline
timeout
reminder
schedule
cycle
SLA
```

Camunda mendokumentasikan timer events sebagai event yang dapat dipakai untuk start process, intermediate event, atau boundary event; timer dapat didefinisikan sebagai date, duration, atau cycle. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/timer-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5.2 Timer Types

| Timer Type | Makna | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Date | Trigger pada waktu absolut | `2026-07-20T09:00:00+07:00` |
| Duration | Trigger setelah durasi tertentu | `PT24H`, `P3D` |
| Cycle | Trigger berulang | reminder berkala / scheduled process |

---

## 5.3 Timer Event Types

| Type | Use Case |
|---|---|
| Timer Start Event | Scheduled process, batch, daily SLA sweep |
| Intermediate Timer Catch | Delay sebelum lanjut |
| Timer Boundary Event | Timeout/reminder saat activity aktif |
| Timer Event Subprocess | Process-level scheduled reaction |

---

## 5.4 Pattern: SLA Timeout on User Task

```text
User Task: Senior Officer Review
  │
  ├── Boundary Timer interrupting: PT48H
  │       ↓
  │   Escalate to Supervisor
  │
  └── normal completion
          ↓
      Continue
```

Makna:

```text
Jika officer tidak complete review dalam 48 jam,
user task dibatalkan,
process masuk escalation path.
```

Cocok untuk:

```text
approval deadline
manual review timeout
document submission deadline
external verification timeout
```

---

## 5.5 Pattern: Reminder Without Cancelling Task

```text
User Task: Senior Officer Review
  │
  ├── Boundary Timer non-interrupting: every 24h
  │       ↓
  │   Send Reminder
  │
  └── user still can complete task
```

Makna:

```text
Main task tetap aktif.
Reminder berjalan sebagai side path.
```

Practical caution:

```text
Non-interrupting repeating timer bisa spam reminder.
Tambahkan max reminder count atau reminder policy.
```

---

## 5.6 Pattern: Callback vs Timeout

```text
Send Verification Request
  ↓
Event-Based Gateway
  ├── Message: VerificationCompleted
  └── Timer: PT24H
```

Ini pattern paling penting untuk integration.

Rule:

```text
External wait hampir selalu perlu timeout.
Kalau tidak, process instance bisa menggantung.
```

---

## 5.7 Timer Start for Batch/Sweep

```text
Timer Start: every 1 hour
  ↓
Find Overdue Cases
  ↓
Create Escalations
  ↓
End
```

DB support table:

```sql
CREATE TABLE scheduled_process_run (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    process_name VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    schedule_window_start TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    schedule_window_end TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    processed_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    error_message TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT uq_scheduled_process_window UNIQUE (
        process_name,
        schedule_window_start,
        schedule_window_end
    )
);
```

Practical concern:

```text
Timer start harus idempotent.
Kalau process redeploy/retry/cluster event terjadi, batch tidak boleh memproses window yang sama dua kali tanpa kontrol.
```

Camunda timer docs juga mencatat bahwa ketika process dideploy, timer dijadwalkan untuk setiap timer start event, dan scheduled timers dari versi sebelumnya dapat dipengaruhi oleh deployment versi baru berdasarkan BPMN process id. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/timer-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5.8 Timer Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Contoh | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|---|
| Delay di Java worker | `Thread.sleep(24h)` | Worker/thread/resource terkunci | BPMN timer |
| External wait tanpa timeout | Menunggu callback selamanya | Hanging instance | Event-based gateway + timer |
| Repeating reminder tanpa limit | Reminder tiap jam selamanya | Spam/operational noise | Reminder count + stop condition |
| Timer start tanpa idempotency | Batch double-run | Duplicate processing | Unique window table |
| Timezone tidak jelas | SLA salah | Simpan timezone/absolute deadline |
| Timer terlalu granular | Ribuan timer kecil tanpa alasan | Load/complexity | Consolidate dengan sweep jika cocok |
| Timeout branch tidak update domain state | BPMN lanjut tapi DB tidak tahu timeout | Inconsistent reporting | Persist `TIMED_OUT` status |

---

# 6. Signal Events

## 6.1 Mental Model

Signal event adalah broadcast. Camunda menjelaskan bahwa signal events mereferensikan signal, dan broadcasting signal akan trigger semua signal events yang matching dengan nama signal tersebut. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/signal-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```text
Signal = broadcast
Message = targeted
```

Contoh:

```text
Broadcast Signal: PolicyUpdated
  → semua process yang menunggu PolicyUpdated dapat bereaksi
```

---

## 6.2 Signal Event Types

| Type | Use Case |
|---|---|
| Signal Start Event | Signal memulai process baru |
| Intermediate Signal Catch | Process aktif menunggu broadcast |
| Intermediate Signal Throw | Process mem-broadcast signal |
| Signal End Event | Process selesai sambil broadcast signal |
| Signal Boundary Event | Activity bereaksi terhadap broadcast signal |

---

## 6.3 Message vs Signal

| Concern | Message | Signal |
|---|---|---|
| Target | Satu process/instance tertentu | Semua listener yang cocok |
| Correlation key | Ya | Biasanya tidak |
| Use case | Payment callback untuk `orderId` tertentu | Global policy update |
| Risiko | Failed correlation | Too many receivers |
| Enterprise analogy | Direct command/callback | Broadcast announcement |

Rule praktis:

```text
Kalau event punya caseId/orderId/applicationId target tertentu → message.
Kalau event dimaksudkan untuk banyak process → signal.
```

---

## 6.4 Good Signal Use Cases

| Signal | Makna |
|---|---|
| `PolicyUpdated` | Banyak active case perlu re-evaluate policy |
| `EmergencyStopRequested` | Banyak process perlu berhenti/masuk safe path |
| `SystemMaintenanceStarted` | Process tertentu pause/avoid external calls |
| `MassRecalculationRequested` | Banyak process trigger recalculation |
| `RegulatoryRuleChanged` | Banyak workflow perlu update/review |

---

## 6.5 Bad Signal Use Cases

| Bad Use | Kenapa Salah | Gunakan |
|---|---|---|
| Payment callback untuk satu order | Harus targeted | Message |
| Registry result untuk satu case | Harus correlation-specific | Message |
| Approval result untuk satu task | Harus instance-specific | User task/message |
| Document uploaded untuk satu case | Harus targeted | Message |
| Retry satu external request | Bukan broadcast | Service task/job retry |

---

## 6.6 Pattern: Emergency Stop

```text
Main Case Process
  ↓
Processing...

Boundary/Event Subprocess:
  Signal Catch: EmergencyStopRequested
    ↓
  Mark Case Processing Suspended
    ↓
  Notify Operations
```

Implementation consideration:

```text
Signal impact luas.
Harus ada governance: siapa boleh broadcast, kapan, audit apa, rollback bagaimana.
```

DB audit:

```sql
CREATE TABLE operational_signal_audit (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    signal_name VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    broadcast_by VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    reason TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload JSONB,
    broadcast_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

---

## 6.7 Signal Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Dampak | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|
| Signal untuk targeted callback | Banyak process bisa trigger salah | Message + correlation key |
| Nama signal terlalu generik | Receiver tidak jelas | Gunakan event name spesifik |
| Broadcast tanpa authorization | Risiko operasional besar | Guard API + audit |
| Tidak ada impact analysis | Banyak process berubah state | Simulasi/listener inventory |
| Signal payload besar | Security/performance issue | Payload minimal, detail di DB |
| Signal untuk business event biasa | Coupling tidak terlihat | Message/event bus dengan contract eksplisit |

---

# 7. Conditional Events

## 7.1 Mental Model

Conditional event trigger berdasarkan **perubahan/evaluasi variable**, bukan external message atau broadcast. Camunda menjelaskan conditional events sebagai cara process bereaksi otomatis ketika variable memenuhi FEEL condition; conditional event mirip message/signal, tetapi dipicu oleh variable changes, bukan external correlation/broadcast. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/conditionals/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```text
Intermediate Conditional Catch:
  = riskScore >= 90
```

Makna:

```text
Process menunggu sampai condition bernilai true.
```

---

## 7.2 Conditional Event Types

| Type | Use Case |
|---|---|
| Conditional Start Event | Process mulai saat condition terpenuhi |
| Intermediate Conditional Catch | Process menunggu variable condition |
| Boundary Conditional Event | Activity terganggu/ditanggapi ketika condition true |
| Conditional Event Subprocess | Scope bereaksi saat condition true |

Camunda conditional event docs menjelaskan intermediate conditional catch event menunggu sampai condition menjadi true, lalu process lanjut. Expression memakai FEEL dan harus menghasilkan boolean `true` atau `false`. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/conditional-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 7.3 FEEL Condition Examples

```text
= riskScore >= 90
= caseStatus = "READY_FOR_REVIEW"
= cancellationRequested = true
= documentStatus = "COMPLETE"
= slaBreached = true
= priority = "URGENT" and assignedOfficer = null
```

Rule:

```text
Condition harus sederhana, observable, dan berbasis variable yang jelas.
```

---

## 7.4 Conditional vs Message

| Scenario | Prefer |
|---|---|
| External system memberi callback | Message |
| Internal variable berubah dan process harus lanjut | Conditional |
| User upload dokumen via API | Biasanya message, atau service sets variable then conditional |
| State di DB berubah di luar engine | Message/signal lebih eksplisit |
| Process-local derived variable berubah | Conditional |

Practical warning:

```text
Conditional event mudah terlihat elegan, tapi bisa membuat trigger tersembunyi.
Untuk integration antar sistem, message biasanya lebih explicit dan auditable.
```

---

## 7.5 Pattern: Wait Until Case Ready

```text
Collect Documents
  ↓
Conditional Catch: = documentStatus = "COMPLETE"
  ↓
Assign Reviewer
```

Better production pattern untuk external document upload:

```text
User uploads document via API
  ↓
Persist document
  ↓
Update document checklist
  ↓
Publish/correlate message: DocumentChecklistCompleted
  ↓
Process continues
```

Kenapa?

```text
API event eksplisit.
Audit upload jelas.
Correlation jelas.
Tidak bergantung pada variable change yang sulit dilacak.
```

---

## 7.6 Pattern: Cancellation Requested

```text
Main flow active

Event Subprocess:
  Conditional Start:
    = cancellationRequested = true
      ↓
    Validate Cancellation
      ↓
    Cancel Case
```

Cocok jika `cancellationRequested` memang variable process yang di-set oleh task/API internal.

Namun kalau cancellation datang dari external API, pattern yang sering lebih jelas:

```text
POST /cases/{caseId}/cancellations
  ↓
Persist cancellation request
  ↓
Correlate Message: CancellationRequested
```

---

## 7.7 Conditional Event Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Dampak | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Condition terlalu kompleks | Sulit test/debug | Hitung di service/DMN, expose boolean sederhana |
| Menunggu DB state tanpa variable update | Event tidak trigger | Gunakan message atau scheduled sweep |
| Conditional untuk external callback | Correlation kabur | Message event |
| Variable tidak terdokumentasi | Process sulit dipahami | Variable contract |
| Condition punya side effect | Salah konsep | Condition hanya evaluasi boolean |
| Multiple conditional event overlap | Trigger tidak terduga | Buat decision model eksplisit |

---

# 8. Combined Production Patterns

## Pattern A - External Request + Message + Timer

```text
Send External Verification Request
  ↓
Event-Based Gateway
  ├── Message: VerificationCompleted
  │       ↓
  │   Continue
  │
  └── Timer: PT24H
          ↓
      Escalate Timeout
```

Use case:

```text
Best pattern untuk async external system.
```

Implementation checklist:

```text
- outbound request persisted
- callback persisted
- messageName defined
- correlationKey stable
- timeout branch updates domain state
- late callback policy exists
```

---

## Pattern B - User Task + Reminder + Escalation

```text
User Task: Review Case
  │
  ├── Non-interrupting Timer: every 24h
  │       ↓
  │   Send Reminder
  │
  └── Interrupting Timer: PT72H
          ↓
      Escalate to Supervisor
```

Use case:

```text
Human workflow dengan SLA.
```

Implementation checklist:

```text
- due date visible to UI
- reminder count controlled
- escalation task owner clear
- original task cancellation behavior clear
```

---

## Pattern C - Broadcast Operational Signal

```text
Signal Throw: EmergencyStopRequested
  ↓
Many active process instances catch signal
  ↓
Suspend or route to safe path
```

Use case:

```text
Operational emergency, policy update, regulatory hold.
```

Implementation checklist:

```text
- who can broadcast
- audit signal
- listener inventory
- rollback/resume plan
```

---

## Pattern D - Conditional Internal Readiness

```text
Parallel:
  ├── Collect Documents
  ├── Complete Identity Check
  └── Complete Risk Features

Conditional:
  = documentsComplete and identityCheckComplete and riskFeaturesComplete

Then:
  Assign Review
```

Use case:

```text
Process-local readiness flag.
```

Practical improvement:

```text
Sebaiknya readiness dihitung oleh service/DMN menjadi variable tunggal:
  reviewReady = true
```

---

# 9. Java/Jersey Service Abstraction

Buat boundary service supaya resource/controller tidak tahu detail Camunda terlalu banyak.

```java
public interface WorkflowMessageService {

    void publishMessage(
            String messageName,
            String correlationKey,
            Map<String, Object> variables
    );

    void broadcastSignal(
            String signalName,
            Map<String, Object> variables
    );
}
```

Callback service:

```java
public final class PaymentCallbackApplicationService {

    private final PaymentCallbackRepository callbackRepository;
    private final WorkflowMessageService workflowMessageService;

    public void handle(PaymentCallbackRequest request) {
        callbackRepository.upsertCallback(
                "PaymentReceived",
                request.paymentInstructionId(),
                request.status(),
                request.rawPayload()
        );

        if (!request.isValidSignature()) {
            callbackRepository.markRejected(
                    "PaymentReceived",
                    request.paymentInstructionId(),
                    "INVALID_SIGNATURE"
            );
            return;
        }

        workflowMessageService.publishMessage(
                "PaymentReceived",
                request.paymentInstructionId(),
                Map.of(
                        "paymentStatus", request.status(),
                        "paymentReferenceNo", request.paymentReferenceNo(),
                        "paidAt", request.paidAt().toString()
                )
        );
    }
}
```

Design notes:

```text
1. Validate signature/authenticity before correlation.
2. Store callback even if invalid.
3. Publish minimal variables.
4. Keep authoritative payment record in DB.
```

---

# 10. Event Contract Template

Gunakan template ini untuk setiap message/signal/conditional/timer penting.

```text
Event name:
Event type:
Catch or throw:
Scope:
Business meaning:
Trigger source:
Correlation key:
Payload:
Expected variable changes:
Timeout:
Duplicate policy:
Late event policy:
Security/authentication:
Audit table:
Owner:
Runbook:
```

Contoh:

```text
Event name:
  RegistryCheckCompleted

Event type:
  Message intermediate catch

Catch or throw:
  Catch

Scope:
  Case Intake Process / External Verification phase

Business meaning:
  External registry has completed checking case subject

Trigger source:
  External Registry callback API

Correlation key:
  caseId

Payload:
  registryStatus
  registryReferenceNo
  checkedAt

Timeout:
  PT48H via event-based gateway

Duplicate policy:
  Unique callback by messageName + caseId + registryReferenceNo

Late event policy:
  Store as late_callback=true; manual reconciliation if case already escalated

Security:
  Verify provider signature / mTLS / API key

Audit table:
  external_callback_log

Owner:
  Case Integration Team

Runbook:
  Retry correlation if callback_status=PENDING and process still waiting
```

---

# 11. Production Checklist

## Message Event Checklist

```text
1. Message name jelas?
2. Correlation key stabil?
3. Correlation key tersedia sebelum subscription aktif?
4. Payload minimal dan tervalidasi?
5. Callback idempotent?
6. Duplicate callback aman?
7. Late callback aman?
8. Wrong correlation key dicatat?
9. Signature/authentication dicek?
10. Ada timeout jika menunggu external system?
```

## Timer Event Checklist

```text
1. Timer type date/duration/cycle jelas?
2. Timezone jelas?
3. Timer merepresentasikan business time?
4. Timeout branch update domain state?
5. Reminder punya max count?
6. Timer start idempotent?
7. Batch/sweep punya window lock?
8. Long delay tidak ditaruh di worker?
```

## Signal Event Checklist

```text
1. Broadcast memang diinginkan?
2. Semua listener diketahui?
3. Nama signal spesifik?
4. Ada authorization untuk broadcast?
5. Ada audit?
6. Ada rollback/resume plan?
7. Signal tidak menggantikan targeted message?
```

## Conditional Event Checklist

```text
1. Condition sederhana?
2. FEEL expression boolean?
3. Variable yang dibaca terdokumentasi?
4. Variable change benar-benar terjadi di process scope?
5. Condition tidak menggantikan external message?
6. Tidak ada side effect dalam condition?
7. Ada test untuk true/false transition?
```

---

# 12. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Gejala | Risiko | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Message tanpa correlation key | Callback tidak match instance | Lost callback | Definisikan business key |
| Correlation pakai email/name | Tidak stabil | Salah instance | Pakai `caseId/orderId` |
| Menunggu callback tanpa timer | Instance menggantung | Operational debt | Event-based gateway + timer |
| Timer di worker | Thread sleep panjang | Resource leak | BPMN timer |
| Signal untuk satu case | Banyak process bisa trigger | Data corruption | Message |
| Conditional untuk integration eksternal | Trigger tersembunyi | Sulit audit | Message/callback |
| Callback langsung update process tanpa DB log | Data hilang saat correlate gagal | No recovery | Persist callback first |
| Late callback dianggap error fatal | Reconciliation buruk | Incident noise | Late callback policy |
| Reminder repeat tak terbatas | Spam | User fatigue | Max count / escalation |
| Broadcast tanpa audit | Sulit investigasi | Operational risk | Signal audit table |

---

# 13. Ringkasan Seri 7

Inti praktis:

```text
Message      = targeted event + correlation key
Timer        = time-based trigger + SLA/timeout/reminder
Signal       = broadcast event to many listeners
Conditional  = variable-driven trigger via FEEL condition
```

Decision rule:

```text
External callback untuk satu case/order        → Message
Menunggu waktu/deadline/SLA                    → Timer
Memberi tahu banyak process sekaligus          → Signal
React terhadap variable process menjadi true   → Conditional
```

Production rule paling penting:

```text
Event harus punya contract.
Contract minimal = name, trigger source, correlation/scope, payload, timeout, idempotency, late/duplicate policy, audit.
```

Untuk Java/Jersey/MyBatis/PostgreSQL/Camunda:

```text
REST callback endpoint
  → validate/authenticate
  → persist callback idempotently
  → publish/correlate message
  → update correlation status
  → handle duplicate/late/unmatched callback safely
```

---

# Status

**Seri 7/12 selesai - Message, Timer, Signal, Conditional Events.**

Rekomendasi berikutnya: **Seri 8/12 - Error, Escalation, Compensation, Cancel Events**, fokus pada exception modeling, business escalation, recovery, rollback/compensation, cancellation semantics, dan pola production untuk workflow yang defensible secara audit.

---

**Sources:**

- [Overview](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/16/2026, 9:53:30 AM

Lanjut ke **Seri 8/12 — Error, Escalation, Compensation, Cancel Events**. Fokusnya: membedakan technical failure vs business exception, escalation vs error, dan bagaimana mendesain recovery/rollback yang realistis di workflow enterprise.

# Seri 8/12 - Error, Escalation, Compensation, Cancel Events

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas event yang dipakai untuk **exception handling, escalation, cancellation, dan recovery**:

```text
Error Event
Escalation Event
Compensation Event
Cancel / Terminate Thinking
```

Fokus praktisnya adalah membedakan:

```text
expected business result
vs
business exception
vs
technical failure
vs
business escalation
vs
logical rollback / compensation
vs
termination / cancellation
```

Dalam Camunda 8.9, error event dipakai agar process model bisa bereaksi terhadap deviasi dari happy path; error memiliki `errorCode`, dan thrown error bisa ditangkap oleh error boundary event atau error event subprocess. Jika error tidak tertangkap, Camunda membuat incident untuk menunjukkan failure. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/error-events/))

---

## 2. Diagram Decomposition

```text
BPMN Exception & Recovery Events
│
├── 1. Error Events
│   ├── Error End Event
│   ├── Error Boundary Event
│   ├── Error Event Subprocess
│   ├── ThrowError from worker
│   ├── errorCode
│   ├── Catch specific error
│   ├── Catch-all error
│   ├── Propagation to parent scope
│   └── Incident if unhandled
│
├── 2. Escalation Events
│   ├── Intermediate Escalation Throw
│   ├── Escalation End Event
│   ├── Escalation Boundary Event
│   ├── Escalation Event Subprocess
│   ├── escalationCode
│   ├── Non-critical signal to higher scope
│   ├── Interrupting catch
│   └── Non-interrupting catch
│
├── 3. Compensation Events
│   ├── Compensation Boundary Event
│   ├── Compensation Handler
│   ├── Intermediate Compensation Throw
│   ├── Compensation End Event
│   ├── Completed activity only
│   ├── Scope-based compensation
│   ├── Specific activity compensation
│   └── Saga-like reversal
│
├── 4. Cancel / Terminate Thinking
│   ├── Terminate End Event
│   ├── Process-level termination
│   ├── Subprocess-level termination
│   ├── External cancellation message
│   ├── Event subprocess cancellation
│   └── Domain state cancellation
│
└── 5. Production Recovery Design
    ├── Retry vs BPMN error
    ├── Incident vs business route
    ├── Compensation table
    ├── Idempotent reversal
    ├── Audit trail
    ├── Manual recovery task
    └── Runbook
```

---

## 3. Quick Reference Table

| Event / Pattern | Makna Praktis | Kapan Dipakai | Jangan Dipakai Untuk |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Error Event** | Exception yang mengubah alur proses | Tidak bisa mencapai expected result; butuh handler khusus | Normal decision/result yang bisa dimodelkan gateway |
| **Escalation Event** | Sinyal bisnis ke scope lebih tinggi; non-critical by default | SLA breach, supervisor attention, policy exception | Technical failure yang harus retry/incident |
| **Compensation Event** | Undo/reversal untuk aktivitas yang sudah sukses | Refund, release reservation, cancel registration | Distributed ACID rollback |
| **Terminate End Event** | Menghentikan semua element instance dalam scope | Stop parallel work yang tidak lagi diperlukan | Normal end biasa |
| **Cancellation Message** | External request untuk membatalkan process/case | User/customer/system meminta cancel | Menghapus audit/history |
| **Incident** | Runtime failure yang perlu operasional handling | Technical failure setelah retry habis | Business rejection normal |

---

## 4. Mental Model Utama

### Jangan semua masalah dijadikan BPMN error

Camunda best practice membedakan dua pendekatan: retry/incident untuk masalah teknis yang tidak ingin memenuhi diagram, dan explicit branch path untuk problem yang memang perlu reaksi bisnis. Dokumentasi Camunda juga menyarankan expected problematic result lebih cocok ditulis sebagai variable lalu diarahkan oleh XOR gateway, sedangkan exceptional error yang menghalangi expected result lebih cocok dimodelkan sebagai boundary error event. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/best-practices/development/dealing-with-problems-and-exceptions/))

Contoh expected result:

```text
Task: Check Creditworthiness
Output:
  creditWorthy = false
```

Ini bukan error. Ini hasil bisnis yang valid.

Model:

```text
Check Creditworthiness
  ↓
Gateway: creditWorthy?
  ├── yes → Continue
  └── no  → Decline Application
```

Contoh exceptional error:

```text
Task: Check Creditworthiness
Problem:
  Applicant identity cannot be resolved,
  so creditworthiness cannot be checked at all.
```

Model:

```text
Check Creditworthiness
  ├── success → Gateway: creditWorthy?
  └── Error Boundary: IdentityNotResolved
          ↓
      Manual Identity Resolution
```

---

## 5. Error Events

## 5.1 Makna

Error event dipakai saat proses tidak bisa mencapai expected outcome dari sebuah task/subprocess, dan proses perlu pindah ke path exception.

Camunda 8 error events menggunakan `errorCode` untuk mencocokkan thrown error dengan catch event. Error dapat dilempar dengan error end event, atau dari worker menggunakan ThrowError command saat sedang memproses job. Error yang dilempar akan dicari handler-nya dari scope saat error terjadi, lalu bisa propagate ke parent scope; error boundary event dan error event subprocess harus interrupting. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/error-events/))

---

## 5.2 Error Event Types

| Type | Makna | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Error End Event | Path berakhir dengan BPMN error | Child process gagal dengan `RiskAssessmentUnavailable` |
| Error Boundary Event | Menangkap error dari activity/subprocess | Service task gagal business exception |
| Error Event Subprocess | Handler error dalam scope process/subprocess | Global handler untuk error di fase tertentu |
| Worker ThrowError | Worker memberi tahu process bahwa business error terjadi | `InvalidApplicantIdentity`, `CaseAlreadyClosed` |
| Catch-all Error | Menangkap semua error dalam scope | Fallback manual recovery |

---

## 5.3 Error vs Failed Job vs Incident

Ini distinction paling penting untuk implementasi.

| Situation | Pilihan | Kenapa |
|---|---|---|
| DB connection timeout | Failed job + retry | Technical transient |
| External API 503 | Failed job + retry / incident | Tidak perlu BPMN branch kalau hanya gangguan teknis |
| External API returns “identity not found” | BPMN error atau normal result, tergantung business meaning | Bisa mengubah alur bisnis |
| Validation menghasilkan invalid | Normal output + gateway | Expected business result |
| Task tidak bisa menghasilkan result karena data kontradiktif | BPMN error | Exceptional business condition |
| Retry habis | Incident | Butuh operational handling |
| Human harus resolve exception | Error boundary → user task | Business recovery path |

Rule praktis:

```text
Expected result        → variable + gateway
Recoverable technical  → fail job + retry
Unrecoverable technical→ incident/manual runbook
Business exception     → BPMN error
Business attention     → escalation
Undo completed action  → compensation
```

---

## 5.4 Java Worker: Fail Job vs Throw BPMN Error

### A. Technical transient failure → fail job

```java
private void handle(JobClient client, ActivatedJob job) {
    try {
        riskClient.calculateRisk(...);

        client.newCompleteCommand(job.getKey())
                .variables(Map.of("riskStatus", "CALCULATED"))
                .send()
                .join();

    } catch (ExternalServiceUnavailableException ex) {
        client.newFailCommand(job.getKey())
                .retries(Math.max(job.getRetries() - 1, 0))
                .errorMessage("Risk service unavailable: " + ex.getMessage())
                .send()
                .join();
    }
}
```

Makna:

```text
Masalah teknis.
Coba retry.
Kalau retry habis, incident.
```

---

### B. Business exception → throw BPMN error

Camunda best-practice docs menunjukkan worker dapat melempar BPMN error melalui `newThrowErrorCommand`, misalnya dengan `errorCode("GOOD_UNAVAILABLE")`; konsekuensinya setara dengan explicit BPMN error end event. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/best-practices/development/dealing-with-problems-and-exceptions/))

```java
private void handle(JobClient client, ActivatedJob job) {
    String caseId = (String) job.getVariablesAsMap().get("caseId");

    try {
        IdentityCheckResult result = identityService.check(caseId);

        if (result.status() == IdentityStatus.NOT_RESOLVABLE) {
            client.newThrowErrorCommand(job)
                    .errorCode("IDENTITY_NOT_RESOLVABLE")
                    .errorMessage("Applicant identity cannot be resolved")
                    .variables(Map.of(
                            "identityCheckStatus", "NOT_RESOLVABLE",
                            "identityFailureReason", result.reasonCode()
                    ))
                    .send()
                    .join();
            return;
        }

        client.newCompleteCommand(job.getKey())
                .variables(Map.of(
                        "identityCheckStatus", "RESOLVED",
                        "identityReferenceNo", result.referenceNo()
                ))
                .send()
                .join();

    } catch (ExternalServiceUnavailableException ex) {
        client.newFailCommand(job.getKey())
                .retries(Math.max(job.getRetries() - 1, 0))
                .errorMessage(ex.getMessage())
                .send()
                .join();
    }
}
```

BPMN:

```text
Check Applicant Identity
  ├── success → Continue
  └── Error Boundary: IDENTITY_NOT_RESOLVABLE
          ↓
      Manual Identity Resolution
```

---

## 5.5 Error Boundary Event Pattern

```text
Service Task: Check Applicant Identity
  │
  ├── Error Boundary: IDENTITY_NOT_RESOLVABLE
  │       ↓
  │   User Task: Resolve Identity Manually
  │       ↓
  │   Gateway: resolved?
  │       ├── yes → Continue
  │       └── no  → Reject Case
  │
  └── normal success
          ↓
      Continue Processing
```

Use case:

```text
External/business validation tidak bisa menghasilkan expected result.
Butuh path recovery yang eksplisit.
```

---

## 5.6 Error Event Subprocess Pattern

```text
Subprocess: External Verification
  ├── Send Verification Request
  ├── Wait Verification Response
  └── Evaluate Verification Result

Event Subprocess:
  Error Start: VERIFICATION_FAILED
    ↓
  Mark Verification Failed
    ↓
  Create Manual Recovery Task
```

Cocok jika:

```text
Error bisa terjadi di banyak titik dalam satu subprocess.
Handler-nya sama.
Kamu tidak ingin menempel boundary error di setiap task.
```

---

## 5.7 Error Propagation ke Parent Process

Kalau error dilempar dari called process, parent process bisa menangkap error pada call activity boundary. Camunda docs menyebut error yang dilempar akan dicari catch event-nya pada scope hierarchy, dan jika process instance dibuat via call activity, error juga bisa ditangkap oleh parent process yang memanggil. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/error-events/))

```text
Parent: Case Intake
  ↓
Call Activity: Risk Assessment Process
  │
  ├── Error Boundary: RISK_ASSESSMENT_FAILED
  │       ↓
  │   Manual Risk Review
  │
  └── success
          ↓
      Continue
```

Child process:

```text
Risk Assessment Process
  ↓
Load Risk Features
  ↓
Evaluate Rule
  ↓
Error End: RISK_ASSESSMENT_FAILED
```

---

## 6. Escalation Events

## 6.1 Makna

Escalation event adalah sinyal bisnis ke scope yang lebih tinggi. Berbeda dari error, escalation bersifat **non-critical by default**: proses pada lokasi throwing tetap bisa lanjut, dan jika tidak ada catch event yang cocok, tidak ada incident yang dibuat. Camunda 8.9 escalation events menggunakan `escalationCode` untuk mencocokkan throw dengan catch; escalation dapat dilempar oleh intermediate escalation throw event atau escalation end event, dan dapat ditangkap oleh boundary event atau event subprocess. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/escalation-events/))

Mental model:

```text
Error       = process cannot continue normally
Escalation  = process can continue, but higher scope/role must be notified/react
```

---

## 6.2 Escalation Event Types

| Type | Makna | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Intermediate Escalation Throw | Melempar escalation di tengah flow | `ReviewSlaBreached` |
| Escalation End Event | Path selesai sambil escalate | `HighRiskEscalated` |
| Escalation Boundary Event | Menangkap escalation dari activity/subprocess | Supervisor attention |
| Escalation Event Subprocess | Handler escalation dalam scope | SLA breach handler |
| Catch-all Escalation | Menangkap semua escalation dalam scope | Generic escalation handling |

---

## 6.3 Escalation Pattern: SLA Breach Non-Interrupting

```text
User Task: Senior Officer Review
  │
  ├── Non-interrupting Timer Boundary: PT24H
  │       ↓
  │   Throw Escalation: REVIEW_SLA_WARNING
  │
  └── User completes review
          ↓
      Continue
```

Parent/subprocess handler:

```text
Escalation Boundary/Event Subprocess: REVIEW_SLA_WARNING
  ↓
Notify Supervisor
  ↓
Create Escalation Audit
```

Makna:

```text
Review task tetap aktif.
Supervisor diberi tahu.
Process tidak otomatis gagal.
```

---

## 6.4 Escalation Pattern: Policy Exception

```text
Evaluate Case Policy
  ↓
Gateway: policy exception found?
  ├── yes → Throw Escalation: POLICY_EXCEPTION_FOUND
  │             ↓
  │         Continue with Controlled Review
  └── no  → Continue Normal Path
```

Handler:

```text
Escalation Catch: POLICY_EXCEPTION_FOUND
  ↓
Create Supervisor Review Task
```

Escalation cocok untuk:

```text
High-risk case needs attention
SLA warning
Policy exception
Manual oversight required
Potential breach but process can continue
```

---

## 6.5 Error vs Escalation

| Concern | Error | Escalation |
|---|---|---|
| Severity | Critical for normal path | Non-critical by default |
| Jika tidak tertangkap | Incident | Tidak ada incident; process lanjut |
| Handler | Error boundary / error event subprocess | Escalation boundary / escalation event subprocess |
| Interrupting | Error catch harus interrupting | Escalation catch bisa interrupting/non-interrupting |
| Use case | Tidak bisa mencapai expected result | Perlu perhatian/reaksi bisnis |
| Code | `errorCode` | `escalationCode` |

Rule praktis:

```text
Kalau proses tidak boleh lanjut normal → Error.
Kalau proses masih boleh lanjut tapi perlu perhatian → Escalation.
```

---

## 7. Compensation Events

## 7.1 Makna

Compensation event dipakai untuk undo/reversal atas aktivitas yang **sudah berhasil selesai**, ketika hasilnya tidak lagi diinginkan. Camunda 8.9 menjelaskan compensation boundary event ditempel ke activity yang ingin bisa di-revert; boundary event tersebut terkait dengan compensation handler, yaitu activity ber-marker compensation yang bertugas membalik efek activity tersebut. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/compensation-events/))

Mental model:

```text
Compensation bukan rollback database transaction.
Compensation adalah business reversal.
```

Contoh:

| Original Action | Compensation |
|---|---|
| Reserve budget | Release budget |
| Register case | Cancel registration |
| Send payment instruction | Send reversal/refund instruction |
| Create investigation | Close investigation as cancelled |
| Send notification | Send correction/cancellation notice |
| Lock entity | Unlock entity |

---

## 7.2 Compensation Trigger Semantics

Saat compensation intermediate throw atau compensation end event dimasuki, Camunda memicu compensation dalam scope dan memanggil compensation handler untuk activity yang sudah selesai; handler untuk activity yang masih aktif atau sudah terminated tidak dipanggil. Compensation throw event tetap aktif sampai semua handler selesai. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/compensation-events/))

Practical consequence:

```text
Compensation hanya untuk step yang sudah sukses.
Kalau task belum selesai, compensation handler-nya tidak berjalan.
```

---

## 7.3 Compensation Pattern: Case Registration Reversal

```text
Register Case
  │
  └── Compensation Boundary
          ↓
      Undo Register Case

Later:
  Error / Cancel / Rejection after registration
      ↓
  Throw Compensation
      ↓
  End: Case Reversed
```

Implementation:

```text
Original worker:
  register-case

Compensation worker:
  undo-register-case
```

Original action:

```sql
UPDATE case_record
SET status = 'REGISTERED',
    registered_at = now()
WHERE case_id = #{caseId}
  AND status = 'APPROVED';
```

Compensation action:

```sql
UPDATE case_record
SET status = 'REGISTRATION_REVERSED',
    reversed_at = now(),
    reversal_reason = #{reason}
WHERE case_id = #{caseId}
  AND status = 'REGISTERED';
```

Production rule:

```text
Compensation handler harus idempotent.
Kalau dijalankan dua kali, hasilnya tetap aman.
```

---

## 7.4 Compensation Scope

Camunda docs menyebut compensation by default memanggil semua compensation handler di scope; jika order penting, compensation dapat ditargetkan ke activity spesifik memakai `activityRef`. Compensation handler child process tidak otomatis dipanggil; triggering compensation berhenti pada call activity, sehingga untuk revert efek child process, compensation boundary event perlu ditempel pada call activity. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/compensation-events/))

Practical implication:

```text
Jika Call Activity: Payment Process punya side effect,
parent jangan berharap compensation otomatis masuk ke dalam child process.
Modelkan compensation boundary pada call activity.
```

Contoh:

```text
Call Activity: Execute Payment Instruction
  │
  └── Compensation Boundary
          ↓
      Call Activity / Service Task: Reverse Payment Instruction
```

---

## 7.5 Compensation Ordering

Default compensation handlers bisa dipanggil sekaligus tanpa urutan spesifik. Jika reversal harus berurutan, trigger compensation untuk activity tertentu satu per satu.

Contoh butuh urutan:

```text
1. Cancel external notification
2. Reverse registration
3. Release reserved resource
```

Model:

```text
Throw Compensation for Notify Applicant
  ↓
Throw Compensation for Register Case
  ↓
Throw Compensation for Reserve Resource
```

---

## 7.6 Saga Thinking

Untuk microservice / external system, gunakan pola saga-like:

```text
Do A
  ↓
Do B
  ↓
Do C
  ↓
If failure after C:
  Undo C
  Undo B
  Undo A
```

Contoh case management:

```text
Reserve Case Number
  ↓
Register Case
  ↓
Notify External Agency
  ↓
Gateway: later cancellation?
  ├── yes → Compensate Notify → Compensate Register → Release Case Number
  └── no  → Continue
```

---

## 8. Cancel / Terminate Thinking

## 8.1 Cancel vs Terminate vs Business Cancellation

Dalam BPMN symbol reference, “cancel event” biasanya terkait transaction semantics. Dalam Camunda 8.9 BPMN coverage, cancel event terlihat sebagai symbol category tetapi tidak ditandai seperti documented executable elements yang punya halaman detail seperti error/escalation/compensation/terminate; untuk production Camunda 8, pola pembatalan yang praktis biasanya memakai **message/event subprocess + terminate end event + explicit domain state update**. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/bpmn-coverage/))

Bedakan:

| Konsep | Makna |
|---|---|
| Business cancellation | Case/order/request dibatalkan secara domain |
| Terminate end event | Menghentikan semua active element instance dalam scope |
| Compensation | Membalik side effect yang sudah selesai |
| Cancel event BPMN transaction | Konsep BPMN transaction-specific; validasi dukungan engine sebelum dipakai |

---

## 8.2 Terminate End Event

Terminate end event menghentikan semua element instances dalam flow scope yang sama. Jika berada di process scope, ia menghentikan semua element instances process instance dan process selesai; jika berada dalam subprocess, hanya element instances di subprocess tersebut yang dihentikan. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/terminate-events/))

Pattern:

```text
Parallel:
  ├── Long Manual Review
  ├── External Verification
  └── Duplicate Check
          ↓
      Gateway: duplicate confirmed?
          ├── yes → Mark Duplicate → Terminate End
          └── no  → Continue
```

Makna:

```text
Jika duplicate confirmed,
manual review dan external verification yang masih aktif dihentikan.
```

---

## 8.3 Business Cancellation Pattern

```text
Main Case Process
  ├── Validate Case
  ├── External Verification
  ├── Manual Review
  └── Register Case

Event Subprocess:
  Message Start: CancelCaseRequested
    ↓
  Validate Cancellation Allowed
    ↓
  Mark Case Cancelled
    ↓
  Throw Compensation if needed
    ↓
  Terminate / End: Case Cancelled
```

Jersey endpoint:

```java
@Path("/cases/{caseId}/cancellation-requests")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CaseCancellationResource {

    private final CaseCancellationApplicationService service;

    @POST
    public Response requestCancellation(
            @PathParam("caseId") String caseId,
            CancellationRequest request
    ) {
        service.requestCancellation(caseId, request);

        return Response.accepted(Map.of(
                "caseId", caseId,
                "status", "CANCELLATION_REQUEST_ACCEPTED"
        )).build();
    }
}
```

Application service:

```java
public final class CaseCancellationApplicationService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final WorkflowMessageService workflowMessageService;

    public void requestCancellation(String caseId, CancellationRequest request) {
        caseRepository.insertCancellationRequest(
                caseId,
                request.reasonCode(),
                request.requestedBy()
        );

        workflowMessageService.publishMessage(
                "CancelCaseRequested",
                caseId,
                Map.of(
                        "cancellationReasonCode", request.reasonCode(),
                        "cancellationRequestedBy", request.requestedBy()
                )
        );
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text
Cancellation request adalah domain event.
Jangan langsung hard-delete process/domain data.
Persist request, correlate message, route process.
```

---

## 9. Practical Decision Matrix

```text
Problem terjadi di service task:
│
├── Apakah ini transient technical failure?
│   └── fail job + retry
│
├── Apakah retry habis?
│   └── incident + runbook/manual fix
│
├── Apakah task berhasil memberi expected negative result?
│   └── complete job with variable + XOR gateway
│
├── Apakah task tidak bisa mencapai expected result karena business exception?
│   └── throw BPMN error + boundary/event subprocess handler
│
├── Apakah proses bisa lanjut tapi butuh perhatian/approval?
│   └── throw escalation / create escalation task
│
├── Apakah action yang sudah sukses harus dibalik?
│   └── compensation
│
└── Apakah seluruh scope harus dihentikan?
    └── terminate end / cancellation event subprocess
```

---

## 10. Production Example: External Verification Failure

### Scenario

```text
Case dikirim.
System meminta external verification.
External provider bisa:
  - success
  - not found
  - provider timeout
  - invalid identity
  - response terlambat
```

### Model

```text
Send Verification Request
  ↓
Event-Based Gateway
  ├── Message: VerificationCompleted
  │       ↓
  │   Evaluate Verification Result
  │       ├── result = VERIFIED → Continue
  │       ├── result = NOT_FOUND → Manual Verification
  │       └── result = INVALID_IDENTITY → Throw Error: INVALID_IDENTITY
  │
  └── Timer: PT48H
          ↓
      Throw Escalation: VERIFICATION_TIMEOUT
```

### Handling

| Situation | Pattern |
|---|---|
| Provider down saat send request | fail job + retry |
| Provider tidak balas 48 jam | timer + escalation |
| Provider balas NOT_FOUND | normal result + gateway |
| Provider balas INVALID_IDENTITY | BPMN error jika process tidak bisa lanjut normal |
| Callback setelah timeout | late callback table + reconciliation |
| Case sudah registered lalu harus cancel | compensation |

---

## 11. PostgreSQL Tables untuk Recovery

### A. Error / Exception Audit

```sql
CREATE TABLE workflow_exception_log (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    process_id VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    process_instance_key VARCHAR(128),
    element_id VARCHAR(128),
    exception_type VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    error_code VARCHAR(128),
    escalation_code VARCHAR(128),
    reason_code VARCHAR(128),
    message TEXT,
    payload JSONB,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

---

### B. Compensation Action Log

```sql
CREATE TABLE compensation_action_log (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    original_action VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    compensation_action VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(256) NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    reason_code VARCHAR(128),
    requested_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    error_message TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT uq_compensation_idempotency UNIQUE (idempotency_key)
);
```

---

### C. Cancellation Request

```sql
CREATE TABLE case_cancellation_request (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    requested_by VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    reason_code VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    reason_text TEXT,
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'REQUESTED',
    requested_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    decided_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    CONSTRAINT uq_case_cancellation_active UNIQUE (case_id, status)
);
```

Catatan praktis: unique `(case_id, status)` terlalu ketat jika status historis bisa berulang. Di production PostgreSQL, sering lebih aman pakai partial unique index:

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_case_cancellation_active
ON case_cancellation_request(case_id)
WHERE status IN ('REQUESTED', 'IN_REVIEW');
```

---

## 12. MyBatis Mapper Pattern

### A. Insert workflow exception

```xml
<insert id="insertWorkflowException">
  INSERT INTO workflow_exception_log (
      case_id,
      process_id,
      process_instance_key,
      element_id,
      exception_type,
      error_code,
      escalation_code,
      reason_code,
      message,
      payload
  )
  VALUES (
      #{caseId},
      #{processId},
      #{processInstanceKey},
      #{elementId},
      #{exceptionType},
      #{errorCode},
      #{escalationCode},
      #{reasonCode},
      #{message},
      CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb)
  )
</insert>
```

### B. Idempotent compensation log

```xml
<insert id="upsertCompensationAction">
  INSERT INTO compensation_action_log (
      case_id,
      original_action,
      compensation_action,
      idempotency_key,
      reason_code,
      status
  )
  VALUES (
      #{caseId},
      #{originalAction},
      #{compensationAction},
      #{idempotencyKey},
      #{reasonCode},
      'PENDING'
  )
  ON CONFLICT (idempotency_key)
  DO NOTHING
</insert>
```

### C. Mark compensation completed

```xml
<update id="markCompensationCompleted">
  UPDATE compensation_action_log
  SET
      status = 'COMPLETED',
      completed_at = now(),
      error_message = NULL
  WHERE idempotency_key = #{idempotencyKey}
    AND status IN ('PENDING', 'FAILED')
</update>
```

---

## 13. Implementation Pattern: Compensation Worker

```java
public final class UndoRegisterCaseWorker {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final CompensationRepository compensationRepository;

    public void handle(JobClient client, ActivatedJob job) {
        Map<String, Object> vars = job.getVariablesAsMap();

        String caseId = (String) vars.get("caseId");
        String reasonCode = (String) vars.getOrDefault(
                "compensationReasonCode",
                "PROCESS_COMPENSATION"
        );

        String idempotencyKey = caseId + ":undo-register-case";

        try {
            compensationRepository.upsertAction(
                    caseId,
                    "register-case",
                    "undo-register-case",
                    idempotencyKey,
                    reasonCode
            );

            caseRepository.reverseRegistration(caseId, reasonCode);

            compensationRepository.markCompleted(idempotencyKey);

            client.newCompleteCommand(job.getKey())
                    .variables(Map.of(
                            "registrationCompensated", true
                    ))
                    .send()
                    .join();

        } catch (Exception ex) {
            compensationRepository.markFailed(idempotencyKey, ex.getMessage());

            client.newFailCommand(job.getKey())
                    .retries(Math.max(job.getRetries() - 1, 0))
                    .errorMessage(ex.getMessage())
                    .send()
                    .join();
        }
    }
}
```

---

## 14. Naming Convention

### Error Code

Gunakan noun/condition yang spesifik:

```text
IDENTITY_NOT_RESOLVABLE
RISK_ASSESSMENT_FAILED
DOCUMENT_VERIFICATION_FAILED
CASE_ALREADY_CLOSED
PAYMENT_INSTRUCTION_REJECTED
```

Hindari:

```text
ERROR
FAILED
EXCEPTION
TECHNICAL_ERROR
BAD
```

### Escalation Code

```text
REVIEW_SLA_WARNING
HIGH_RISK_CASE_DETECTED
POLICY_EXCEPTION_FOUND
VERIFICATION_TIMEOUT
MANUAL_OVERSIGHT_REQUIRED
```

### Compensation Action

```text
undo-register-case
release-reserved-case-number
reverse-payment-instruction
cancel-external-notification
unlock-subject-record
```

### Cancellation Message

```text
CancelCaseRequested
WithdrawApplicationRequested
StopInvestigationRequested
CloseDuplicateCaseRequested
```

---

## 15. Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Gejala | Dampak | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Semua exception jadi BPMN error | Technical timeout dibuat boundary error di semua task | Diagram bloat | Retry/incident untuk technical failure |
| Expected negative result jadi error | `creditWorthy=false` dilempar error | Semantik kacau | Variable + gateway |
| Error tidak ditangkap | Error end tanpa boundary/event subprocess | Incident / stuck | Tambahkan handler |
| Escalation dianggap error | SLA warning menghentikan proses | Over-interruption | Escalation non-interrupting |
| Escalation tanpa handler | Throw escalation tapi tidak ada catch | Process lanjut tanpa efek | Tambahkan catch/audit atau jangan throw |
| Compensation dianggap rollback ACID | Harap external API bisa rollback otomatis | Inconsistent state | Explicit business reversal |
| Compensation handler tidak idempotent | Retry reversal membuat side effect ganda | Data corruption | Idempotency key |
| Terminate tanpa update domain state | Process berhenti tapi DB status tidak berubah | Audit gap | Mark domain state sebelum terminate |
| Cancellation = delete data | Case dihapus | Hilang audit/regulatory trail | Status `CANCELLED` + audit |
| Catch-all error terlalu generik | Semua error masuk satu path | Root cause kabur | Specific handler + catch-all fallback |

---

## 16. Review Checklist

Untuk setiap error/escalation/compensation/cancel design, cek:

```text
1. Ini expected result atau exception?
2. Kalau exception, apakah business atau technical?
3. Kalau technical, apakah retry cukup?
4. Kalau retry habis, runbook incident-nya apa?
5. Kalau business exception, errorCode apa?
6. Error ditangkap di boundary, event subprocess, atau parent process?
7. Escalation perlu interrupting atau non-interrupting?
8. Kalau escalation tidak tertangkap, apakah itu acceptable?
9. Activity mana yang punya side effect dan perlu compensation?
10. Compensation handler idempotent?
11. Compensation order penting atau tidak?
12. Cancellation request dipersist sebelum process message?
13. Terminate event scope-nya benar?
14. Domain status di DB sinkron dengan process path?
15. Audit cukup untuk menjawab siapa, kapan, kenapa, action apa?
```

---

## 17. Testing Matrix

| Scenario | Expected |
|---|---|
| Technical transient failure | Job retry |
| Retry exhausted | Incident created |
| Expected negative result | Gateway route, no BPMN error |
| Worker throws known BPMN error | Boundary/event subprocess catches |
| Worker throws unknown BPMN error | Incident/unhandled error behavior |
| Child process throws error | Parent call activity boundary catches |
| Escalation caught non-interrupting | Main flow continues + handler runs |
| Escalation caught interrupting | Attached scope terminated |
| Escalation not caught | Process continues without incident |
| Compensation triggered | Completed activity handlers invoked |
| Compensation triggered before activity complete | Handler not invoked |
| Compensation handler fails | Retry/incident/manual recovery |
| Terminate in process scope | All active process elements cancelled |
| Terminate inside subprocess | Only subprocess scope cancelled |
| Cancel message duplicate | Idempotent cancellation request |
| Cancel after process completed | Store/reject as late cancellation |

---

## 18. Ringkasan Seri 8

Inti praktis:

```text
Error        = exceptional condition that changes normal process path
Escalation   = non-critical business attention signal to higher scope
Compensation = explicit undo/reversal for completed successful work
Terminate    = stop all active element instances in current flow scope
Cancellation = domain request/state transition, not data deletion
Incident     = operational runtime failure, usually technical/retry exhausted
```

Decision rule:

```text
Expected result                → variable + gateway
Transient technical failure    → retry
Retry exhausted                → incident + runbook
Business exception             → BPMN error
Business attention             → escalation
Completed side effect unwanted → compensation
Whole scope must stop          → terminate/cancellation path
```

Untuk production Camunda/Jersey/MyBatis/PostgreSQL:

```text
Persist domain state first.
Throw/correlate BPMN event second.
Make compensation idempotent.
Do not delete audit.
Do not over-model technical retry in BPMN.
Always define error/escalation code, owner, recovery path, and runbook.
```

---
