**Sources:**

- [Error events | Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/error-events/)

## Response:
7/16/2026, 9:56:13 AM

Lanjut ke **Seri 9/12 — Boundary Events**. Fokusnya: kapan event ditempel ke task/subprocess, kapan event membatalkan aktivitas utama, dan kapan hanya membuat side-flow seperti reminder/escalation.

# Seri 9/12 - Boundary Events

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas **boundary event**, yaitu event yang ditempelkan pada **activity** atau **subprocess** untuk menangani sesuatu yang terjadi **selama activity/subprocess itu masih aktif**.

Mental model:

```text
Boundary Event = "apa yang terjadi kalau event X muncul ketika task/subprocess Y sedang berjalan?"
```

Boundary event adalah salah satu simbol paling penting untuk workflow production karena langsung terkait dengan:

```text
timeout
SLA
reminder
callback
cancellation
exception handling
escalation
compensation
recovery path
```

Dalam BPMN/Camunda, event bisa membuat token menunggu, bereaksi, atau menginterupsi progress token. Camunda menjelaskan event sebagai sesuatu yang terjadi; process bisa menangkap event atau melempar event, dan event dapat digunakan bukan hanya untuk wait state, tetapi juga untuk menginterupsi progress token. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/events/))

---

# 2. Core Mental Model

Boundary event selalu punya **host activity**.

```text
Activity / Subprocess
  │
  ├── Boundary Event
  │       ↓
  │   Exception / timeout / reminder / recovery path
  │
  └── Normal completion
          ↓
      Continue normal flow
```

Contoh:

```text
User Task: Senior Officer Review
  │
  ├── Boundary Timer: PT48H
  │       ↓
  │   Escalate Review Timeout
  │
  └── Officer completes review
          ↓
      Continue Case Processing
```

Artinya:

```text
Selama "Senior Officer Review" masih aktif,
timer ikut aktif.

Jika review selesai duluan:
  boundary timer tidak relevan lagi.

Jika timer terjadi duluan:
  boundary path dijalankan.
```

---

# 3. Interrupting vs Non-Interrupting

Ini konsep paling penting.

## A. Interrupting Boundary Event

Interrupting boundary event **membatalkan activity utama**.

```text
User Task: Review Case
  │
  ├── Interrupting Timer: PT48H
  │       ↓
  │   Escalate to Supervisor
  │
  └── normal completion
```

Makna:

```text
Kalau timer 48 jam terjadi,
Review Case dihentikan.
User tidak bisa lagi menyelesaikan task utama.
Process pindah ke escalation path.
```

Visual BPMN biasanya memakai **solid border** pada boundary event.

---

## B. Non-Interrupting Boundary Event

Non-interrupting boundary event **tidak membatalkan activity utama**.

```text
User Task: Review Case
  │
  ├── Non-Interrupting Timer: every 24h
  │       ↓
  │   Send Reminder
  │
  └── Review Case tetap aktif
```

Makna:

```text
Timer membuat side-flow reminder.
Task review tetap berjalan.
User masih bisa complete task utama.
```

Visual BPMN biasanya memakai **dashed border** pada boundary event.

Camunda message boundary docs memberikan contoh prinsip ini: jika non-interrupting boundary event ter-trigger, activity tidak diterminasi dan multiple messages dapat dikorelasikan. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/message-events/))

---

# 4. Diagram Decomposition

```text
Boundary Events
│
├── 1. Core Semantics
│   ├── Attached to activity/subprocess
│   ├── Active only while host activity is active
│   ├── Interrupting
│   ├── Non-interrupting
│   └── Normal path vs boundary path
│
├── 2. Timer Boundary Event
│   ├── Timeout
│   ├── SLA breach
│   ├── Reminder
│   ├── Escalation deadline
│   └── Multi-stage timeout
│
├── 3. Message Boundary Event
│   ├── Cancel while waiting
│   ├── Alternative callback
│   ├── External update
│   ├── Document submitted
│   └── Multiple message handlers
│
├── 4. Error Boundary Event
│   ├── Catch BPMN error from activity/subprocess
│   ├── Business exception
│   ├── Recovery path
│   ├── Manual resolution
│   └── Parent catch for call activity
│
├── 5. Escalation Boundary Event
│   ├── Business attention
│   ├── Supervisor notification
│   ├── SLA warning
│   ├── Interrupting / non-interrupting escalation
│   └── Higher scope communication
│
├── 6. Compensation Boundary Event
│   ├── Attach reversible action
│   ├── Compensation handler
│   ├── Undo completed activity
│   ├── Idempotent reversal
│   └── Saga-like recovery
│
├── 7. Signal / Conditional Boundary Event
│   ├── Broadcast reaction
│   ├── Variable-driven reaction
│   ├── Operational trigger
│   └── Use carefully
│
└── 8. Boundary vs Event Subprocess
    ├── Event applies to one activity → boundary
    ├── Event applies to whole scope → event subprocess
    ├── Handler simple → boundary path
    ├── Handler complex/multi-step → event subprocess
    └── Variable scope difference
```

---

# 5. Quick Reference Table

| Boundary Event | Interrupting? | Non-Interrupting? | Practical Use | Common Pattern |
|---|---:|---:|---|---|
| Timer Boundary | Ya | Ya | Timeout, SLA, reminder | Review overdue, callback timeout |
| Message Boundary | Ya | Ya | Cancel/update while task waits | Cancel request, alternative response |
| Error Boundary | Ya | Tidak | Catch BPMN error | Business exception recovery |
| Escalation Boundary | Ya | Ya | Business attention/escalation | SLA warning, policy exception |
| Compensation Boundary | Special | N/A | Attach undo handler | Reverse completed action |
| Signal Boundary | Ya | Ya | Broadcast reaction | Emergency stop / operational signal |
| Conditional Boundary | Ya | Ya | Variable condition reaction | State changed while task active |

Catatan: dukungan detail setiap event bisa berbeda antara Camunda 7 dan Camunda 8, jadi untuk executable model harus selalu cek BPMN coverage engine yang dipakai. Camunda 8 docs sendiri menandai bahwa tidak semua event didukung dan merujuk ke halaman BPMN coverage untuk gambaran dukungan lengkap. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/events/))

---

# 6. Boundary Event vs Intermediate Catch Event

## Intermediate catch event

```text
Task A
  ↓
Wait Message: RegistryCheckCompleted
  ↓
Task B
```

Makna:

```text
Token memang sampai di titik wait.
Process secara eksplisit berhenti di situ.
```

---

## Boundary event

```text
Receive Task: Wait Registry Response
  │
  ├── Boundary Timer: PT48H
  │       ↓
  │   Escalate Timeout
  │
  └── response received
          ↓
      Continue
```

Makna:

```text
Activity utama sedang aktif.
Boundary event adalah kejadian alternatif/exception selama activity itu aktif.
```

Camunda best-practice docs menjelaskan receive task dengan boundary event sebagai alternatif event-based gateway: receive task menunggu response, sementara boundary timer dapat menangani kondisi response tidak datang, dan boundary message dapat menangani message lain seperti “not deliverable”. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/best-practices/modeling/modeling-beyond-the-happy-path/))

---

# 7. Timer Boundary Event

## 7.1 Makna

Timer boundary event terjadi ketika waktu tertentu tercapai **selama host activity/subprocess masih aktif**.

Use case:

```text
User task overdue
External callback timeout
Manual review SLA breach
Document submission deadline
Reminder while task is pending
Subprocess phase timeout
```

---

## 7.2 Interrupting Timer Boundary - Timeout

```text
User Task: Senior Officer Review
  │
  ├── Interrupting Timer: PT48H
  │       ↓
  │   Escalate to Supervisor
  │       ↓
  │   End / Continue escalation process
  │
  └── normal review completion
          ↓
      Continue
```

Makna:

```text
Jika 48 jam lewat sebelum review selesai,
task review dibatalkan.
Process tidak lagi menunggu officer pertama.
```

Cocok untuk:

```text
hard deadline
approval expired
external response no longer useful
manual task harus diambil alih
```

---

## 7.3 Non-Interrupting Timer Boundary - Reminder

```text
User Task: Senior Officer Review
  │
  ├── Non-Interrupting Timer: every 24h
  │       ↓
  │   Send Reminder
  │       ↓
  │   End reminder branch
  │
  └── Review Case tetap aktif
```

Makna:

```text
Reminder dikirim,
tetapi user task tetap aktif.
```

Cocok untuk:

```text
reminder email
notification to assignee
dashboard alert
soft SLA warning
```

Production warning:

```text
Repeating non-interrupting timer bisa membuat reminder spam.
Selalu desain max count, stop condition, atau escalation stage.
```

---

## 7.4 Multi-Stage SLA Pattern

```text
User Task: Review Case
  │
  ├── Non-Interrupting Timer: PT24H
  │       ↓
  │   Send Reminder to Officer
  │
  ├── Non-Interrupting Timer: PT48H
  │       ↓
  │   Notify Supervisor
  │
  └── Interrupting Timer: PT72H
          ↓
      Reassign to Escalation Queue
```

Makna:

```text
24 jam: reminder ringan
48 jam: supervisor aware
72 jam: task utama dibatalkan dan dialihkan
```

Ini lebih realistis untuk workflow enterprise daripada hanya satu timeout keras.

---

## 7.5 Timer Boundary pada Subprocess

```text
Subprocess: External Verification
  ├── Send Verification Request
  ├── Wait Verification Response
  └── Evaluate Response

Boundary Timer: PT48H
  ↓
Escalate Verification Timeout
```

Makna:

```text
Timer berlaku untuk seluruh fase External Verification.
Jika subprocess belum selesai dalam 48 jam,
semua activity aktif di dalam subprocess dihentikan.
```

Gunakan ini jika SLA berlaku untuk satu fase, bukan satu task.

---

# 8. Message Boundary Event

## 8.1 Makna

Message boundary event menangkap message saat activity/subprocess tertentu aktif.

Camunda 8 message docs menyebut activity dapat punya satu atau lebih message boundary events, dan masing-masing harus punya message name unik. Saat activity dimasuki, message subscription dibuat untuk setiap boundary message event. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/message-events/))

---

## 8.2 Interrupting Message Boundary - Cancel While Waiting

```text
Receive Task: Wait Registry Response
  │
  ├── Message Boundary: CancelCaseRequested
  │       ↓
  │   Cancel Verification
  │       ↓
  │   Mark Case Cancelled
  │
  └── RegistryCheckCompleted
          ↓
      Continue
```

Makna:

```text
Selama process menunggu registry response,
customer/system bisa meminta cancel.

Jika CancelCaseRequested masuk duluan,
wait registry response dibatalkan.
```

---

## 8.3 Non-Interrupting Message Boundary - Additional Info While Task Continues

```text
User Task: Review Case
  │
  ├── Non-Interrupting Message Boundary: AdditionalDocumentSubmitted
  │       ↓
  │   Attach Document to Case
  │       ↓
  │   Notify Reviewer
  │
  └── Review Case tetap aktif
```

Makna:

```text
Dokumen tambahan masuk.
Task review tidak dibatalkan.
Reviewer diberi tahu dan tetap bisa menyelesaikan review.
```

Cocok untuk:

```text
additional document submitted
comment added
external information updated
supplemental evidence received
```

---

## 8.4 Multiple Message Boundary Events

```text
Receive Task: Wait External Provider Response
  │
  ├── Message Boundary: ProviderReturnedResult
  │       ↓
  │   Process Result
  │
  ├── Message Boundary: ProviderRejectedRequest
  │       ↓
  │   Handle Rejection
  │
  └── Message Boundary: CancelCaseRequested
          ↓
      Cancel Waiting
```

Practical warning:

```text
Setiap message boundary harus punya message name unik.
Jangan membuat semua callback masuk ke satu generic message lalu parsing manual tanpa kontrak.
```

---

## 8.5 Jersey Callback Mapping

```java
@Path("/cases/{caseId}/documents")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CaseDocumentResource {

    private final CaseDocumentApplicationService service;

    @POST
    public Response submitAdditionalDocument(
            @PathParam("caseId") String caseId,
            SubmitDocumentRequest request
    ) {
        service.submitAdditionalDocument(caseId, request);

        return Response.accepted(Map.of(
                "caseId", caseId,
                "status", "DOCUMENT_ACCEPTED"
        )).build();
    }
}
```

Application service:

```java
public final class CaseDocumentApplicationService {

    private final CaseDocumentRepository documentRepository;
    private final WorkflowMessageService workflowMessageService;

    public void submitAdditionalDocument(String caseId, SubmitDocumentRequest request) {
        documentRepository.insertDocumentIfAbsent(
                caseId,
                request.documentId(),
                request.documentType(),
                request.storageRef()
        );

        workflowMessageService.publishMessage(
                "AdditionalDocumentSubmitted",
                caseId,
                Map.of(
                        "latestDocumentId", request.documentId(),
                        "latestDocumentType", request.documentType()
                )
        );
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text
Persist document first.
Then correlate message.
```

---

# 9. Error Boundary Event

## 9.1 Makna

Error boundary event menangkap BPMN error dari activity/subprocess. Error boundary event cocok untuk **business exception**, bukan sekadar technical retry.

Camunda error event docs menjelaskan error memiliki `errorCode`; error dapat dilempar oleh error end event atau oleh worker menggunakan throw error command, lalu ditangkap oleh error boundary event atau error event subprocess. Jika tidak tertangkap, incident dibuat. ([Camunda 7 Documentation](https://docs.camunda.org/get-started/archive/rpa/error-handling/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 9.2 Pattern: Business Exception Recovery

```text
Service Task: Check Applicant Identity
  │
  ├── Error Boundary: IDENTITY_NOT_RESOLVABLE
  │       ↓
  │   User Task: Resolve Identity Manually
  │       ↓
  │   Gateway: identity resolved?
  │       ├── yes → Continue
  │       └── no  → Reject Case
  │
  └── success
          ↓
      Continue
```

Makna:

```text
Worker tidak sekadar gagal teknis.
Worker berhasil menentukan bahwa identity tidak bisa di-resolve secara otomatis.
Process perlu human recovery.
```

---

## 9.3 Java Worker: Throw BPMN Error

```java
private void handle(JobClient client, ActivatedJob job) {
    String caseId = (String) job.getVariablesAsMap().get("caseId");

    try {
        IdentityCheckResult result = identityService.check(caseId);

        if (result.status() == IdentityStatus.NOT_RESOLVABLE) {
            client.newThrowErrorCommand(job)
                    .errorCode("IDENTITY_NOT_RESOLVABLE")
                    .errorMessage("Applicant identity cannot be resolved automatically")
                    .variables(Map.of(
                            "identityCheckStatus", "NOT_RESOLVABLE",
                            "identityFailureReason", result.reasonCode()
                    ))
                    .send()
                    .join();
            return;
        }

        client.newCompleteCommand(job.getKey())
                .variables(Map.of(
                        "identityCheckStatus", "RESOLVED",
                        "identityReferenceNo", result.referenceNo()
                ))
                .send()
                .join();

    } catch (ExternalServiceUnavailableException ex) {
        client.newFailCommand(job.getKey())
                .retries(Math.max(job.getRetries() - 1, 0))
                .errorMessage(ex.getMessage())
                .send()
                .join();
    }
}
```

Decision rule:

```text
Business exception → throw BPMN error
Technical transient failure → fail job + retry
Expected negative result → complete job + gateway
```

---

## 9.4 Error Boundary pada Call Activity

```text
Call Activity: Risk Assessment Process
  │
  ├── Error Boundary: RISK_ASSESSMENT_FAILED
  │       ↓
  │   Manual Risk Review
  │
  └── success
          ↓
      Continue Case Intake
```

Cocok jika child process bisa melempar business error yang harus ditangani parent process.

---

## 9.5 Anti-Pattern Error Boundary

| Anti-Pattern | Masalah | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Semua technical failure dibuat BPMN error | Diagram penuh error path teknis | Gunakan retry/incident |
| Expected rejection dibuat error | Business semantics kacau | Output variable + gateway |
| Error boundary tanpa recovery jelas | Exception path menggantung | Tambahkan manual task / rejection / compensation |
| Catch-all error terlalu awal | Root cause hilang | Specific error first, catch-all fallback |
| Error code generic `ERROR` | Sulit audit/debug | Gunakan error code spesifik |

---

# 10. Escalation Boundary Event

## 10.1 Makna

Escalation boundary event menangkap escalation dari activity/subprocess/called process. Escalation berbeda dari error: escalation bersifat non-critical dan throwing location bisa tetap melanjutkan execution. Camunda docs menyebut escalation dipakai untuk berkomunikasi ke flow scope yang lebih tinggi; escalation punya `escalationCode`, dapat dilempar oleh intermediate escalation throw event atau escalation end event, dan dapat ditangkap oleh boundary event atau event subprocess. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/escalation-events/))

---

## 10.2 Pattern: Non-Interrupting Business Escalation

```text
Subprocess: Manual Review
  ├── Officer Review
  ├── Additional Check
  └── Submit Recommendation

Boundary Escalation: HIGH_RISK_ATTENTION_REQUIRED
  ↓
Notify Supervisor
  ↓
Create Escalation Audit
```

Makna:

```text
Main manual review bisa tetap berjalan.
Supervisor tetap mendapatkan visibility.
```

---

## 10.3 Pattern: Interrupting Escalation

```text
Subprocess: Standard Review
  │
  ├── Escalation Boundary: CRITICAL_POLICY_EXCEPTION
  │       ↓
  │   Transfer to Special Handling
  │
  └── normal review completion
```

Makna:

```text
Kalau critical policy exception dilempar,
standard review dihentikan.
Case masuk special handling.
```

Use case:

```text
critical risk detected
policy exception
regulatory escalation
supervisor takeover
```

---

## 10.4 Escalation vs Timer Boundary

Timer boundary bisa langsung menuju escalation task tanpa escalation event:

```text
User Task: Review Case
  │
  ├── Timer Boundary: PT48H
  │       ↓
  │   Escalate to Supervisor
```

Escalation boundary/event dipakai kalau ada subprocess/called process yang **melempar escalation ke parent scope**.

```text
Subprocess: Review Case
  ├── Throw Escalation: REVIEW_SLA_WARNING
  └── Continue

Parent catches escalation:
  Boundary Escalation: REVIEW_SLA_WARNING
      ↓
  Notify Supervisor
```

Rule praktis:

```text
Timer boundary cocok untuk waktu.
Escalation event cocok untuk komunikasi business condition ke scope lebih tinggi.
```

---

# 11. Compensation Boundary Event

## 11.1 Makna

Compensation boundary event ditempelkan ke activity yang efeknya mungkin perlu dibalik nanti. Camunda compensation docs menjelaskan compensation boundary event dikaitkan dengan compensation handler, yaitu activity dengan compensation marker yang bertugas membalik efek activity yang sudah berhasil selesai. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/compensation-events/))

Mental model:

```text
Compensation boundary tidak langsung jalan saat activity aktif.
Ia mendaftarkan "cara undo" jika activity berhasil selesai.
Undo baru dijalankan saat compensation dilempar.
```

---

## 11.2 Pattern: Register Case + Undo Register

```text
Service Task: Register Case
  │
  └── Compensation Boundary
          ↓
      Service Task: Undo Register Case

Later:
  Throw Compensation
      ↓
  Undo Register Case executed
```

Makna:

```text
Jika Register Case sudah berhasil,
dan kemudian process perlu rollback secara bisnis,
Undo Register Case dipanggil.
```

Camunda docs menjelaskan compensation hanya memanggil handler untuk activity yang sudah selesai; handler untuk activity yang masih aktif atau sudah terminated tidak dipanggil. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/compensation-events/))

---

## 11.3 PostgreSQL Compensation Log

```sql
CREATE TABLE compensation_action_log (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    original_action VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    compensation_action VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(256) NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    reason_code VARCHAR(128),
    requested_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    error_message TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT uq_compensation_idempotency UNIQUE (idempotency_key)
);
```

---

## 11.4 Compensation Worker

```java
public final class UndoRegisterCaseWorker {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final CompensationRepository compensationRepository;

    public void handle(JobClient client, ActivatedJob job) {
        Map<String, Object> vars = job.getVariablesAsMap();

        String caseId = (String) vars.get("caseId");
        String reasonCode = (String) vars.getOrDefault(
                "compensationReasonCode",
                "PROCESS_COMPENSATION"
        );

        String idempotencyKey = caseId + ":undo-register-case";

        try {
            compensationRepository.insertIfAbsent(
                    caseId,
                    "register-case",
                    "undo-register-case",
                    idempotencyKey,
                    reasonCode
            );

            caseRepository.reverseRegistration(caseId, reasonCode);

            compensationRepository.markCompleted(idempotencyKey);

            client.newCompleteCommand(job.getKey())
                    .variables(Map.of(
                            "registrationCompensated", true
                    ))
                    .send()
                    .join();

        } catch (Exception ex) {
            compensationRepository.markFailed(idempotencyKey, ex.getMessage());

            client.newFailCommand(job.getKey())
                    .retries(Math.max(job.getRetries() - 1, 0))
                    .errorMessage(ex.getMessage())
                    .send()
                    .join();
        }
    }
}
```

Rule production:

```text
Compensation handler harus idempotent.
Compensation bukan rollback teknis, tetapi business reversal.
```

---

## 11.5 Compensation Scope Warning

Camunda docs menyebut compensation handlers dari child process tidak otomatis dipanggil; compensation berhenti di call activity, sehingga untuk membalik efek child process, compensation boundary event perlu ditempel pada call activity. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/compensation-events/))

Practical implication:

```text
Jika Call Activity: Payment Process punya side effect,
parent process harus punya compensation boundary di call activity
atau child process harus menyediakan explicit reversal process.
```

---

# 12. Signal Boundary Event

## 12.1 Makna

Signal boundary event menangkap broadcast signal saat activity/subprocess aktif.

Use case:

```text
EmergencyStopRequested
PolicyUpdated
SystemMaintenanceStarted
RegulatoryHoldApplied
```

Pattern:

```text
Subprocess: Process Case
  │
  ├── Signal Boundary: RegulatoryHoldApplied
  │       ↓
  │   Suspend Case Processing
  │
  └── normal process
```

Signal cocok untuk broadcast. Jangan pakai signal untuk callback satu case/order tertentu; gunakan message event dengan correlation key.

---

# 13. Conditional Boundary Event

## 13.1 Makna

Conditional boundary event aktif saat condition berbasis variable menjadi true selama host activity/subprocess aktif.

Use case:

```text
cancellationRequested = true
casePriority = "URGENT"
policyHoldApplied = true
manualInterventionRequired = true
```

Pattern:

```text
User Task: Review Case
  │
  ├── Conditional Boundary: = cancellationRequested = true
  │       ↓
  │   Cancel Review
  │
  └── normal review completion
```

Caution:

```text
Conditional boundary event powerful, tapi bisa sulit di-debug.
Untuk event dari external system, message boundary biasanya lebih eksplisit.
```

---

# 14. Boundary Event vs Event Subprocess

Ini sering rancu.

Camunda event subprocess docs menjelaskan event subprocess mirip boundary event, tetapi berada **di dalam scope**, bukan ditempelkan pada scope. Event subprocess bisa interrupting atau non-interrupting; non-interrupting bisa dipicu beberapa kali, sedangkan interrupting hanya sekali dan akan menghentikan active instances dalam containing scope. Event subprocess juga dapat mengakses dan memodifikasi local variables dalam scope, sedangkan boundary event berada di luar scope. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/event-subprocesses/))

## Gunakan Boundary Event jika:

```text
Event relevan hanya selama satu activity/subprocess tertentu aktif.
Handler relatif sederhana.
Event harus terlihat langsung menempel pada activity.
```

Contoh:

```text
Review Case overdue
Wait callback timeout
Service task throws BPMN error
Cancel while waiting external response
```

---

## Gunakan Event Subprocess jika:

```text
Event bisa terjadi kapan saja selama process/subprocess aktif.
Handler punya beberapa langkah.
Event berlaku untuk seluruh scope, bukan satu task.
Perlu akses/modifikasi variable lokal scope.
```

Contoh:

```text
CancelCaseRequested kapan saja selama case process aktif
EmergencyStopRequested selama subprocess aktif
PolicyExceptionFound selama review phase aktif
SLA monitoring process-level
```

Camunda best-practice docs juga memakai event subprocess untuk cancellation requested by customer karena cancellation bisa terjadi “wherever we currently are” dalam flow; interrupting message start event pada event subprocess mengekspresikan bahwa main process harus diinterupsi. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/best-practices/modeling/modeling-beyond-the-happy-path/))

---

## Decision Matrix

| Situation | Prefer |
|---|---|
| Timeout satu user task | Timer boundary |
| Timeout seluruh subprocess phase | Timer boundary pada subprocess |
| Cancel hanya saat menunggu external response | Message boundary pada receive task |
| Cancel kapan saja selama process aktif | Event subprocess |
| Error dari service task tertentu | Error boundary |
| Error dari banyak task dalam satu scope | Error event subprocess |
| Reminder selama task aktif | Non-interrupting timer boundary |
| Global emergency selama process aktif | Event subprocess / signal boundary pada subprocess |
| Need access local variables of subprocess | Event subprocess |
| Compensation handler untuk activity tertentu | Compensation boundary |

---

# 15. Practical Pattern: Receive Task + Boundary Events

## Problem

External system bisa memberi beberapa kemungkinan:

```text
success response
failure response
no response
cancel request from user
```

## BPMN

```text
Receive Task: Wait Registry Response
  │
  ├── Message Boundary: RegistryRejectedRequest
  │       ↓
  │   Handle Registry Rejection
  │
  ├── Message Boundary: CancelCaseRequested
  │       ↓
  │   Cancel External Wait
  │
  ├── Timer Boundary: PT48H
  │       ↓
  │   Escalate Timeout
  │
  └── RegistryCheckCompleted
          ↓
      Continue Normal Flow
```

Kenapa ini berguna?

```text
Receive task tetap menjadi wait state utama.
Boundary events menangani alternative/exception event selama wait state aktif.
```

Camunda best-practice docs menyebut receive task dengan boundary events sebagai alternatif event-based gateway; model ini membantu menangani missing response atau message lain yang terjadi ketika sedang menunggu response. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/best-practices/modeling/modeling-beyond-the-happy-path/))

---

# 16. Practical Pattern: User Task SLA

```text
User Task: Senior Officer Review
  │
  ├── Non-Interrupting Timer: PT24H
  │       ↓
  │   Send Reminder to Officer
  │
  ├── Non-Interrupting Timer: PT48H
  │       ↓
  │   Notify Supervisor
  │
  ├── Interrupting Timer: PT72H
  │       ↓
  │   Reassign to Escalation Queue
  │
  └── User completes review
          ↓
      Continue
```

Implementation detail:

| Boundary | Effect | Domain Update |
|---|---|---|
| PT24H reminder | Task tetap aktif | insert `case_task_reminder` |
| PT48H supervisor notice | Task tetap aktif | insert `case_escalation_log` |
| PT72H reassignment | Task utama dibatalkan | update task/case escalation status |

PostgreSQL:

```sql
CREATE TABLE case_task_sla_event (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    task_name VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    sla_event_type VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    event_level VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    triggered_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    payload JSONB,
    CONSTRAINT uq_case_task_sla_event UNIQUE (
        case_id,
        task_name,
        sla_event_type
    )
);
```

---

# 17. Practical Pattern: Error Boundary + Manual Recovery

```text
Service Task: Validate External Identity
  │
  ├── Error Boundary: IDENTITY_NOT_RESOLVABLE
  │       ↓
  │   User Task: Manual Identity Resolution
  │       ↓
  │   Gateway: resolved?
  │       ├── yes → Continue
  │       └── no  → Reject Case
  │
  └── success
          ↓
      Continue
```

Domain table:

```sql
CREATE TABLE case_exception_resolution (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    exception_code VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    resolution_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    assigned_group VARCHAR(128),
    resolved_by VARCHAR(128),
    resolution_note TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    resolved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    CONSTRAINT uq_case_exception_active UNIQUE (
        case_id,
        exception_code,
        resolution_status
    )
);
```

Better partial unique index for active-only resolution:

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uq_case_exception_active
ON case_exception_resolution(case_id, exception_code)
WHERE resolution_status = 'PENDING';
```

---

# 18. Practical Pattern: Cancellation While Review Active

## Option A - Boundary Message on User Task

```text
User Task: Review Case
  │
  ├── Message Boundary: CancelCaseRequested
  │       ↓
  │   Cancel Review
  │       ↓
  │   Mark Case Cancelled
  │
  └── review completed
```

Cocok jika cancellation hanya valid saat review task aktif.

---

## Option B - Event Subprocess

```text
Case Handling Process

Main flow:
  Validate
    ↓
  Review
    ↓
  Register

Event Subprocess:
  Message Start: CancelCaseRequested
    ↓
  Validate Cancellation
    ↓
  Mark Case Cancelled
    ↓
  End
```

Cocok jika cancellation bisa terjadi kapan saja selama process aktif.

---

# 19. Implementation: Cancellation Message Endpoint

```java
@Path("/cases/{caseId}/cancellation-requests")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CaseCancellationResource {

    private final CaseCancellationApplicationService service;

    @POST
    public Response requestCancellation(
            @PathParam("caseId") String caseId,
            CancellationRequest request
    ) {
        service.requestCancellation(caseId, request);

        return Response.accepted(Map.of(
                "caseId", caseId,
                "status", "CANCELLATION_REQUEST_ACCEPTED"
        )).build();
    }
}
```

```java
public final class CaseCancellationApplicationService {

    private final CaseCancellationRepository repository;
    private final WorkflowMessageService workflowMessageService;

    public void requestCancellation(String caseId, CancellationRequest request) {
        repository.insertCancellationRequestIfAbsent(
                caseId,
                request.requestedBy(),
                request.reasonCode(),
                request.reasonText()
        );

        workflowMessageService.publishMessage(
                "CancelCaseRequested",
                caseId,
                Map.of(
                        "cancellationReasonCode", request.reasonCode(),
                        "cancellationRequestedBy", request.requestedBy()
                )
        );
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text
Cancellation request adalah domain event.
Jangan langsung delete data/process.
Persist request dulu, lalu correlate message.
```

---

# 20. Boundary Event Production Checklist

Untuk setiap boundary event, isi ini:

```text
Host activity:
Boundary event type:
Interrupting or non-interrupting:
Trigger:
Message name / timer / errorCode / escalationCode:
Correlation key:
Expected behavior on trigger:
Does host activity continue?
Domain state update:
Duplicate trigger policy:
Late trigger policy:
Retry/incident behavior:
Audit table:
Owner:
Runbook:
```

Contoh:

```text
Host activity:
  Senior Officer Review

Boundary event type:
  Timer boundary

Interrupting:
  No, first reminder
  Yes, final timeout

Trigger:
  PT24H reminder
  PT72H timeout

Expected behavior:
  PT24H sends reminder, task remains active
  PT72H cancels task and routes to escalation queue

Domain state update:
  Insert case_task_sla_event
  Update case_status/escalation_status for final timeout

Owner:
  Case Operations Team
```

---

# 21. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Gejala | Dampak | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Timer boundary interrupting untuk reminder | Reminder membatalkan task | User task hilang | Pakai non-interrupting timer |
| Non-interrupting timer tanpa limit | Reminder spam | Operational noise | Max count / staged escalation |
| Message boundary tanpa correlation key | Callback tidak match instance | Lost event | Gunakan `caseId/orderId` |
| Boundary event ditempel pada task salah | Event tidak aktif saat dibutuhkan | Event missed | Tempel di scope yang benar |
| Error boundary untuk technical retry | BPMN terlalu noisy | Diagram sulit dirawat | Fail job + retry/incident |
| Error boundary tanpa recovery | Exception path kosong | Stuck/unclear operation | Tambahkan manual recovery/reject/compensate |
| Escalation dianggap error | Proses dihentikan padahal hanya warning | Overreaction | Non-interrupting escalation |
| Compensation boundary dianggap langsung undo | Salah paham lifecycle | Reversal tidak terjadi | Butuh compensation throw event |
| Boundary dipakai untuk event process-wide | Harus ditempel banyak tempat | Model bloat | Event subprocess |
| Event subprocess dipakai untuk satu task saja | Model terlalu besar | Kurang lokal/readable | Boundary event |

---

# 22. Testing Matrix

| Scenario | Expected |
|---|---|
| Host task completes before timer | Boundary timer cancelled / not triggered |
| Interrupting timer fires first | Host task terminated, boundary path taken |
| Non-interrupting timer fires | Side-flow runs, host task remains active |
| Message boundary receives matching message | Boundary path runs |
| Duplicate message on non-interrupting boundary | Multiple correlation possible, handler must be idempotent |
| Message arrives after host activity completed | No active subscription; store/reconcile in application layer |
| Worker throws matching BPMN error | Error boundary catches |
| Worker technical failure | Retry/incident, not error boundary |
| Escalation thrown and caught | Escalation handler runs |
| Escalation not caught | Process continues; no incident |
| Compensation thrown after activity completed | Compensation handler invoked |
| Compensation thrown before activity completed | Handler not invoked |
| Boundary event on subprocess fires | Active elements inside subprocess interrupted if interrupting |
| Event applies to whole process | Event subprocess handles better than boundary |

---

# 23. Naming Convention

## Timer Boundary

```text
Review SLA Reached
External Verification Timed Out
Reminder Due
Submission Deadline Reached
```

## Message Boundary

```text
Cancel Case Requested
Additional Document Submitted
Provider Rejected Request
External Update Received
```

## Error Boundary

```text
IDENTITY_NOT_RESOLVABLE
RISK_ASSESSMENT_FAILED
DOCUMENT_VERIFICATION_FAILED
PAYMENT_INSTRUCTION_REJECTED
```

## Escalation Boundary

```text
REVIEW_SLA_WARNING
HIGH_RISK_ATTENTION_REQUIRED
POLICY_EXCEPTION_FOUND
MANUAL_OVERSIGHT_REQUIRED
```

## Compensation Handler

```text
Undo Register Case
Release Reserved Case Number
Reverse Payment Instruction
Cancel External Notification
```

Bad names:

```text
Timer1
Error
Message
Escalate
Undo
Boundary
```

Better names:

```text
Timer: Review SLA Reached
Message: Cancel Case Requested
Error: IDENTITY_NOT_RESOLVABLE
Escalation: HIGH_RISK_ATTENTION_REQUIRED
Compensation: Undo Register Case
```

---

# 24. Practical Review Checklist

Saat review BPMN kantor, untuk setiap boundary event tanyakan:

```text
1. Boundary event ini ditempel ke activity/subprocess yang benar?
2. Event hanya perlu aktif selama host activity aktif?
3. Harus interrupting atau non-interrupting?
4. Kalau interrupting, apakah host activity aman dibatalkan?
5. Kalau non-interrupting, apakah side-flow bisa terjadi berkali-kali?
6. Kalau timer, apakah ini reminder atau hard timeout?
7. Kalau message, apa message name dan correlation key?
8. Kalau error, apakah ini business exception, bukan technical retry?
9. Kalau escalation, apakah main flow tetap boleh lanjut?
10. Kalau compensation, activity apa yang di-undo dan kapan compensation dilempar?
11. Apa domain status yang berubah ketika boundary path diambil?
12. Apa audit table/event yang mencatat boundary trigger?
13. Apa yang terjadi jika event datang terlambat?
14. Apa yang terjadi jika event datang duplicate?
15. Apakah event lebih cocok jadi event subprocess?
```

---

# 25. Ringkasan Seri 9

Inti praktis:

```text
Boundary Event = event yang aktif selama host activity/subprocess aktif.
```

Rule paling penting:

```text
Interrupting boundary event:
  event terjadi → host activity dibatalkan

Non-interrupting boundary event:
  event terjadi → side-flow berjalan, host activity tetap aktif
```

Decision rule:

```text
Timeout hard deadline               → Interrupting timer boundary
Reminder / soft warning             → Non-interrupting timer boundary
Cancel while waiting one activity   → Message boundary
Cancel anytime in process           → Event subprocess
Business exception from task        → Error boundary
Business attention to parent scope  → Escalation boundary
Undo completed action               → Compensation boundary + compensation throw
Broadcast while activity active     → Signal boundary, use carefully
Variable condition while active     → Conditional boundary, use carefully
```

Untuk production Camunda/Jersey/MyBatis/PostgreSQL:

```text
Boundary event harus punya:
  trigger contract
  interrupting semantics
  domain state update
  idempotency policy
  late/duplicate event handling
  audit record
  operational runbook
```

# Seri 10/12 - Data & Artifacts

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini membahas simbol dan konsep data dalam BPMN:

```text id="d7vj77"
Data Object
Data Store
Text Annotation
Group
Association
Process Variable
Local Variable
Input/Output Mapping
Business Key
Correlation Key
Domain State
Audit Data
```

Fokusnya bukan sekadar menggambar ikon data, tapi memahami perbedaan antara:

```text id="u3t8ii"
Data yang digambar di BPMN
vs
Data yang hidup sebagai process variable
vs
Data authoritative di PostgreSQL
vs
Data kontrak antar task/message/worker
vs
Data audit/regulatory evidence
```

BPMN dibuat sebagai notasi standar yang bisa dipahami business user sekaligus cukup presisi untuk technical implementer; OMG menjelaskan BPMN sebagai bridge antara process design dan process implementation. ([OMG](https://www.omg.org/bpmn/))

---

# 2. Mental Model Utama

Dalam Camunda-oriented implementation:

```text id="4s3ctq"
BPMN data symbol = dokumentasi visual / semantic hint
Process variable = runtime state/context di engine
Domain DB = authoritative business state
Audit table = defensibility/history/evidence
Message payload = integration contract
```

Camunda 8 menjelaskan bahwa process data direpresentasikan sebagai **variables**; variable punya nama dan JSON value, visibility-nya ditentukan oleh variable scope. Camunda juga menegaskan BPMN process tidak punya explicit data model/schema; variable dibuat implicit saat process instance start, worker complete job, message correlated, atau input/output mapping diterapkan. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/data-handling/))

Jadi rule praktisnya:

```text id="neqb6w"
Jangan menganggap BPMN data object = database schema.
Jangan menganggap process variable = source of truth.
Jangan menganggap audit cukup hanya dari engine history.
```

---

# 4. Quick Reference Table

| Item | Kategori | Makna Praktis | Runtime Semantics | Production Concern |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Data Object | BPMN artifact | Dokumen/data yang digunakan atau dihasilkan task | Biasanya visual/documentation | Jangan disamakan dengan DB table |
| Data Store | BPMN artifact | Persistent store seperti database/repository | Biasanya visual/documentation | Detail schema tetap di technical design |
| Text Annotation | BPMN artifact | Catatan penjelas | Tidak mengubah execution | Jangan sembunyikan business rule penting di annotation |
| Group | BPMN artifact | Pengelompokan visual | Tidak mengubah execution | Jangan dipakai sebagai scope runtime |
| Association | Connector artifact | Menghubungkan data/annotation ke task | Tidak sama dengan sequence flow | Jangan dipakai untuk control flow |
| Process Variable | Runtime data | Context/state process instance | Dipakai gateway, worker, message, task | Jangan terlalu besar/sensitif |
| Local Variable | Runtime scoped data | Data terbatas pada task/subprocess/scope | Hilang saat scope selesai kecuali dipropagate | Baik untuk isolasi multi-instance/task context |
| Input Mapping | Variable mapping | Membuat local variable untuk element | Mengontrol input task/subprocess | Cegah worker membaca semua root variables |
| Output Mapping | Variable mapping | Mengontrol variable yang keluar ke parent scope | Mengontrol propagation | Cegah variable leakage/collision |
| Business Key | Domain identity | ID bisnis utama instance | Dipakai traceability | Harus immutable/stabil |
| Correlation Key | Event identity | Key untuk match message ke instance | Message correlation | Harus diketahui external system |
| Idempotency Key | Duplicate protection | Key untuk mencegah side effect ganda | Application/DB concern | Wajib untuk retry/callback/outbox |
| Domain Table | Persistence | Source of truth business state | PostgreSQL/MyBatis | Harus punya constraint/audit |
| Audit Table | Evidence | Jejak decision/action/event | Queryable dan defensible | Jangan hanya bergantung pada log text |

---

# 5. BPMN Data Object

## 5.1 Makna

Data object adalah simbol untuk menunjukkan data/dokumen yang digunakan atau dihasilkan oleh aktivitas.

Contoh:

```text id="yb3o5t"
[Validate Case Data] --- association ---> Data Object: Case Submission
[Review Documents]  --- association ---> Data Object: Evidence Document
[Approve Case]      --- association ---> Data Object: Approval Decision
```

Gunakan data object untuk menjawab:

```text id="s8uo85"
Task ini membaca data apa?
Task ini menghasilkan data apa?
Dokumen/data bisnis apa yang relevan untuk stakeholder?
```

## 5.2 Kapan Dipakai

Data object berguna saat:

```text id="nlvclg"
Dokumen/data penting untuk pemahaman bisnis
Ada input/output formal di task tertentu
Ada evidence yang perlu terlihat di diagram
Ada handoff dokumen antar human task
Ada data hasil decision yang perlu disebut
```

Contoh:

| Task | Data Object |
|---|---|
| Submit Case | Case Submission Form |
| Validate Documents | Supporting Documents |
| Calculate Risk | Risk Feature Set |
| Officer Review | Review Recommendation |
| Register Case | Registration Record |
| Notify Applicant | Notification Letter |

## 5.3 Anti-Pattern

Buruk:

```text id="vvh1gt"
Data Object: case_record table
Data Object: mapper result
Data Object: JSON payload string
Data Object: connection object
```

Kenapa buruk?

```text id="9o4wpy"
BPMN menjadi terlalu teknis.
Data object bukan ERD.
Data object bukan Java DTO diagram.
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="v53bky"
Data Object: Case Submission
Data Object: Risk Assessment Result
Data Object: Review Decision
Data Object: Notification Evidence
```

---

# 6. BPMN Data Store

## 6.1 Makna

Data store menunjukkan persistent storage yang relevan terhadap proses.

Contoh:

```text id="4z2jn8"
[Register Case] --- association ---> Data Store: Case Repository
[Save Audit]    --- association ---> Data Store: Audit Store
[Store Docs]    --- association ---> Data Store: Document Repository
```

Data store biasanya berguna untuk menunjukkan bahwa data bertahan setelah process selesai.

## 6.2 Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan data store untuk:

```text id="cpdgm7"
Case Repository
Document Repository
Audit Store
External Registry
Reporting Warehouse
Notification Outbox
```

## 6.3 Jangan Over-detail

Buruk:

```text id="mho6t7"
Data Store: PostgreSQL table case_record
Data Store: index idx_case_status
Data Store: partition case_record_2026_07
```

Lebih baik di BPMN:

```text id="23wzo6"
Data Store: Case Repository
```

Detail teknis seperti table, index, partition, constraint, dan MyBatis mapper ditempatkan di architecture/technical design, bukan diagram BPMN utama.

---

# 7. Text Annotation

## 7.1 Makna

Text annotation memberi penjelasan tambahan.

Contoh:

```text id="8exfxb"
Annotation:
  SLA: officer review must be completed within 48 hours

Annotation:
  High risk threshold follows Risk Policy v2026.07
```

## 7.2 Kapan Dipakai

Gunakan annotation untuk:

```text id="g028hk"
Penjelasan singkat
Policy reference
SLA note
Assumption
Clarification untuk pembaca
```

## 7.3 Jangan Sembunyikan Rule Penting

Buruk:

```text id="kjyqnt"
Annotation:
  If riskScore >= 80 then send to senior officer,
  otherwise auto approve.
```

Kenapa buruk?

```text id="td02q3"
Business rule tersembunyi.
Tidak executable.
Sulit dites.
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="shej7r"
Business Rule Task: Determine Risk Band
  ↓
Gateway: riskBand?
  ├── HIGH → Senior Officer Review
  └── LOW  → Auto Register
```

Rule:

```text id="u7m33e"
Annotation boleh menjelaskan rule.
Annotation tidak boleh menjadi tempat utama rule.
```

---

# 8. Group

## 8.1 Makna

Group adalah pengelompokan visual. Ia tidak memberi execution scope.

Contoh:

```text id="pe671g"
Group: Validation Phase
  - Validate Payload
  - Check Documents
  - Check Duplicate
```

## 8.2 Group vs Subprocess

| Concern | Group | Subprocess |
|---|---|---|
| Execution scope | Tidak | Ya |
| Boundary event | Tidak | Ya |
| Variable scope | Tidak | Ya |
| Token semantics | Tidak berubah | Ada scope |
| Use case | Visual grouping | Runtime grouping/fase proses |

Rule praktis:

```text id="r677f5"
Kalau hanya ingin highlight visual → group.
Kalau butuh timeout/error/variable scope → subprocess.
```

---

# 9. Association

Association menghubungkan artifact seperti data object, data store, annotation, atau group ke element BPMN.

```text id="vw10y9"
Task --- association ---> Data Object
Task --- association ---> Text Annotation
Task --- association ---> Data Store
```

Association bukan sequence flow.

Buruk:

```text id="drcc3l"
Task A --- association ---> Task B
```

Lebih benar:

```text id="2a72oj"
Task A → sequence flow → Task B
```

---

# 10. Process Variables

## 10.1 Makna

Process variable adalah runtime data yang dibawa process instance. Camunda 8 menjelaskan variable sebagai nama + JSON value, dengan visibility berdasarkan scope. Variables bisa dibuat saat process instance start, worker complete job, message correlated, atau mapping diterapkan. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/data-handling/))

Contoh variable:

```json id="o8r5jw"
{
  "caseId": "CASE-2026-001",
  "caseType": "ENFORCEMENT",
  "submitterId": "USR-100",
  "validationStatus": "VALID",
  "duplicateFound": false,
  "riskScore": 87,
  "riskBand": "HIGH",
  "reviewDecision": "APPROVED"
}
```

## 10.2 Variable Digunakan Untuk

| Use | Contoh |
|---|---|
| Gateway routing | `riskBand = "HIGH"` |
| Worker input | `caseId`, `caseType` |
| Message correlation | `caseId`, `paymentInstructionId` |
| User task form context | `caseSummary`, `riskBand` |
| DMN input | `caseType`, `riskScore`, `duplicateFound` |
| Call activity contract | `applicantId`, `documentSetId` |
| Audit enrichment | `processOutcome`, `decisionCode` |

## 10.3 Variable Bukan Source of Truth

Process variable bagus untuk orchestration context, bukan authoritative record.

```text id="8965ie"
Process variable:
  riskBand = HIGH

Domain DB:
  risk_assessment table records score, band, reason codes, version, timestamp
```

Kenapa?

```text id="any6cq"
Reporting butuh queryable DB.
Audit butuh stable evidence.
Regulatory review butuh decision version/reason.
Process engine history tidak selalu menjadi domain source of truth.
```

---

# 11. Variable Scope

Camunda 8 menjelaskan local variables berguna untuk mengisolasi data pada scope tertentu, misalnya per-instance data dalam multi-instance activity agar menghindari race condition, subprocess-specific data, atau task-specific context; local variables dihapus saat scope keluar kecuali dipropagate lewat output mapping. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/variables/))

## Root Process Variable

```text id="zcq581"
caseId
caseType
riskBand
caseStatus
```

Ini variable yang memang relevan untuk seluruh process.

## Local Variable

```text id="h5xmkx"
documentId pada satu instance document review
currentReviewer pada satu user task
temporaryValidationResult pada subprocess
rawExternalResponse pada receive task
```

## Practical Rule

```text id="1gfste"
Variable yang dipakai banyak element → root process variable.
Variable yang hanya dipakai satu task/subprocess → local variable.
Variable hasil multi-instance → local per instance, lalu aggregate output.
```

---

# 12. Input Mapping

## 12.1 Makna

Input mapping membuat variable lokal untuk element tertentu. Camunda menyatakan input mappings bisa didefinisikan pada service task, script task, business rule task, call activity, user task, send task, dan subprocess; saat diterapkan, input mapping membuat local variable di scope element. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/variables/))

Contoh:

```text id="j3j30a"
Root variable:
  caseId
  applicantId
  caseType
  fullCasePayload
  internalNotes
  sensitiveRawData

Input mapping untuk worker validate-case-data:
  caseId       → caseId
  caseType     → caseType
```

Worker tidak perlu melihat semua variable.

## 12.2 Kenapa Penting

Input mapping membantu:

```text id="h10r3q"
mengurangi coupling worker ke process global
menghindari sensitive data leakage
membuat task contract eksplisit
memudahkan testing
menghindari variable collision
```

## 12.3 Task Input Contract

```text id="s5hk3x"
Task:
  Check Duplicate Case

Input:
  caseId
  subjectId
  caseType

Not needed:
  full applicant payload
  uploaded document content
  internal reviewer notes
```

---

# 13. Output Mapping

## 13.1 Makna

Output mapping mengontrol variable apa yang dipropagate dari element ke parent scope. Camunda menjelaskan output mappings bisa dipakai untuk mengatur bagaimana variables digabung ke process instance; jika output mapping ada, result variables diset sebagai local variables lalu mapping menentukan variable baru yang merge ke parent. Jika tidak ada output mapping, semua result variables digabung ke process instance. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/variables/))

## 13.2 Kenapa Penting

Tanpa output mapping, worker bisa tanpa sengaja mem-publish variable terlalu banyak:

```json id="d1p5v9"
{
  "rawProviderResponse": "...large JSON...",
  "debugTrace": "...",
  "accessToken": "...",
  "riskScore": 87,
  "riskBand": "HIGH"
}
```

Dengan output mapping, hanya yang perlu dipropagate:

```json id="ycx07v"
{
  "riskScore": 87,
  "riskBand": "HIGH",
  "riskAssessmentId": "RA-2026-001"
}
```

## 13.3 Production Rule

```text id="atc8om"
Worker boleh menerima/menghasilkan data besar secara internal,
tetapi process variable harus minimal, stabil, dan relevan untuk orchestration.
```

---

# 14. Variable Naming Convention

## Good

```text id="bhypba"
caseId
caseType
caseStatus
submitterId
riskScore
riskBand
reviewDecision
reviewComment
duplicateFound
externalReferenceNo
paymentInstructionId
registryStatus
documentChecklistStatus
```

## Bad

```text id="57f6og"
id
status
flag
data
result
response
x
payload
case_id
CaseID
risk-score
```

## Rule

```text id="cm69w9"
Gunakan camelCase.
Gunakan nama domain yang eksplisit.
Jangan gunakan nama generik seperti status/result/data tanpa context.
```

Camunda docs juga memberi catatan agar menghindari output mapping atau result variable yang mengandung titik seperti `customer.name`, karena dapat mengubah property dari process variable existing di task scope dan menyebabkan behavior yang membingungkan. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/variables/))

---

# 15. Business Key, Correlation Key, Idempotency Key

## 15.1 Business Key

Business key adalah identity bisnis utama dari process instance.

Contoh:

```text id="wsu55q"
caseId = CASE-2026-001
orderId = ORD-2026-001
applicationNo = APP-2026-001
```

Fungsi:

```text id="e5o40k"
Traceability
Search/debug
Audit
Correlation dengan domain DB
```

## 15.2 Correlation Key

Correlation key adalah key untuk mencocokkan external message ke process instance yang menunggu.

Contoh:

```text id="q5ox9d"
Message:
  RegistryCheckCompleted

Correlation key:
  caseId
```

## 15.3 Idempotency Key

Idempotency key dipakai untuk mencegah duplicate side effect akibat retry/callback ulang.

Contoh:

```text id="qzxj0e"
submit-case:{externalRequestId}
send-notification:{caseId}:{template}:{recipient}
registry-callback:{caseId}:{registryReferenceNo}
register-case:{caseId}
```

## 15.4 Comparison

| Key | Dipakai Untuk | Scope |
|---|---|---|
| Business key | Identitas domain process | Process/domain |
| Correlation key | Message matching | Event/message |
| Idempotency key | Duplicate protection | DB/application side effect |
| External reference | ID dari external system | Integration |
| Process instance key | ID internal engine | Runtime/debug |

Rule:

```text id="zkne5o"
Jangan pakai process instance key sebagai public external correlation contract.
Gunakan business identifier yang stabil.
```

---

# 16. Data Contract per BPMN Element

## 16.1 Process-Level Contract

```text id="26n5jw"
Process:
  case-intake-process

Required start variables:
  caseId: string
  caseType: string
  submitterId: string
  submittedAt: datetime

Produced variables:
  validationStatus: VALID | INVALID | NEEDS_REVIEW
  duplicateFound: boolean
  riskBand: LOW | MEDIUM | HIGH
  reviewDecision: APPROVED | REJECTED | NEEDS_CLARIFICATION
  caseStatus: REGISTERED | REJECTED | CANCELLED
```

## 16.2 Service Task Contract

```text id="ihz3fo"
Task:
  Calculate Risk Features

Input:
  caseId
  caseType
  subjectId

Output:
  riskFeatureSetId
  riskFeatureStatus
```

## 16.3 Business Rule Task Contract

```text id="bn8iqm"
Decision:
  determine-risk-band

Input:
  caseType
  riskFeatureSetId
  duplicateFound
  historicalViolationCount

Output:
  riskBand
  riskScore
  riskReasonCodes
  requiredReviewLevel
```

## 16.4 User Task Contract

```text id="xstd4v"
User task:
  Senior Officer Review

Input:
  caseId
  caseSummary
  riskBand
  riskReasonCodes
  documentChecklistStatus

Output:
  reviewDecision
  reviewComment
  reviewedBy
  reviewedAt
```

## 16.5 Message Contract

```text id="thgr0f"
Message:
  RegistryCheckCompleted

Correlation key:
  caseId

Payload:
  registryStatus
  registryReferenceNo
  checkedAt
```

## 16.6 Call Activity Contract

```text id="5q8026"
Call activity:
  Run Risk Assessment

Input:
  caseId
  applicantId
  caseType

Output:
  riskAssessmentId
  riskScore
  riskBand
  riskReasonCodes
```

---

# 17. Practical Data Architecture Pattern

## Recommended split

```text id="l5ptan"
Process Variable:
  lightweight orchestration state

PostgreSQL Domain Table:
  authoritative business state

PostgreSQL Audit Table:
  decision/action/event history

Outbox Table:
  reliable outbound integration

Callback Log:
  inbound message idempotency/audit

Document Store:
  large documents / binary payloads

Object Storage:
  attachments, evidence files, generated letters
```

## Example

```text id="c1y83l"
Process variable:
  caseId = CASE-2026-001
  riskBand = HIGH
  reviewDecision = APPROVED

case_record:
  full case status, owner, timestamps

risk_assessment:
  score, band, reason codes, model/rule version

case_review:
  reviewer, decision, comment, timestamp

external_callback_log:
  registry callback raw payload and correlation status

integration_outbox:
  notification/event to publish
```

---

# 18. PostgreSQL Schema Patterns

## 18.1 Case Record

```sql id="ai6siz"
CREATE TABLE case_record (
    case_id VARCHAR(64) PRIMARY KEY,
    case_type VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    submitter_id VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    case_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    submitted_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    registered_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    closed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

## 18.2 Risk Assessment

```sql id="xem50h"
CREATE TABLE risk_assessment (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES case_record(case_id),
    assessment_version VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    risk_score NUMERIC(6,2) NOT NULL,
    risk_band VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL,
    reason_codes JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
    assessed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT uq_risk_assessment_case_version
        UNIQUE (case_id, assessment_version)
);
```

## 18.3 Case Review

```sql id="4ktco0"
CREATE TABLE case_review (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES case_record(case_id),
    task_name VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    reviewer_id VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    decision VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    comment TEXT,
    reviewed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT uq_case_review_task
        UNIQUE (case_id, task_name)
);
```

## 18.4 External Callback Log

```sql id="c32t00"
CREATE TABLE external_callback_log (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    message_name VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    correlation_key VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    external_reference_no VARCHAR(128),
    payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    correlation_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    correlated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    late_callback BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    error_message TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT uq_external_callback
        UNIQUE (message_name, correlation_key, external_reference_no)
);
```

## 18.5 Outbox

```sql id="jr1y3f"
CREATE TABLE integration_outbox (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    aggregate_type VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    event_type VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(256) NOT NULL,
    payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    published_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    error_message TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT uq_integration_outbox_idempotency
        UNIQUE (idempotency_key)
);
```

---

# 19. MyBatis Mapper Patterns

## 19.1 Idempotent Case Create

```xml id="0ax42z"
<insert id="insertCaseIfAbsent">
  INSERT INTO case_record (
      case_id,
      case_type,
      submitter_id,
      case_status,
      submitted_at
  )
  VALUES (
      #{caseId},
      #{caseType},
      #{submitterId},
      'SUBMITTED',
      #{submittedAt}
  )
  ON CONFLICT (case_id)
  DO NOTHING
</insert>
```

## 19.2 Update Case Status with Guard

```xml id="0u9cc5"
<update id="updateCaseStatus">
  UPDATE case_record
  SET
      case_status = #{newStatus},
      updated_at = now(),
      version = version + 1
  WHERE case_id = #{caseId}
    AND case_status = #{expectedStatus}
</update>
```

Practical meaning:

```text id="rregxo"
State transition harus guarded.
Jangan update status tanpa expected current state.
```

## 19.3 Upsert Risk Assessment

```xml id="1tfqzo"
<insert id="upsertRiskAssessment">
  INSERT INTO risk_assessment (
      case_id,
      assessment_version,
      risk_score,
      risk_band,
      reason_codes
  )
  VALUES (
      #{caseId},
      #{assessmentVersion},
      #{riskScore},
      #{riskBand},
      CAST(#{reasonCodesJson} AS jsonb)
  )
  ON CONFLICT (case_id, assessment_version)
  DO UPDATE SET
      risk_score = EXCLUDED.risk_score,
      risk_band = EXCLUDED.risk_band,
      reason_codes = EXCLUDED.reason_codes,
      assessed_at = now()
</insert>
```

## 19.4 Insert Review Result

```xml id="sj9b1a"
<insert id="insertReviewResult">
  INSERT INTO case_review (
      case_id,
      task_name,
      reviewer_id,
      decision,
      comment
  )
  VALUES (
      #{caseId},
      #{taskName},
      #{reviewerId},
      #{decision},
      #{comment}
  )
  ON CONFLICT (case_id, task_name)
  DO UPDATE SET
      reviewer_id = EXCLUDED.reviewer_id,
      decision = EXCLUDED.decision,
      comment = EXCLUDED.comment,
      reviewed_at = now()
</insert>
```

## 19.5 Insert Outbox Event

```xml id="6pg6wf"
<insert id="insertOutboxEvent">
  INSERT INTO integration_outbox (
      aggregate_type,
      aggregate_id,
      event_type,
      idempotency_key,
      payload
  )
  VALUES (
      #{aggregateType},
      #{aggregateId},
      #{eventType},
      #{idempotencyKey},
      CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb)
  )
  ON CONFLICT (idempotency_key)
  DO NOTHING
</insert>
```

---

# 20. Worker Data Handling Pattern

## Bad Pattern

```java id="o02tc3"
public void handle(JobClient client, ActivatedJob job) {
    Map<String, Object> vars = job.getVariablesAsMap();

    // Reads everything, trusts everything, logs everything.
    log.info("Variables: {}", vars);

    // Updates process variable only, no domain persistence.
    client.newCompleteCommand(job.getKey())
            .variables(Map.of("caseStatus", "REGISTERED"))
            .send()
            .join();
}
```

Masalah:

```text id="po4cp3"
Sensitive data bisa masuk log.
Worker terlalu coupling ke variable global.
Domain DB tidak update.
Audit lemah.
```

## Better Pattern

```java id="b0uex4"
public final class RegisterCaseWorker {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final OutboxRepository outboxRepository;

    public void handle(JobClient client, ActivatedJob job) {
        String caseId = (String) job.getVariablesAsMap().get("caseId");

        try {
            caseRepository.markRegistered(caseId);

            outboxRepository.insertIfAbsent(
                    "CASE",
                    caseId,
                    "CaseRegistered",
                    "case-registered:" + caseId,
                    """
                    {
                      "caseId": "%s",
                      "eventType": "CaseRegistered"
                    }
                    """.formatted(caseId)
            );

            client.newCompleteCommand(job.getKey())
                    .variables(Map.of(
                            "caseStatus", "REGISTERED",
                            "caseRegistered", true
                    ))
                    .send()
                    .join();

        } catch (Exception ex) {
            client.newFailCommand(job.getKey())
                    .retries(Math.max(job.getRetries() - 1, 0))
                    .errorMessage(ex.getMessage())
                    .send()
                    .join();
        }
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="mygdko"
Persist authoritative state first.
Then complete job with minimal process variables.
```

---

# 21. User Task Form Data Pattern

## User Task Input

```json id="j7kbz9"
{
  "caseId": "CASE-2026-001",
  "caseSummary": {
    "caseType": "ENFORCEMENT",
    "riskBand": "HIGH",
    "submittedAt": "2026-07-16T09:00:00+07:00"
  },
  "documentChecklistStatus": "COMPLETE"
}
```

## User Task Output

```json id="hq8n3m"
{
  "reviewDecision": "APPROVED",
  "reviewComment": "Sufficient evidence",
  "reviewedBy": "officer-123",
  "reviewedAt": "2026-07-16T10:30:00+07:00"
}
```

## Domain Persistence

```sql id="e9g9mg"
INSERT INTO case_review (
    case_id,
    task_name,
    reviewer_id,
    decision,
    comment,
    reviewed_at
)
VALUES (
    #{caseId},
    #{taskName},
    #{reviewerId},
    #{decision},
    #{comment},
    #{reviewedAt}
);
```

Rule:

```text id="xwh9mq"
Complete user task bukan satu-satunya audit.
Persist review decision ke domain/audit table.
```

---

# 22. Message Payload Pattern

## Good Message Payload

```json id="arombu"
{
  "caseId": "CASE-2026-001",
  "registryReferenceNo": "REG-9981",
  "registryStatus": "MATCHED",
  "checkedAt": "2026-07-16T09:30:00+07:00"
}
```

## Bad Message Payload

```json id="07z44d"
{
  "case": {
    "allPersonalData": "...",
    "allDocuments": "...",
    "debug": "...",
    "accessToken": "..."
  }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="b8u7f0"
Message payload should be minimal, authenticated, validated, idempotent, and auditable.
```

---

# 23. Process Variable Size & Payload Minimization

Camunda variables are JSON values, but that does not mean every payload should be stored in process variables. Camunda’s variable docs explicitly include a “variable size limitation” topic and model data as JSON values with scoping rules, so production design should keep variables lightweight and put large/domain-authoritative data in external stores. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/variables/))

## Keep in process variable

```text id="g7kw7t"
caseId
caseStatus
riskBand
reviewDecision
documentChecklistStatus
externalReferenceNo
```

## Keep in PostgreSQL / object store

```text id="68kyea"
full documents
large raw API responses
PII-heavy payloads
binary files
full audit trails
external callback raw payload
generated letters
```

## Store reference instead

```json id="n3ks29"
{
  "caseId": "CASE-2026-001",
  "documentSetId": "DOCSET-123",
  "riskAssessmentId": "RA-456",
  "callbackLogId": 991
}
```

---

# 24. Data Object vs Process Variable vs DB Table

| Concern | Data Object | Process Variable | PostgreSQL Table |
|---|---|---|---|
| Purpose | Visual/documentation | Runtime orchestration | Authoritative persistence |
| Schema | Informal | JSON variable contract | DDL constraints |
| Query/reporting | Tidak | Terbatas | Kuat |
| Audit defensibility | Rendah | Bergantung engine/history | Tinggi jika dirancang |
| Sensitive data control | Visual only | Harus diminimalkan | Policy/ACL/encryption |
| Versioning | Diagram version | Process definition/version | Migration/schema version |
| Example | “Review Decision” | `reviewDecision` | `case_review` |

Rule:

```text id="a5xx2x"
Data object tells readers what data matters.
Process variable lets engine route/continue.
DB table proves what happened.
```

---

# 25. Data Contract Catalog

Untuk production, buat catalog variable/data contract per process.

```text id="gu1g13"
Process: case-intake-process
Version: 2026.07
```

| Variable | Type | Scope | Producer | Consumer | Required? | Sensitive? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---:|---:|
| `caseId` | string | root | API start | all tasks/messages | Yes | No |
| `caseType` | string | root | API start | validation/risk | Yes | No |
| `submitterId` | string | root | API start | notification | Yes | Yes-ish |
| `validationStatus` | enum | root | validate-case-data | gateway | Yes | No |
| `duplicateFound` | boolean | root | check-duplicate-case | gateway/risk | Yes | No |
| `riskBand` | enum | root | determine-risk-band | gateway/review | Yes | No |
| `reviewDecision` | enum | root | user task | gateway/register/reject | Conditional | No |
| `registryStatus` | enum | root | message callback | gateway | Conditional | No |
| `rawRegistryPayload` | JSON | DB only | callback endpoint | audit/replay | No process var | Sensitive |

---

# 26. Versioning & Migration

Variable contract adalah API internal antar BPMN element.

Breaking changes:

```text id="bthgna"
rename caseId → case_id
change riskBand values HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW → H/M/L
remove reviewDecision
change boolean duplicateFound → duplicateStatus enum
change message payload field registryStatus → status
```

Non-breaking changes:

```text id="9vmi2d"
add optional variable
add new output field not consumed by old gateways
add new DB column nullable/defaulted
add new enum value only if gateway default handles it
```

Rule:

```text id="vxnoei"
Treat process variables and message payloads like API contracts.
Version them intentionally.
```

---

# 27. Security & Compliance

## Sensitive Data Rule

Jangan taruh data sensitif lengkap di process variable jika tidak perlu.

```text id="malwjo"
Avoid:
  full identity number
  access token
  full document content
  raw external API payload with PII
  secrets
  credentials
```

Better:

```text id="wv1esb"
documentId
subjectRef
maskedIdentifier
callbackLogId
riskAssessmentId
storageRef
```

## Logging Rule

Buruk:

```java id="cn7jbq"
log.info("Job variables: {}", job.getVariables());
```

Lebih baik:

```java id="u1zy9z"
log.info(
    "Handling jobType={}, caseId={}, processInstanceKey={}, elementId={}",
    job.getType(),
    job.getVariablesAsMap().get("caseId"),
    job.getProcessInstanceKey(),
    job.getElementId()
);
```

---

# 28. Observability Data

Minimal fields untuk log/metric/tracing:

```text id="uijmu2"
caseId
processInstanceKey
bpmnProcessId
processDefinitionVersion
elementId
elementName
jobKey
jobType
messageName
correlationKey
businessOutcome
errorCode
escalationCode
```

## Structured Log Example

```json id="xz1gpa"
{
  "event": "workflow_task_completed",
  "caseId": "CASE-2026-001",
  "bpmnProcessId": "case-intake-process",
  "processInstanceKey": "2251799813685251",
  "elementId": "calculate-risk-score",
  "jobType": "calculate-risk-score",
  "riskBand": "HIGH",
  "durationMs": 245
}
```

# 29. Practical Modeling Example

## BPMN

```text id="hsagyk"
Start: Case Submitted
  ↓
Data Object: Case Submission
  ↓
Validate Case Data
  ↓
Data Object: Validation Result
  ↓
Check Duplicate Case
  ↓
Determine Risk Band
  ↓
Data Object: Risk Assessment Result
  ↓
Gateway: riskBand?
  ├── HIGH → Senior Officer Review
  │             ↓
  │          Data Object: Review Decision
  │
  └── LOW  → Auto Register Case
  ↓
Register Case
  ↓
Data Store: Case Repository
  ↓
End: Case Registered
```

## Runtime Variables

```json id="o3z554"
{
  "caseId": "CASE-2026-001",
  "validationStatus": "VALID",
  "duplicateFound": false,
  "riskBand": "HIGH",
  "reviewDecision": "APPROVED",
  "caseStatus": "REGISTERED"
}
```

## PostgreSQL State

```text id="3d8o3y"
case_record:
  CASE-2026-001, REGISTERED

risk_assessment:
  CASE-2026-001, score 87, band HIGH, reason codes [...]

case_review:
  CASE-2026-001, APPROVED, officer-123

integration_outbox:
  CaseRegistered event pending/published
```

---

# 30. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Gejala | Dampak | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Process variable jadi database | Semua data disimpan di variables | Query/audit/security buruk | DB authoritative + variable minimal |
| Data object dianggap executable | Menggambar data object lalu berharap engine enforce schema | False confidence | Contract + validation + DB schema |
| Annotation menyimpan rule utama | Rule bisnis tersembunyi di note | Tidak testable | DMN/gateway/task eksplisit |
| Worker membaca semua variables | Coupling tinggi | Fragile + data leakage | Input mapping |
| Worker output semua result | Variable bloat | Sensitive/large data leak | Output mapping |
| Variable name generik | `status`, `result`, `data` | Ambiguity | Domain-specific names |
| Correlation key tidak stabil | pakai email/name | Wrong correlation | Use `caseId/orderId` |
| Callback tidak dipersist | Correlation gagal → data hilang | No recovery | callback log |
| Review hanya di Camunda task history | Domain audit kurang | Reporting sulit | `case_review` table |
| Outbound event langsung publish tanpa outbox | DB commit sukses, event gagal | Inconsistent integration | outbox pattern |

---

# 31. Production Checklist

Untuk setiap proses BPMN, cek:

```text id="yxbqbu"
1. Apa business key utama?
2. Apa correlation key untuk setiap message?
3. Apa variable root yang wajib?
4. Apa variable local per task/subprocess?
5. Apakah input mapping membatasi data task?
6. Apakah output mapping membatasi variable leakage?
7. Apa authoritative DB table untuk setiap business state?
8. Apa audit table untuk setiap decision penting?
9. Apakah raw callback payload disimpan?
10. Apakah outbound integration memakai outbox?
11. Apakah large/sensitive payload tidak masuk process variable?
12. Apakah variable naming konsisten?
13. Apakah variable contract versioned?
14. Apakah gateway membaca variable yang pasti tersedia?
15. Apakah user task decision dipersist sebelum complete?
16. Apakah process end state cocok dengan domain state?
17. Apakah observability field cukup?
18. Apakah retention/security policy jelas?
```

---

# 32. Mini Exercise

Ambil satu BPMN di kantor, lalu buat tabel ini:

| BPMN Element | Input Data | Output Data | Process Variable | DB Table | Audit Needed? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Start Case | Submit request | `caseId` | `caseId`, `caseType` | `case_record` | Yes |
| Validate Case | `caseId` | validation result | `validationStatus` | `case_validation` | Yes |
| Check Duplicate | `caseId`, `subjectId` | duplicate flag | `duplicateFound` | `duplicate_check` | Yes |
| Determine Risk | risk features | risk band | `riskBand` | `risk_assessment` | Yes |
| Review Case | review context | decision | `reviewDecision` | `case_review` | Yes |
| Register Case | approved decision | registered status | `caseStatus` | `case_record` | Yes |
| Notify Applicant | case status | notification ref | `notificationStatus` | `integration_outbox` | Yes |

Kalau ada process variable yang tidak punya owner/producer/consumer jelas, variable itu kandidat untuk dibersihkan.

---

# 33. Ringkasan Seri 10

Inti praktis:

```text id="vwc7j0"
Data Object      = data/dokumen yang penting secara visual
Data Store       = persistent storage secara konseptual
Annotation       = penjelasan, bukan rule utama
Group            = visual grouping, bukan runtime scope
Process Variable = runtime orchestration state
Local Variable   = scoped task/subprocess data
Input Mapping    = contract masuk element
Output Mapping   = contract keluar element
Domain DB        = authoritative business state
Audit Table      = evidence/defensibility
```

Rule paling penting:

```text id="ic0lu7"
BPMN menggambarkan data yang penting.
Camunda variables membawa context runtime.
PostgreSQL menyimpan kebenaran bisnis.
Audit tables membuktikan keputusan dan kejadian.
```

Untuk production Camunda/Jersey/MyBatis/PostgreSQL:

```text id="1d5gkq"
Keep process variables small.
Use explicit input/output mappings.
Persist domain state before completing jobs/tasks.
Store callbacks before correlation.
Use outbox for outbound events.
Use idempotency keys for retries.
Treat variable/message contracts like APIs.
```
