7/16/2026, 10:00:05 AM

Lanjut ke **Seri 11/12 — Camunda Execution Mapping**. Saya akan susun dari sudut runtime: process definition/instance, job worker, user task, message correlation, timer, incident, retry, deployment, dan versioning.

# Seri 11/12 - Camunda Execution Mapping

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri ini memetakan simbol BPMN ke runtime Camunda secara praktis:

```text id="fsqi5u"
BPMN model
→ deployment
→ process definition
→ process instance
→ token flow
→ job
→ job worker
→ user task
→ message subscription
→ timer
→ incident
→ retry
→ versioning
→ operational runbook
```

Fokusnya: **bagaimana diagram BPMN benar-benar hidup di runtime**, khususnya untuk arsitektur Java/Jersey/MyBatis/PostgreSQL.

Untuk Camunda 8 modern, Camunda Java Client adalah official Java library untuk integrasi aplikasi dengan Camunda 8, termasuk deploy process/decision, start/manage process, implementasi job worker, dan query/manage process data. Dokumentasi Camunda 8.9 juga mencatat bahwa Camunda Java Client menggantikan Zeebe Java Client sejak 8.8, memakai REST sebagai default protocol dengan gRPC configurable, dan Zeebe Java Client direncanakan dihapus pada 8.10. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/apis-tools/java-client/getting-started/))

---

# 2. Runtime Mental Model

```text id="oaov5m"
BPMN XML
  ↓ deploy
Process Definition
  ↓ start
Process Instance
  ↓ token moves
BPMN Element Instance
  ↓
Task / Event / Gateway
  ↓
Job / User Task / Message Subscription / Timer / Incident
```

## Mapping singkat

| BPMN | Runtime Camunda | Java/Jersey Mapping | PostgreSQL/MyBatis Mapping |
|---|---|---|---|
| Process model | Process definition | deployment artifact | release metadata |
| Start event | Process instance start | Jersey endpoint / message / timer | idempotent domain create |
| Service task | Job | Java job worker | domain transaction |
| User task | Human task lifecycle | Task API/custom UI | review/approval table |
| Message catch | Message subscription | callback endpoint publishes/correlates message | callback log |
| Timer event | Scheduled wait state | engine-managed timer | SLA/audit table |
| Gateway | Routing decision | variable expression | decision result persisted |
| Error event | Business exception path | throw BPMN error | exception audit |
| Incident | Runtime failure | Operate/API runbook | operational incident log |
| End event | Path/process completion | complete state / event publish | final domain status |

---

# 3. Diagram Execution Mapping

```text id="7p8dtk"
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ BPMN File: case-intake.bpmn                  │
└─────────────────────┬────────────────────────┘
                      │ deploy
                      ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Process Definition                           │
│ bpmnProcessId = case-intake-process          │
│ version       = 2026.07                      │
└─────────────────────┬────────────────────────┘
                      │ start with variables
                      ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Process Instance                             │
│ businessKey / caseId = CASE-2026-001         │
│ variables: caseId, caseType, riskBand, ...   │
└─────────────────────┬────────────────────────┘
                      │ token flow
                      ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ BPMN Elements                                │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Service Task  → Job → Java Worker            │
│ User Task     → Human Task → UI/API          │
│ Message Event → Subscription → Callback      │
│ Timer Event   → Scheduled wait               │
│ Gateway       → FEEL condition               │
│ Error         → Boundary/Error handler       │
│ Incident      → Operate/API recovery         │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

# 4. Process Definition vs Process Instance

## Process Definition

Process definition adalah blueprint hasil deploy BPMN.

```text id="58oibs"
case-intake-process v1
case-intake-process v2
case-intake-process v3
```

Ia menjawab:

```text id="a8u4jb"
Model proses apa yang tersedia?
Versi mana yang dideploy?
Task type apa yang dipakai?
Message apa yang ditunggu?
Variable contract apa yang diasumsikan?
```

## Process Instance

Process instance adalah satu kasus berjalan.

```text id="v4o0ib"
CASE-2026-001 → process instance A
CASE-2026-002 → process instance B
CASE-2026-003 → process instance C
```

Ia menjawab:

```text id="2ehp7n"
Kasus ini sedang di step mana?
Variable saat ini apa?
Task aktif apa?
Menunggu message/timer apa?
Ada incident atau tidak?
```

---

# 5. Start Process dari Jersey

## Pattern

```text id="y21kef"
POST /cases
  ↓
validate API request
  ↓
create domain case record
  ↓
start Camunda process instance
  ↓
return 202 Accepted / 201 Created
```

## Java/Jersey Resource

```java id="obqvix"
@Path("/cases")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CaseResource {

    private final CaseApplicationService caseApplicationService;

    @POST
    public Response submitCase(SubmitCaseRequest request) {
        SubmitCaseResult result = caseApplicationService.submitCase(request);

        return Response.accepted(Map.of(
                "caseId", result.caseId(),
                "processInstanceKey", result.processInstanceKey()
        )).build();
    }
}
```

## Application Service

```java id="68ghir"
public final class CaseApplicationService {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final WorkflowProcessService workflowProcessService;

    public SubmitCaseResult submitCase(SubmitCaseRequest request) {
        String caseId = caseRepository.createCaseIfAbsent(
                request.idempotencyKey(),
                request.caseType(),
                request.submitterId(),
                request.submittedAt()
        );

        long processInstanceKey = workflowProcessService.startProcess(
                "case-intake-process",
                caseId,
                Map.of(
                        "caseId", caseId,
                        "caseType", request.caseType(),
                        "submitterId", request.submitterId(),
                        "submittedAt", request.submittedAt().toString()
                )
        );

        return new SubmitCaseResult(caseId, processInstanceKey);
    }
}
```

## Rule penting

```text id="21rrin"
Persist domain aggregate first.
Start workflow second.
Return business key to caller.
```

Kenapa?

```text id="ybaw81"
Kalau process start sukses tapi domain case belum ada,
worker berikutnya bisa gagal karena caseId tidak ditemukan.

Kalau domain case sudah ada tapi process start gagal,
request bisa di-retry secara idempotent.
```

```sql id="9vr1zc"
CREATE TABLE workflow_release (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    release_version VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    bpmn_process_id VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    process_version_tag VARCHAR(64),

    )
```

# 7. Service Task → Job → Job Worker

Camunda 8 task overview menjelaskan bahwa task adalah unit kerja atomik; ketika token mencapai task, token berhenti, Zeebe membuat job, worker menjalankan pekerjaan, lalu setelah handler memberi sinyal completion, token melanjutkan outgoing sequence flow. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/tasks/))

## Runtime flow

```text id="myksg2"
Token reaches service task
  ↓
Camunda creates job
  ↓
Job has type, key, variables, headers
  ↓
Worker polls/activates job by type
  ↓
Worker executes business logic
  ↓
Worker completes/fails/throws BPMN error
  ↓
Token continues / retries / incident / boundary error path
```

Job worker punya `type`, custom headers, key, dan variables. Dokumentasi Camunda juga mencatat job type case-sensitive dan menyarankan menjaga job type tetap dalam limit 256 karakter jika memakai RDBMS-backed secondary storage atau mungkin migrasi ke sana. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/job-workers/))

---

## Service Task Contract

Camunda service task membutuhkan `taskDefinition`; property `type` dipakai sebagai referensi worker yang mengambil job, sedangkan `retries` menentukan jumlah retry saat worker memberi sinyal failure. Default retries adalah tiga. Service task juga dapat memiliki task headers dan input/output variable mappings. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/service-tasks/))

```text id="3674fx"
BPMN element:
  Validate Case Data

Task definition:
  type    = validate-case-data
  retries = 3

Input:
  caseId
  caseType
  submitterId

Output:
  validationStatus
  validationErrorCount
```

---

## Java Worker Skeleton

```java id="ij8t0r"
import io.camunda.client.CamundaClient;
import io.camunda.client.api.response.ActivatedJob;
import io.camunda.client.api.worker.JobClient;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.Map;

public final class ValidateCaseDataWorker {

    private final CaseRepository caseRepository;
    private final CaseValidationService validationService;

    public ValidateCaseDataWorker(
            CaseRepository caseRepository,
            CaseValidationService validationService
    ) {
        this.caseRepository = caseRepository;
        this.validationService = validationService;
    }

    public void register(CamundaClient client) {
        client.newWorker()
                .jobType("validate-case-data")
                .handler(this::handle)
                .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(30))
                .name("validate-case-data-worker")
                .open();
    }

    private void handle(JobClient client, ActivatedJob job) {
        Map<String, Object> variables = job.getVariablesAsMap();
        String caseId = (String) variables.get("caseId");

        try {
            CaseRecord caseRecord = caseRepository.findByCaseId(caseId)
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException("Case not found: " + caseId));

            ValidationResult result = validationService.validate(caseRecord);

            caseRepository.saveValidationResult(caseId, result);

            client.newCompleteCommand(job.getKey())
                    .variables(Map.of(
                            "validationStatus", result.status(),
                            "validationErrorCount", result.errors().size()
                    ))
                    .send()
                    .join();

        } catch (RecoverableTechnicalException ex) {
            client.newFailCommand(job.getKey())
                    .retries(Math.max(job.getRetries() - 1, 0))
                    .errorMessage(ex.getMessage())
                    .send()
                    .join();

        } catch (Exception ex) {
            client.newFailCommand(job.getKey())
                    .retries(0)
                    .errorMessage(ex.getMessage())
                    .send()
                    .join();
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 8. Worker Failure Decision

```text id="fem9ht"
Worker problem:
│
├── Business result expected?
│   └── complete job with variable
│
├── Business exception?
│   └── throw BPMN error
│
├── Technical transient?
│   └── fail job with retries > 0
│
├── Technical permanent?
│   └── fail job with retries = 0 → incident
│
└── Unknown/unclassified?
    └── fail job with limited retries + alert
```

## Mapping table

| Case | Worker Action | BPMN Result |
|---|---|---|
| Case invalid | complete job with `validationStatus=INVALID` | gateway routes to reject/manual review |
| Identity not resolvable | throw BPMN error `IDENTITY_NOT_RESOLVABLE` | error boundary path |
| PostgreSQL timeout | fail job, retries left | retry |
| External API 503 | fail job, retries left | retry |
| Bad variable shape | fail job, retries 0 or incident | incident |
| Duplicate side effect detected | complete idempotently | token continues |

---

# 9. Retry & Incident Mapping

Incidents di Camunda dibuat dalam beberapa situasi, antara lain job failed tanpa retries tersisa, condition tidak menghasilkan boolean, timer expression salah tipe, decision tidak bisa dievaluasi, atau BPMN error dilempar tetapi tidak ditangkap boundary/error event subprocess. Untuk resolusi job incident, dokumentasi menyarankan mengidentifikasi dan memperbaiki masalah, menaikkan retries bila perlu, lalu resolve incident sehingga job dapat diaktifkan worker lagi. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/incidents/))

## Incident lifecycle

```text id="0kho6o"
Job fails
  ↓
retries > 0?
  ├── yes → job retried
  └── no  → incident created
              ↓
          operator fixes root cause
              ↓
          variables/retries updated if needed
              ↓
          incident resolved
              ↓
          job can be activated again
```

## Incident runbook template

```text id="6fx5o6"
Incident:
  processInstanceKey:
  caseId:
  elementId:
  jobType:
  errorMessage:
  retries:
  firstSeenAt:

Diagnosis:
  variable issue?
  external dependency down?
  database error?
  bad deployment/version mismatch?
  non-idempotent side effect?

Recovery:
  update variable?
  fix downstream service?
  increase retries?
  manually compensate?
  terminate/migrate instance?

Post-recovery:
  add test?
  adjust retry?
  add validation?
  improve alert?
```

---

# 10. User Task Runtime Mapping

User task adalah pekerjaan manusia. Dalam implementasi application/UI, lifecycle perlu didefinisikan sebelum logic/UI dibuat. Camunda task lifecycle docs memberi lifecycle dasar: user bisa assigned/assign self, start working, complete task, dan jika aplikasi mendukung interrupted work, draft/intermediate data perlu dipersist sebelum user meninggalkan form. Dokumen yang sama juga menyebut engine derives task state via CQRS/event stream, bukan single `status` attribute pada task. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/apis-tools/frontend-development/task-applications/user-task-lifecycle/))

## User task practical lifecycle

```text id="sdowvy"
created
  ↓
available / candidate group
  ↓
claimed / assigned
  ↓
started
  ↓
draft saved
  ↓
completed
  ↓
process continues
```

## Enterprise mapping

| Lifecycle | UI/API | PostgreSQL |
|---|---|---|
| Available | show in queue | optional task projection |
| Claimed | assign to user | task assignment audit |
| Started | user opens form | task work session |
| Draft saved | save partial input | draft table |
| Completed | complete user task | review/decision table |
| Cancelled/escalated | task no longer active | SLA/escalation audit |

---

## Custom UI completion pattern

```text id="0lzd57"
Vue UI
  ↓
Jersey API
  ↓
Authorization check
  ↓
Persist review decision
  ↓
Complete Camunda user task
  ↓
Process continues
```

```java id="618xxw"
@Path("/cases/{caseId}/reviews")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CaseReviewResource {

    private final CaseReviewApplicationService reviewService;

    @POST
    public Response completeReview(
            @PathParam("caseId") String caseId,
            CompleteReviewRequest request
    ) {
        CompleteReviewResult result = reviewService.completeReview(caseId, request);

        return Response.ok(Map.of(
                "caseId", caseId,
                "reviewDecision", result.reviewDecision(),
                "completed", true
        )).build();
    }
}
```

```java id="88fwmu"
public final class CaseReviewApplicationService {

    private final CaseReviewRepository reviewRepository;
    private final WorkflowTaskService workflowTaskService;
    private final AuthorizationService authorizationService;

    public CompleteReviewResult completeReview(
            String caseId,
            CompleteReviewRequest request
    ) {
        authorizationService.assertCanReview(caseId, request.reviewerId());

        ActiveUserTask task = workflowTaskService.findActiveTaskByCaseIdAndTaskName(
                caseId,
                "Senior Officer Review"
        );

        reviewRepository.upsertReviewDecision(
                caseId,
                "Senior Officer Review",
                request.reviewerId(),
                request.decision(),
                request.comment()
        );

        workflowTaskService.completeTask(
                task.taskKey(),
                Map.of(
                        "reviewDecision", request.decision(),
                        "reviewComment", request.comment(),
                        "reviewedBy", request.reviewerId(),
                        "reviewedAt", request.reviewedAt().toString()
                )
        );

        return new CompleteReviewResult(request.decision());
    }
}
```

## Rule penting

```text id="0qcrep"
Persist business decision before completing user task.
```

Jika task completion berhasil tetapi review decision tidak tersimpan di domain DB, audit menjadi lemah.

---

# 11. Camunda User Task vs Job Worker-Based User Task

Untuk Camunda 8, hati-hati membedakan Camunda user tasks dengan user tasks yang diimplementasikan memakai job workers. Dokumentasi Camunda mencatat job worker-based user tasks punya limitasi: tidak dapat dikelola dengan Orchestration Cluster REST API untuk user task, engine tidak punya visibility lifecycle/state management seperti Camunda user tasks, reporting user-task-specific berkurang, dan assignment/scheduling harus ditangani custom. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/user-tasks/))

## Practical recommendation

```text id="aln9kw"
Untuk enterprise case management:
  prefer Camunda user task / task APIs
  kecuali benar-benar butuh lifecycle custom ekstrem.
```

Kenapa?

```text id="yy45px"
Lebih baik untuk assignment.
Lebih baik untuk metrics/reporting.
Lebih baik untuk task lifecycle governance.
Lebih sedikit custom state machine yang harus kamu rawat sendiri.
```

---

# 12. Message Correlation Mapping

Message event dipakai untuk komunikasi 1:1: satu process instance menunggu message dari external system/secondary process. Dokumentasi Camunda message event menyatakan message tidak boleh punya lebih dari satu recipient dan cocok untuk single sender to single recipient relationship. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/message-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Runtime flow

```text id="nwvdpi"
Process enters message catch event
  ↓
Message subscription opened
  ↓
External callback arrives
  ↓
Application persists callback
  ↓
Application publishes/correlates message
  ↓
Message name + correlation key match
  ↓
Process continues
```

Camunda message concepts menjelaskan pattern request-reply dengan unique correlation key: service task mengirim outbound message dan menghasilkan unique key, external system menerima key itu, lalu reply mengandung key yang sama sehingga message catch event dapat menerima reply untuk instance yang benar. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/messages/))

---

## Callback endpoint

```java id="bdih0l"
@Path("/external/registry/callbacks")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class RegistryCallbackResource {

    private final RegistryCallbackApplicationService service;

    @POST
    public Response receive(RegistryCallbackRequest request) {
        service.handle(request);

        return Response.accepted(Map.of(
                "caseId", request.caseId(),
                "accepted", true
        )).build();
    }
}
```

```java id="0oy6h5"
public final class RegistryCallbackApplicationService {

    private final RegistryCallbackRepository callbackRepository;
    private final WorkflowMessageService workflowMessageService;

    public void handle(RegistryCallbackRequest request) {
        callbackRepository.upsertCallback(
                "RegistryCheckCompleted",
                request.caseId(),
                request.registryReferenceNo(),
                request.registryStatus(),
                request.rawPayload()
        );

        workflowMessageService.publishMessage(
                "RegistryCheckCompleted",
                request.caseId(),
                Map.of(
                        "registryStatus", request.registryStatus(),
                        "registryReferenceNo", request.registryReferenceNo(),
                        "checkedAt", request.checkedAt().toString()
                )
        );

        callbackRepository.markCorrelationAttempted(
                "RegistryCheckCompleted",
                request.caseId(),
                request.registryReferenceNo()
        );
    }
}
```

Camunda Orchestration Cluster REST API `POST /messages/publication` publishes a single message; messages are published to partitions computed from correlation keys and can be buffered. The endpoint does not wait for a correlation result; Camunda says to use the message correlation endpoint when that behavior is needed. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/apis-tools/orchestration-cluster-api-rest/specifications/publish-message/))

---

# 13. Timer Runtime Mapping

## BPMN

```text id="7a7030"
User Task: Review Case
  boundary timer PT48H
    ↓
  Escalate Review Timeout
```

## Runtime

```text id="x4dhbq"
Token enters user task
  ↓
timer subscription/schedule created
  ↓
if user completes before timer:
  timer is no longer relevant
  process continues normal path

if timer fires before completion:
  boundary path runs
  interrupting timer cancels task
  non-interrupting timer runs side-flow
```

## PostgreSQL SLA log

```sql id="xrc4h9"
CREATE TABLE case_task_sla_event (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    task_name VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    sla_event_type VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    event_level VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    triggered_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    payload JSONB,
    CONSTRAINT uq_case_task_sla_event UNIQUE (
        case_id,
        task_name,
        sla_event_type
    )
);
```

---

# 14. Gateway Runtime Mapping

Gateway runtime paling sering gagal karena variable contract tidak rapi.

```text id="0u7s9a"
Service Task: Determine Risk Band
  output:
    riskBand = HIGH

Gateway: riskBand?
  HIGH   → Senior Review
  MEDIUM → Officer Review
  LOW    → Auto Register
  default → Manual Triage
```

## Production rule

```text id="w1j95t"
Gateway must read variables that are:
  produced before the gateway
  typed predictably
  persisted if decision is audit-relevant
  covered by default path
```

## Bad

```text id="ky3jmt"
Gateway reads:
  status
```

Masalah:

```text id="7tfe2s"
Status apa?
Case status?
Payment status?
Validation status?
Review status?
```

## Good

```text id="9x1ly5"
validationStatus
riskBand
reviewDecision
paymentStatus
documentChecklistStatus
```

---

# 15. End-to-End Example: Case Intake Execution

## BPMN logical flow

```text id="sldw1m"
Start: Case Submitted
  ↓
Validate Case Data              service task
  ↓
Gateway: valid?
  ├── invalid → Reject Case
  └── valid
       ↓
     Check Duplicate Case        service task
       ↓
     Determine Risk Band         business rule task
       ↓
     Gateway: riskBand?
       ├── HIGH → Senior Officer Review user task
       └── LOW  → Register Case service task
       ↓
     Notify Applicant            send/service task
       ↓
End: Case Registered
```

## Runtime table

| BPMN Element | Runtime | Implementation |
|---|---|---|
| Start | process instance created | Jersey starts process with `caseId` |
| Validate Case Data | job | worker validates + persists result |
| Gateway valid? | FEEL expression | reads `validationStatus` |
| Check Duplicate | job | MyBatis query + duplicate result |
| Determine Risk Band | decision evaluation | DMN/business rule |
| Senior Review | user task | custom UI / task lifecycle |
| Register Case | job | update `case_record` guarded transition |
| Notify Applicant | job/outbox | insert outbox + notification worker |
| End | path/process complete | domain status already persisted |

---

# 16. PostgreSQL Runtime Support Tables

## A. Process instance projection

```sql id="tjxk2w"
CREATE TABLE workflow_process_instance_projection (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    business_key VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    bpmn_process_id VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    process_instance_key VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    process_version_tag VARCHAR(64),
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    last_known_element_id VARCHAR(128),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT uq_workflow_process_instance_key
        UNIQUE (process_instance_key),
    CONSTRAINT uq_workflow_business_process_active
        UNIQUE (business_key, bpmn_process_id)
);
```

Catatan: untuk multiple instances per business key, unique constraint ini harus disesuaikan. Jangan pakai constraint ini jika satu `caseId` memang boleh punya beberapa process aktif dari process type yang sama.

## B. Job execution audit

```sql id="nlui3o"
CREATE TABLE workflow_job_execution_log (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    process_instance_key VARCHAR(128),
    element_id VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    job_type VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    job_key VARCHAR(128),
    execution_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    error_message TEXT,
    input_ref JSONB,
    output_ref JSONB
);
```

---

## C. Message callback log

```sql id="os1qs9"
CREATE TABLE external_callback_log (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    message_name VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    correlation_key VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    external_reference_no VARCHAR(128),
    payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    correlation_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    correlated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    late_callback BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    error_message TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT uq_external_callback
        UNIQUE (message_name, correlation_key, external_reference_no)
);
```

## D. Incident mirror / operational log

```sql id="6cip7f"
CREATE TABLE workflow_incident_log (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64),
    process_instance_key VARCHAR(128),
    element_id VARCHAR(128),
    job_key VARCHAR(128),
    incident_type VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    incident_message TEXT,
    detected_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    resolved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    resolution_note TEXT,
    resolved_by VARCHAR(128)
);
```

# 17. MyBatis Patterns

## A. Guarded state transition

```xml id="7fjt5t"
<update id="markCaseRegistered">
  UPDATE case_record
  SET
      case_status = 'REGISTERED',
      registered_at = now(),
      updated_at = now(),
      version = version + 1
  WHERE case_id = #{caseId}
    AND case_status = 'APPROVED'
</update>
```

Worker harus cek affected row:

```java id="jbowy5"
int updated = caseRepository.markCaseRegistered(caseId);

if (updated == 0) {
    throw new IllegalStateException(
            "Case cannot be registered from current state: " + caseId
    );
}
```

---

## B. Idempotent outbox

```xml id="w1pgyn"
<insert id="insertOutboxEvent">
  INSERT INTO integration_outbox (
      aggregate_type,
      aggregate_id,
      event_type,
      idempotency_key,
      payload
  )
  VALUES (
      #{aggregateType},
      #{aggregateId},
      #{eventType},
      #{idempotencyKey},
      CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb)
  )
  ON CONFLICT (idempotency_key)
  DO NOTHING
</insert>
```

---

## C. Upsert callback

```xml id="jvvom0"
<insert id="upsertCallback">
  INSERT INTO external_callback_log (
      message_name,
      correlation_key,
      external_reference_no,
      payload,
      correlation_status
  )
  VALUES (
      #{messageName},
      #{correlationKey},
      #{externalReferenceNo},
      CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb),
      'PENDING'
  )
  ON CONFLICT (
      message_name,
      correlation_key,
      external_reference_no
  )
  DO UPDATE SET
      payload = EXCLUDED.payload,
      received_at = now()
</insert>
```

---

# 18. Idempotency Mapping

Idempotency wajib karena:

```text id="g7o9je"
worker can retry
callback can duplicate
client can retry REST request
message publish can repeat
incident recovery can re-run job
```

## Idempotency table

| Operation | Idempotency Key |
|---|---|
| Start case | `submit-case:{externalRequestId}` |
| Validate case | `validate-case:{caseId}` |
| Register case | `register-case:{caseId}` |
| Send notification | `notify:{caseId}:{template}:{recipient}` |
| Publish event | `event:{caseId}:CaseRegistered` |
| Callback | `callback:{messageName}:{correlationKey}:{externalReferenceNo}` |
| Compensation | `compensate:{caseId}:{action}` |

---

# 19. Versioning Mapping

## Process versioning concerns

```text id="b5vx4z"
BPMN process version
DMN decision version
Form version
Java worker application version
Database schema version
Message contract version
```

## Practical release rule

```text id="2nnchm"
Deploy BPMN/DMN/form, Java code, and DB migration as a compatible release set.
```

Bad:

```text id="2b8053"
BPMN v3 expects variable riskBand.
Worker v2 still outputs riskLevel.
Gateway fails or default path incorrectly taken.
```

Good:

```text id="c579tw"
BPMN v3:
  expects riskBand

Worker v3:
  outputs riskBand

Migration:
  persists risk_band

Tests:
  cover HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW/default
```

---

# 20. Worker Compatibility Checklist

Untuk setiap worker release:

```text id="y7qibn"
1. Job type masih sama?
2. Input variables masih kompatibel?
3. Output variables masih kompatibel?
4. Error codes masih sama?
5. BPMN gateway masih membaca variable yang benar?
6. DB migration sudah tersedia?
7. Idempotency key tidak berubah sembarangan?
8. External API contract berubah?
9. Retry policy berubah?
10. Observability fields masih lengkap?
```

---

# 21. Deployment Topology

## A. Embedded worker in same service

```text id="pamstr"
case-service
  ├── Jersey REST API
  ├── MyBatis repositories
  ├── Camunda client
  └── job workers
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="exmflr"
domain-owned tasks
small team
low operational complexity
same scaling profile
```

Risiko:

```text id="nd9lze"
REST API traffic dan worker traffic saling memengaruhi.
Scaling worker berarti scaling API juga.
```

---

## B. Separate worker service

```text id="c3ws7c"
case-api-service
  ├── Jersey REST API
  └── command endpoints

case-worker-service
  ├── Camunda job workers
  ├── MyBatis repositories
  └── external integrations
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="b9hdlm"
worker throughput tinggi
external integration berat
different scaling profile
clear runtime separation
```

---

## C. Per-domain worker

```text id="tsygoa"
case-worker
risk-worker
notification-worker
document-worker
payment-worker
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="g9rip6"
bounded context jelas
ownership berbeda
deployment independen
risk isolation
```

Risiko:

```text id="14so2h"
lebih banyak deployment
lebih banyak observability/runbook
contract antar worker/process harus disiplin
```

---

# 22. Kubernetes/Cloud/On-Prem Practical Notes

## Runtime concerns

```text id="sk5yux"
worker replicas
max jobs active
job timeout
retry backoff
connection pool
database transaction timeout
external API timeout
graceful shutdown
readiness/liveness probe
secret management
network policy
```

## Worker graceful shutdown

```text id="k8zuye"
On shutdown:
  stop accepting new jobs
  finish in-flight jobs if possible
  fail or let timeout jobs safely retry
  ensure DB transaction closed
  ensure idempotency protects re-run
```

## Scaling rule

```text id="m28smc"
Scale worker based on:
  job backlog
  average job duration
  external API capacity
  DB connection pool
  rate limits
```

Jangan hanya menaikkan replica kalau bottleneck sebenarnya PostgreSQL/external API.

# 23. Observability Mapping

## Required log fields

```text id="ryuzab"
caseId
processInstanceKey
bpmnProcessId
processVersion
elementId
elementName
jobType
jobKey
workerName
correlationKey
messageName
errorCode
incidentKey
durationMs
```

## Structured log example

```json id="wt2l10"
{
  "event": "workflow_job_completed",
  "caseId": "CASE-2026-001",
  "bpmnProcessId": "case-intake-process",
  "processInstanceKey": "2251799813685251",
  "elementId": "validate-case-data",
  "jobType": "validate-case-data",
  "jobKey": "2251799813690001",
  "durationMs": 184,
  "workerName": "case-worker-1"
}
```

## Metrics

| Metric | Meaning |
|---|---|
| active process instances | workload |
| jobs activated/completed/failed | worker health |
| incident count by element | process quality |
| user task age | SLA risk |
| callback pending count | integration backlog |
| timer timeout count | external/system delay |
| retry count | dependency health |
| worker duration p95/p99 | performance |
| DB transaction duration | persistence bottleneck |

# 24. Production Runbook by Runtime Object

## Process instance stuck

```text id="9r9tzf"
Check:
  active element
  waiting user task?
  waiting message?
  waiting timer?
  incident?
  gateway variable issue?

Actions:
  complete user task if valid
  correlate missing message
  update variable if wrong
  resolve incident
  migrate/terminate only with approval
```

## Job backlog high

```text id="h3uocr"
Check:
  worker replicas
  external dependency latency
  DB pool saturation
  job timeout
  max active jobs
  retry storm
  poison messages

Actions:
  scale worker
  reduce concurrency
  pause problematic worker
  fix downstream
  add circuit breaker/rate limit
```

## Callback not correlated

```text id="9v3up3"
Check:
  messageName
  correlationKey
  process still waiting?
  subscription opened?
  callback arrived too early?
  callback arrived too late?
  duplicate?
  wrong environment?

Actions:
  store as pending
  retry correlation
  mark late callback
  manual reconciliation
```

## Incident created

```text id="fa147c"
Check:
  failed job?
  expression error?
  decision error?
  timer expression error?
  uncaught BPMN error?

Actions:
  fix data/dependency
  update variables if required
  increase retries if safe
  resolve incident
  document RCA
```

---

# 25. Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Gejala | Dampak | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Worker tidak idempotent | Retry membuat duplicate side effect | Data corruption | idempotency key + unique constraint |
| Complete job sebelum DB commit | Process lanjut tapi data gagal persist | Inconsistent state | commit DB dulu, complete job setelahnya |
| Process variable jadi DB | Semua payload besar masuk variable | performance/security buruk | DB/object storage + variable reference |
| Callback langsung correlate tanpa persist | Correlation gagal → data hilang | no recovery | persist callback first |
| Job type generik | `process`, `service`, `task` | worker ambiguity | domain-specific job type |
| Retry semua exception sama | Business error retry terus | noise/incident | classify failure |
| User task completion tanpa auth | User bisa complete task salah | security incident | authorization by case/task/role |
| Incident manual resolve tanpa root cause | incident muncul ulang | operational churn | fix cause first |
| BPMN/worker version mismatch | variable tidak cocok | routing error/incident | compatible release set |
| No default gateway path | no-match incident | fragile process | default/manual triage |

---

# 26. Practical Review Checklist

Untuk setiap BPMN yang akan production, cek:

```text id="pxoykr"
1. bpmnProcessId jelas?
2. Process version/release tag jelas?
3. Start trigger jelas?
4. Business key/correlation key jelas?
5. Semua service task punya job type stabil?
6. Worker untuk setiap job type tersedia?
7. Worker input/output variables terdokumentasi?
8. Worker idempotent?
9. Retry policy jelas?
10. Business error vs technical failure dibedakan?
11. User task lifecycle jelas?
12. User task completion authorized?
13. Message event punya message name + correlation key?
14. Callback persisted before correlation?
15. Timer event punya business meaning?
16. Gateway punya default path?
17. Incident runbook tersedia?
18. Domain state persisted before process completion?
19. Observability fields lengkap?
20. Release compatibility BPMN/DMN/form/worker/DB sudah diuji?
```

---

# 27. Minimal Test Suite

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| Start process valid request | process instance created + domain case exists |
| Duplicate start request | no duplicate process/domain side effect |
| Service task success | DB updated + variables emitted |
| Service task transient failure | retries happen |
| Service task retries exhausted | incident created |
| Worker business exception | BPMN error boundary path |
| Gateway all known branches | correct route |
| Gateway unknown value | default/manual triage |
| User task complete authorized | review persisted + process continues |
| User task complete unauthorized | rejected, task remains |
| Message callback valid | callback stored + message correlated |
| Duplicate callback | no duplicate side effect |
| Late callback | marked late/reconciled |
| Timer timeout | timeout/escalation path |
| Deployment version mismatch | caught before production |
| Incident recovery | root cause fixed + resolve resumes process |

---

# 28. Ringkasan Seri 11

Inti praktis:

```text id="pdckjy"
Process definition = deployed blueprint
Process instance   = running business case
Service task       = job + worker
User task          = human lifecycle + task API/UI
Message event      = subscription + correlation key
Timer event        = engine-managed wait/deadline
Gateway            = variable-based/event-based routing
Incident           = runtime failure requiring resolution
Retry              = technical recovery mechanism
Versioning         = compatibility across BPMN/DMN/form/worker/DB
```

Rule paling penting:

```text id="iw4n9v"
BPMN runtime bukan hanya diagram.
Ia adalah distributed orchestration contract.
```

Untuk Java/Jersey/MyBatis/PostgreSQL:

```text id="2rso3m"
REST API starts/updates process by business command.
Workers execute automated tasks idempotently.
PostgreSQL stores authoritative business state.
Camunda variables carry orchestration context.
Callback endpoints persist inbound events before correlation.
User task APIs persist decisions before task completion.
Incidents require runbooks, not guesswork.
Deployment must version BPMN, DMN, forms, worker code, and DB migration together.
```

---

## Response:
7/16/2026, 10:02:20 AM

Lanjut ke **Seri 12/12 — Production BPMN Modeling Patterns**. Ini seri penutup: menggabungkan symbol reference menjadi checklist produksi, review governance, test scenario, anti-pattern cleanup, versioning, observability, dan runbook.

# Seri 12/12 - Production BPMN Modeling Patterns

## 1. Tujuan Seri Ini

Seri terakhir ini menggabungkan semua symbol reference menjadi **cara membaca, mendesain, menguji, dan mengoperasikan BPMN production-grade**.

Targetnya bukan lagi “tahu simbol”, tapi bisa menilai:

```text id="k7fd3u"
Apakah BPMN ini readable?
Apakah executable?
Apakah aman untuk production?
Apakah failure path jelas?
Apakah state/domain data konsisten?
Apakah bisa diaudit?
Apakah bisa dioperasikan saat incident?
Apakah aman saat versioning/migration?
```

Camunda sendiri menempatkan best practices BPMN/DMN sebagai kombinasi pengalaman konseptual dan implementasi praktis, bukan aturan absolut; artinya model production tetap harus disesuaikan dengan konteks domain, engine support, runtime topology, dan operational risk. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/best-practices/best-practices-overview/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Production Mental Model

BPMN production harus dibaca sebagai kombinasi dari:

```text id="6y1vwb"
state machine
+ orchestration contract
+ integration contract
+ human task lifecycle
+ failure model
+ audit model
+ operational runbook
```

Bukan sekadar:

```text id="7pqj0x"
diagram proses bisnis
```

Dalam Camunda, BPMN shape punya execution semantics; task, gateway, event, dan flow bukan hanya gambar, tapi mengontrol bagaimana token/runtime berjalan. ([Camunda](https://camunda.com/bpmn/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 3. Diagram Decomposition

```text id="m9iusm"
Production BPMN Modeling Patterns
│
├── 1. Model Readability
│   ├── Clear start/end
│   ├── Business-readable naming
│   ├── No hidden logic
│   ├── Appropriate level of detail
│   └── Diagram size control
│
├── 2. Execution Correctness
│   ├── Token flow
│   ├── Gateway correctness
│   ├── Join/split symmetry
│   ├── Message correlation
│   ├── Timer behavior
│   └── Boundary/event subprocess semantics
│
├── 3. Implementation Contract
│   ├── Job type
│   ├── Worker input/output
│   ├── User task form contract
│   ├── Message payload
│   ├── DMN contract
│   ├── Call activity contract
│   └── Variable catalog
│
├── 4. Data & State Management
│   ├── Business key
│   ├── Correlation key
│   ├── Process variable
│   ├── Domain DB state
│   ├── Audit tables
│   ├── Callback log
│   └── Outbox/idempotency
│
├── 5. Failure & Recovery
│   ├── Retry
│   ├── Incident
│   ├── BPMN error
│   ├── Escalation
│   ├── Timeout
│   ├── Compensation
│   └── Manual recovery
│
├── 6. Versioning & Migration
│   ├── BPMN version
│   ├── Worker compatibility
│   ├── DMN version
│   ├── Form version
│   ├── DB migration
│   ├── Message contract version
│   └── Running instance migration
│
├── 7. Observability & Operations
│   ├── Logs
│   ├── Metrics
│   ├── Traces
│   ├── Operate/monitoring
│   ├── Alerting
│   ├── Runbooks
│   └── RCA loop
│
└── 8. Governance
    ├── Modeling standards
    ├── Review checklist
    ├── Test scenarios
    ├── Release checklist
    ├── Migration approval
    ├── Audit defensibility
    └── Continuous improvement
```

---

# 4. Production BPMN Review Layers

Gunakan 7 layer review berikut saat membaca BPMN di kantor.

| Layer | Fokus | Pertanyaan Utama |
|---|---|---|
| 1. Business readability | Stakeholder paham atau tidak | Apakah proses bisa dijelaskan tanpa membuka source code? |
| 2. BPMN semantics | Simbol dipakai benar | Apakah gateway/event/task punya semantic yang tepat? |
| 3. Runtime execution | Bisa jalan di engine | Apakah elemen yang dipakai supported untuk execution? |
| 4. Implementation contract | Java/Jersey/worker jelas | Apakah job type, variable, form, message contract jelas? |
| 5. Data consistency | DB/process state selaras | Apakah authoritative state tersimpan di PostgreSQL? |
| 6. Failure recovery | Gagalnya jelas | Apakah retry, incident, error, timeout, escalation, compensation jelas? |
| 7. Operations | Bisa dioperasikan | Apakah ada observability, runbook, versioning, migration plan? |

Catatan penting: pada Camunda 8, BPMN coverage membedakan elemen yang didukung untuk modeling dan yang didukung untuk execution; DataObject dan DataStore, misalnya, disebut sebagai supported for modeling purposes only, jadi jangan mengandalkan artifact data sebagai runtime enforcement. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/bpmn-coverage/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. Naming Convention Production

## 5.1 Process Name

Baik:

```text id="zweem6"
case-intake-process
external-verification-process
risk-assessment-process
case-closure-process
complaint-resolution-process
```

Buruk:

```text id="phgbaw"
process1
main-flow
case-flow-new
workflow-final-v2
```

Rule:

```text id="lpe4fa"
Gunakan nama domain + outcome.
Jangan pakai nama temporer.
Jangan encode environment dalam process id.
```

---

## 5.2 Task Name

Gunakan format:

```text id="x3d0gi"
Verb + Object
```

Contoh:

```text id="cuoo7m"
Validate Case Data
Check Duplicate Case
Calculate Risk Score
Determine Risk Band
Review High Risk Case
Register Case
Notify Applicant
```

Hindari:

```text id="19unpi"
Do Validation
Process Data
Call API
Handle Stuff
Update
```

---

## 5.3 Gateway Name

Gateway sebaiknya berupa pertanyaan:

```text id="kq1q1i"
Case valid?
Duplicate found?
High risk?
Review approved?
Additional review required?
External response received before timeout?
```

Outgoing flow harus menjawab pertanyaan:

```text id="i7endn"
yes / no
valid / invalid
approved / rejected
high / medium / low
received / timeout
```

---

## 5.4 Event Name

Event adalah sesuatu yang terjadi.

Baik:

```text id="yj9taa"
Case Submitted
Registry Check Completed
Review SLA Reached
Cancellation Requested
Case Registered
Case Rejected
```

Buruk:

```text id="sov7ft"
Start
Message
Timer
Done
End
Callback
```

---

## 5.5 Job Type Name

Job type adalah kontrak worker.

Baik:

```text id="kpi4ko"
validate-case-data
check-duplicate-case
calculate-risk-score
register-case
send-case-notification
persist-review-result
```

Buruk:

```text id="uj5h1r"
job
worker
service
task1
process
api-call
```

Camunda job worker docs menekankan service task membutuhkan task type dan job worker yang menjalankan pekerjaan tersebut; jadi job type harus diperlakukan sebagai contract, bukan label random. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/best-practices/development/writing-good-workers/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 6. Pattern 1 - Happy Path + Explicit Exception Path

## Buruk

```text id="qoavy0"
Start
  ↓
Process Case
  ↓
End
```

Masalah:

```text id="t7lfkj"
Tidak jelas validasi.
Tidak jelas keputusan.
Tidak jelas failure.
Tidak jelas siapa yang bekerja.
Tidak jelas state akhir.
```

## Lebih baik

```text id="gk2nph"
Start: Case Submitted
  ↓
Validate Case Data
  ↓
Gateway: Case valid?
  ├── no  → Reject Case → End: Case Rejected
  └── yes
       ↓
     Check Duplicate Case
       ↓
     Gateway: Duplicate found?
       ├── yes → Merge Duplicate Case → End: Duplicate Merged
       └── no
            ↓
          Determine Risk Band
            ↓
          Gateway: High risk?
            ├── yes → Senior Officer Review
            └── no  → Register Case
```

Rule:

```text id="ssfcvv"
Happy path harus jelas.
Negative business outcome juga harus jelas.
Exception path tidak boleh disembunyikan dalam service task.
```

---

# 7. Pattern 2 - Business Decision Visible, Technical Detail Hidden

## Buruk

```text id="4q4r4b"
Service Task: Validate And Decide Everything
  ↓
End
```

Masalah:

```text id="m4lh9s"
Business rule tersembunyi dalam Java.
Sulit audit.
Sulit review business stakeholder.
```

## Lebih baik

```text id="et6cjl"
Validate Case Data              service task
  ↓
Calculate Risk Features         service task
  ↓
Determine Risk Band             business rule task / DMN
  ↓
Gateway: riskBand?
  ├── HIGH   → Senior Officer Review
  ├── MEDIUM → Officer Review
  └── LOW    → Auto Register
```

Rule:

```text id="8otbbg"
Business decision penting harus terlihat sebagai gateway/DMN/task eksplisit.
Technical implementation detail tetap di worker/service layer.
```

---

# 8. Pattern 3 - Async External Integration

## Production pattern

```text id="cydtbc"
Send External Verification Request
  ↓
Event-Based Gateway
  ├── Message: VerificationCompleted
  │       ↓
  │   Continue Processing
  │
  └── Timer: PT48H
          ↓
      Escalate Verification Timeout
```

Implementation contract:

```text id="smv6i9"
Message name:
  VerificationCompleted

Correlation key:
  caseId / verificationRequestId

Callback handling:
  persist callback first
  validate signature/auth
  correlate/publish message
  mark correlation status
```

Camunda supports process instance creation by command, message event, or timer event; message/timer semantics are part of runtime behavior, so async integration should be modeled explicitly rather than buried inside long-running Java threads. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/process-instance-creation/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 9. Pattern 4 - Human Task SLA

```text id="olxyx3"
User Task: Senior Officer Review
  │
  ├── Non-interrupting Timer: PT24H
  │       ↓
  │   Send Reminder
  │
  ├── Non-interrupting Timer: PT48H
  │       ↓
  │   Notify Supervisor
  │
  └── Interrupting Timer: PT72H
          ↓
      Reassign to Escalation Queue
```

Rule:

```text id="ea8q6i"
Reminder ≠ timeout.
Reminder biasanya non-interrupting.
Hard deadline biasanya interrupting.
```

DB support:

```sql id="m5npq5"
CREATE TABLE case_task_sla_event (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    task_name VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    sla_event_type VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    event_level VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
    triggered_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    payload JSONB,
    CONSTRAINT uq_case_task_sla_event UNIQUE (
        case_id,
        task_name,
        sla_event_type
    )
);
```

---

# 10. Pattern 5 - Incident Is Operational, Not Business Rejection

Camunda incident terjadi saat runtime tidak bisa melanjutkan karena problem, misalnya job failed tanpa retry tersisa, expression/timer/decision error, atau BPMN error tidak tertangkap. Resolusi incident biasanya membutuhkan root cause fix, update retry/variable bila aman, lalu resolve incident agar execution bisa lanjut. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/process-instance-migration/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Jangan lakukan ini

```text id="5ng3rt"
Case rejected → create incident
```

Rejection adalah outcome bisnis normal.

## Lakukan ini

```text id="2lavf9"
Gateway: reviewDecision?
  ├── APPROVED → Register Case
  └── REJECTED → Reject Case → End: Case Rejected
```

## Incident cocok untuk

```text id="1pp96w"
worker retry exhausted
bad variable shape
gateway expression error
missing required decision
unexpected technical failure
uncaught BPMN error
```

---

# 11. Pattern 6 - Retry vs BPMN Error vs Escalation

```text id="smwaz1"
Problem:
│
├── Technical transient?
│   └── fail job + retry
│
├── Technical permanent / unknown?
│   └── incident + runbook
│
├── Expected negative business result?
│   └── complete job + gateway
│
├── Business exception?
│   └── throw BPMN error + boundary handler
│
├── Needs business attention but can continue?
│   └── escalation
│
└── Completed side effect must be reversed?
    └── compensation
```

Camunda best practices distinguish technical failures from business exceptions: expected problematic results can be modeled as variables and routed by gateway, while BPMN errors are suitable when an activity cannot produce its expected result and the process needs a modeled reaction. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/best-practices/development/dealing-with-problems-and-exceptions/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 12. Pattern 7 - Process Variables Minimal, DB Authoritative

## Good split

```text id="72jnno"
Process variable:
  caseId
  riskBand
  reviewDecision
  caseStatus
  externalReferenceNo

PostgreSQL:
  case_record
  risk_assessment
  case_review
  external_callback_log
  integration_outbox
  compensation_action_log
```

Rule:

```text id="lscg0v"
Process variable = orchestration context.
PostgreSQL = authoritative business state.
Audit table = evidence.
```

## Bad

```text id="ngkwkq"
Process variable contains:
  full applicant payload
  full documents
  raw external API response
  access token
  huge JSON result
```

---

# 13. Pattern 8 - Outbox for Integration Events

Jika process menghasilkan event penting, jangan hanya publish langsung dari worker tanpa persistence.

## Recommended

```text id="zgbzlo"
Worker transaction:
  update domain state
  insert outbox event
  commit

Outbox publisher:
  publish event
  mark published
```

SQL:

```sql id="qaw2w2"
CREATE TABLE integration_outbox (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    aggregate_type VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    event_type VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(256) NOT NULL,
    payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    published_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    error_message TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT uq_integration_outbox_idempotency
        UNIQUE (idempotency_key)
);
```

---

# 14. Pattern 9 - Versioning as Release Contract

Production workflow versioning mencakup:

```text id="mpxhtz"
BPMN version
DMN decision version
Form version
Java worker version
REST/message contract version
Database migration version
Runbook version
```

Camunda has dedicated guidance on versioning process definitions, including the possibility that running instances may need migration when you do not want multiple model versions active at the same time. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/best-practices/operations/versioning-process-definitions/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Compatibility checklist

```text id="4b26xk"
BPMN vNext expects:
  riskBand

Worker vCurrent outputs:
  riskLevel

Result:
  gateway may fail or route incorrectly.
```

Fix:

```text id="vt4noz"
Deploy compatible set:
  BPMN vNext
  worker vNext
  DB migration
  DMN vNext
  tests
  rollback plan
```

---

# 15. Pattern 10 - Migration-Aware Modeling

Process instance migration can change inactive parts of a running instance; the active element is not arbitrarily reshaped, and migration requires mapping source and target elements. Camunda Operate also provides migration flow where selected process instances are moved to a target process version and source/target nodes are mapped. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/process-instance-migration/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Design implication

Jangan ubah BPMN sembarangan jika banyak instance masih aktif.

High-risk changes:

```text id="exufxl"
rename/remove active element IDs
remove user task where many instances wait
change message names
change correlation key variable
change gateway variable contract
change call activity output mapping
change error code names
```

Safer changes:

```text id="jslgnq"
add future path after inactive point
add optional field
add new branch with default fallback
add non-breaking variable
add new worker output without removing old one
```

---

# 16. Production BPMN Checklist

## 16.1 Process-level

```text id="t8iixc"
1. Process purpose jelas?
2. Process owner jelas?
3. Start trigger jelas?
4. End outcomes jelas?
5. Business key jelas?
6. Critical states tersimpan di DB?
7. Diagram readable dalam 5-10 menit?
8. Tidak ada task/gateway/event tanpa nama bermakna?
9. External participant jelas sebagai pool/message?
10. Scope process tidak terlalu luas?
```

---

## 16.2 Task-level

```text id="ds4z3n"
1. Setiap service task punya job type?
2. Setiap job type punya worker owner?
3. Input variable jelas?
4. Output variable jelas?
5. Retry policy jelas?
6. Timeout jelas?
7. Worker idempotent?
8. Domain transaction jelas?
9. Error behavior jelas?
10. Observability fields jelas?
```

---

## 16.3 User task-level

```text id="pzkuux"
1. Candidate group/assignee jelas?
2. Authorization rule jelas?
3. Form input/output jelas?
4. Due date/SLA jelas?
5. Reminder/escalation jelas?
6. Completion variables jelas?
7. Decision dipersist di DB?
8. Audit reviewer jelas?
9. Task cancellation behavior jelas?
10. Manual recovery path ada?
```

---

## 16.4 Gateway-level

```text id="to6kd5"
1. Gateway type tepat: XOR/AND/OR/event-based?
2. Gateway bukan task?
3. Variable/event input jelas?
4. XOR condition mutually exclusive?
5. XOR/OR punya default flow?
6. AND join tidak menunggu path yang tidak mungkin aktif?
7. OR combination sudah dites?
8. Event-based gateway punya timeout?
9. Gateway output path diberi nama?
10. Unknown value masuk safe fallback?
```

---

## 16.5 Event-level

```text id="nveh5o"
1. Message name jelas?
2. Correlation key stabil?
3. Callback persisted sebelum correlation?
4. Duplicate callback aman?
5. Late callback policy jelas?
6. Timer business meaning jelas?
7. Timer timezone/duration/cycle jelas?
8. Signal memang broadcast?
9. Conditional event tidak menyembunyikan integration?
10. End event mencerminkan business outcome?
```

---

## 16.6 Boundary/failure-level

```text id="n78f1q"
1. Boundary event ditempel ke scope yang benar?
2. Interrupting vs non-interrupting benar?
3. Error digunakan untuk business exception, bukan technical retry?
4. Escalation digunakan untuk business attention?
5. Compensation handler idempotent?
6. Terminate scope benar?
7. Incident runbook tersedia?
8. Manual recovery task jelas?
9. Timeout branch update domain state?
10. Failure path diaudit?
```

---

# 17. Testing Matrix Production

| Test Area | Scenario | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| Start | Valid request | Domain case created + process instance started |
| Start | Duplicate request | No duplicate case/process side effect |
| Service task | Success | DB updated + variables emitted |
| Service task | Transient DB/API failure | Job retry |
| Service task | Retry exhausted | Incident |
| Business result | Invalid case | Gateway routes to reject/manual review |
| BPMN error | Identity not resolvable | Error boundary recovery path |
| User task | Authorized complete | Decision persisted + task completed |
| User task | Unauthorized complete | API rejects + task remains active |
| SLA | Reminder timer fires | Reminder branch, task still active |
| SLA | Hard timeout fires | Task cancelled/escalated |
| Message | Valid callback | Callback logged + message correlated |
| Message | Duplicate callback | No duplicate side effect |
| Message | Late callback | Mark late + reconciliation path |
| Gateway | Unknown enum | Default/manual triage |
| Parallel | One branch fails | Retry/incident, join waits |
| Compensation | Reversal executed twice | Idempotent |
| Migration | Instance on old version | Migration plan maps active/inactive elements safely |
| Observability | Incident occurs | caseId/processInstance/jobType visible in logs/metrics |

---

# 18. Operational Runbook Template

Gunakan template ini untuk setiap process production.

```text id="d9gn96"
Process:
Owner:
Business key:
Critical variables:
Critical DB tables:
External dependencies:
Worker services:
User task groups:
Message names:
Timer/SLA rules:
Known incidents:
Manual recovery steps:
Compensation steps:
Migration policy:
Rollback policy:
Dashboard:
Alert rules:
Audit reports:
```

Contoh ringkas:

```text id="ev98sj"
Process:
  case-intake-process

Owner:
  Case Platform Team

Business key:
  caseId

Critical DB tables:
  case_record
  risk_assessment
  case_review
  external_callback_log
  integration_outbox

Worker services:
  case-worker
  risk-worker
  notification-worker

Message names:
  RegistryCheckCompleted
  AdditionalDocumentSubmitted
  CancelCaseRequested

Timers:
  Review reminder PT24H
  Review escalation PT72H
  Registry timeout PT48H

Known incident classes:
  worker retry exhausted
  missing variable riskBand
  callback unmatched
  DMN evaluation failure

Manual recovery:
  check callback log
  verify domain state
  update variable only with approval
  increase retries only if worker idempotent
  resolve incident after root cause fixed
```

---

# 19. Observability Dashboard

Minimum dashboard untuk production BPMN:

| Widget | Metric |
|---|---|
| Active instances by process/version | workload and version distribution |
| Incident count by element/job type | failure hotspot |
| Job backlog by job type | worker pressure |
| Job duration p95/p99 | performance |
| Retry count by worker | dependency health |
| User task age by group | SLA risk |
| Callback pending/unmatched/late | integration health |
| Timer timeout count | SLA/external delays |
| Process outcome distribution | business result |
| Compensation count | recovery frequency |
| Migration pending instances | version governance |

Structured log fields:

```text id="7hlkhx"
caseId
processInstanceKey
bpmnProcessId
processVersion
elementId
elementName
jobType
jobKey
messageName
correlationKey
errorCode
incidentKey
durationMs
workerName
```

---

# 20. BPMN Anti-Pattern Catalog

| Anti-Pattern | Gejala | Risiko | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Big black-box task | `Process Case` | Semua logic tersembunyi | Pecah jadi task/gateway/event bermakna |
| BPMN jadi code flow | Open connection, call mapper, commit | Terlalu teknis | BPMN di level business orchestration |
| Gateway sebagai work | `Gateway: Validate Case` | Semantic salah | Service task dulu, gateway baca hasil |
| No default gateway | No-match incident | Fragile | Default/manual triage |
| Message tanpa timeout | Instance menggantung | Operational debt | Timer/event-based gateway |
| Signal untuk targeted callback | Banyak process bisa trigger | Data corruption | Message + correlation key |
| User task tanpa SLA | Task menggantung | Aging backlog | Due date/timer boundary |
| Worker tidak idempotent | Retry side effect ganda | Data corruption | Unique key/idempotency |
| Process variable jadi DB | Payload besar/sensitif di engine | Security/performance | DB + references |
| Callback tidak dipersist | Correlation gagal → data hilang | No recovery | callback log |
| Business rejection jadi incident | Operational noise | Wrong semantics | gateway normal path |
| Technical retry dimodelkan semua di BPMN | Diagram noise | Hard to maintain | worker retry/incident |
| Compensation tidak idempotent | Reversal double-run | Data corruption | compensation log |
| Version mismatch | BPMN expects variable not produced | Runtime failure | compatible release set |
| No runbook | Incident ditangani ad hoc | Slow recovery | operational runbook |

---

# 21. Production Modeling Heuristics

## Heuristic 1 - Model business truth, hide technical plumbing

```text id="ug5ecz"
Visible:
  Validate Case
  Determine Risk Band
  Review High Risk Case
  Register Case

Hidden in implementation:
  DB connection
  mapper call
  HTTP serialization
  retry library
```

---

## Heuristic 2 - Every wait state needs an owner

Wait state:

```text id="wcfqqd"
User task
Receive task
Message catch
Timer wait
External callback
```

Owner questions:

```text id="bowugq"
Who is responsible?
How long can it wait?
What happens after SLA?
How is it monitored?
How is it recovered?
```

---

## Heuristic 3 - Every external call needs a failure policy

```text id="u6dcfm"
timeout
retry
circuit breaker
idempotency
callback handling
late response
manual reconciliation
```

---

## Heuristic 4 - Every business decision needs evidence

```text id="c0lcnu"
decision input
decision output
rule/model version
actor if human
timestamp
reason code
audit table
```

---

## Heuristic 5 - Every process version is a contract

```text id="4dfycl"
BPMN
DMN
worker code
form
message payload
DB schema
runbook
```

Release them as compatible units.

---

# 22. Final End-to-End Production Example

```text id="xk28sc"
Start: Case Submitted
  ↓
Validate Case Data                  service task
  ↓
Gateway: Case valid?
  ├── invalid
  │     ↓
  │   Reject Case
  │     ↓
  │   End: Case Rejected
  │
  └── valid
        ↓
      Check Duplicate Case           service task
        ↓
      Gateway: Duplicate found?
        ├── yes → Merge Duplicate → End: Duplicate Merged
        └── no
              ↓
            Send Registry Check Request
              ↓
            Event-Based Gateway
              ├── Message: RegistryCheckCompleted
              │       ↓
              │   Determine Risk Band       DMN/business rule
              │       ↓
              │   Gateway: riskBand?
              │       ├── HIGH → Senior Officer Review
              │       │             │
              │       │             ├── Timer PT24H reminder
              │       │             ├── Timer PT72H escalation
              │       │             └── review completed
              │       │                    ↓
              │       │                 Gateway: approved?
              │       │                    ├── no  → Reject Case
              │       │                    └── yes → Register Case
              │       │
              │       └── LOW → Register Case
              │
              └── Timer PT48H
                      ↓
                  Escalate Registry Timeout
```

Production support:

```text id="2xzv37"
case_record
risk_assessment
case_review
external_callback_log
case_task_sla_event
integration_outbox
workflow_incident_log
compensation_action_log
```

---

# 23. Final Review Checklist Before Production

```text id="efxyjq"
[ ] BPMN readable by business and engineering
[ ] Every start/end has business meaning
[ ] Every service task has job type and worker owner
[ ] Every user task has assignment, form, SLA, authorization
[ ] Every gateway has clear condition/default
[ ] Every message has name, correlation key, payload, timeout policy
[ ] Every timer has business meaning and timezone/duration/cycle clarity
[ ] Every external callback is persisted before correlation
[ ] Every worker is idempotent
[ ] Every domain state change is persisted before job/task completion
[ ] Every important decision has audit evidence
[ ] Every technical failure has retry/incident policy
[ ] Every business exception has error/escalation path
[ ] Every compensation handler is idempotent
[ ] Every process variable is documented
[ ] Large/sensitive data is not stored as process variable
[ ] BPMN/DMN/form/worker/DB/message contracts are version-compatible
[ ] Migration plan exists for running instances
[ ] Logs/metrics/traces include business key and process identifiers
[ ] Runbook exists for incident, callback failure, SLA breach, and migration
```

# 24. Ringkasan Seri 12

Inti praktis:

```text id="sxa6al"
Production BPMN = executable business state machine
                 + orchestration contract
                 + integration contract
                 + failure model
                 + audit model
                 + operational model
```

Rule paling penting:

```text id="o8qezb"
Diagram yang bagus bukan yang paling lengkap.
Diagram yang bagus adalah yang:
  readable,
  executable,
  testable,
  observable,
  recoverable,
  versionable,
  auditable.
```

Untuk stack kamu:

```text id="7fikbk"
BPMN/Camunda:
  orchestration and runtime state

Java/Jersey:
  API boundary, command handling, callback endpoint

MyBatis/PostgreSQL:
  authoritative state, audit, idempotency, outbox

Docker/Kubernetes:
  worker/runtime deployment and scaling

On-prem/AWS/Azure:
  operational topology, security, network, observability
```
