## Response:
7/16/2026, 10:04:00 AM

Saya akan buat **cheatsheet ringkas tapi production-ready** sebagai penutup seri BPMN Symbol Reference, dengan fokus ke pemakaian praktis di Camunda/Jersey/MyBatis/PostgreSQL dan checklist review.

# Cheatsheet - BPMN Symbol Reference + Camunda Production Modeling Review Checklist

BPMN adalah standar notasi grafis untuk memodelkan proses bisnis; tujuannya agar bisa dipahami business user sekaligus tetap merepresentasikan semantic proses yang cukup kompleks untuk technical user. ([OMG](https://www.omg.org/bpmn/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))  
Dalam konteks Camunda, jangan baca BPMN sebagai flowchart biasa. Baca sebagai **orchestration contract**: task menjadi pekerjaan, event menjadi trigger/wait/throw, gateway menjadi routing token, dan process variable menjadi runtime context.

---

# 1. Core Mental Model

```text
BPMN Model
  ↓ deploy
Process Definition
  ↓ start
Process Instance
  ↓ token flow
Task / Event / Gateway
  ↓
Worker / User Task / Message Subscription / Timer / Incident
```

| Konsep | Makna Praktis |
|---|---|
| Process Definition | Blueprint hasil deploy BPMN |
| Process Instance | Satu kasus/order/request yang sedang berjalan |
| Token | Posisi eksekusi dalam process |
| Task | Work unit |
| Event | Sesuatu yang terjadi, ditunggu, atau dilempar |
| Gateway | Routing token |
| Process Variable | Runtime context |
| Business Key | Identitas bisnis, misalnya `caseId` |
| Correlation Key | Key untuk message correlation |
| Incident | Runtime failure yang perlu recovery |

---

# 2. BPMN Symbol Quick Reference

## 2.1 Participants & Flow

| Symbol | Makna | Kapan Dipakai | Production Note |
|---|---|---|---|
| Pool | Participant/process boundary | External system, customer, vendor, process owner berbeda | Sequence flow tidak lintas pool |
| Lane | Responsibility partition dalam satu pool | Role/team/component internal | Lane bukan microservice/deployment boundary |
| Sequence Flow | Urutan eksekusi internal | Task A → Task B dalam satu pool | Solid arrow |
| Message Flow | Komunikasi antar participant | External callback, request/response antar pool | Dashed arrow |
| Association | Hubungan ke artifact/data/note | Data object, annotation, data store | Bukan control flow |

---

## 2.2 Activities / Tasks

Camunda menjelaskan service task sebagai work item dengan task type; saat dimasuki, job dibuat, process instance berhenti, worker mengambil job, lalu process lanjut setelah job complete. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/service-tasks/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Task Type | Makna | Mapping Camunda/Java | Kapan Dipakai |
|---|---|---|---|
| Generic Task | Work item belum diklasifikasi | Discovery/modeling awal | Jangan dibiarkan di executable BPMN |
| Service Task | Automated system work | Java worker by `jobType` | DB update, API call, validation, calculation |
| User Task | Human work assisted by engine/app | Task instance + UI/API | Approval, review, manual verification |
| Manual Task | Human work outside engine | Biasanya documentation/pass-through | Offline work yang tidak perlu tracking |
| Send Task | Mengirim message keluar | Worker publish event/email/API command | Notification, Kafka event, webhook |
| Receive Task | Menunggu message masuk | Message subscription | Callback async |
| Business Rule Task | Evaluasi rule/decision | DMN/rule evaluation | Risk band, eligibility, routing decision |
| Script Task | Expression/transformasi kecil | FEEL/script/worker tergantung config | Derive variable sederhana |

User task dipakai untuk pekerjaan manusia yang dibantu workflow engine/software; saat process mencapai user task, task instance dibuat dan process menunggu sampai task selesai. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/user-tasks/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2.3 Gateways

| Gateway | Alias | Makna | Gunakan Saat | Risiko |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Exclusive Gateway | XOR | Pilih tepat satu path | Valid/invalid, approve/reject, high/low risk | No default → incident/no-match |
| Parallel Gateway | AND | Jalankan semua path | Notify + audit + publish event | AND join setelah XOR bisa deadlock |
| Inclusive Gateway | OR | Jalankan satu atau lebih path | Multiple optional review | Kombinasi test meledak |
| Event-Based Gateway | Event race | Event pertama menang | Callback vs timeout | Butuh late callback policy |

Decision rule:

```text
Data decision, one path only      → XOR
Data decision, multiple paths     → OR
All paths must run                → AND
First event wins                  → Event-Based
Logic complex                     → DMN/service task first, gateway after
```

---

## 2.4 Events

Camunda menjelaskan event sebagai sesuatu yang terjadi; process bisa menangkap event atau melempar event. Catching message event, misalnya, membuat token lanjut saat message diterima. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Event | Makna | Praktisnya |
|---|---|---|
| None Start | Start eksplisit | REST/API starts process |
| Message Start | Message memulai process | External event creates instance |
| Timer Start | Jadwal memulai process | Daily/monthly batch |
| Message Catch | Menunggu message | Callback/correlation |
| Timer Catch | Menunggu waktu | Delay, SLA, timeout |
| Signal Catch | Menunggu broadcast | Emergency stop, policy update |
| Conditional Catch | Menunggu condition true | Variable-driven continuation |
| Message Throw | Mengirim message | Publish event/command |
| Error Event | Business exception | Error boundary/event subprocess |
| Escalation Event | Business attention | SLA warning, supervisor attention |
| Compensation Event | Undo/reversal | Refund, release, reverse |
| None End | Path selesai normal | Case completed/rejected |
| Terminate End | Stop semua active path dalam scope | Cancel/duplicate/emergency stop |

Message event digunakan untuk menunggu message yang tepat, cocok untuk relasi satu sender ke satu recipient/process instance; ini berbeda dari signal yang broadcast. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/message-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 2.5 Boundary Events

| Boundary Event | Interrupting? | Non-Interrupting? | Use Case |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Timer Boundary | Ya | Ya | Hard timeout / reminder |
| Message Boundary | Ya | Ya | Cancel/update saat activity aktif |
| Error Boundary | Ya | Tidak | Business exception recovery |
| Escalation Boundary | Ya | Ya | SLA warning / supervisor attention |
| Compensation Boundary | Special | N/A | Attach undo handler |
| Signal Boundary | Ya | Ya | Broadcast reaction |
| Conditional Boundary | Ya | Ya | Variable condition reaction |

Rule:

```text
Interrupting boundary:
  event terjadi → host activity dibatalkan

Non-interrupting boundary:
  event terjadi → side-flow berjalan, host activity tetap aktif
```

---

## 2.6 Data & Artifacts

Camunda 8 BPMN coverage membedakan elemen untuk modeling dan execution; beberapa artifact seperti data object/data store lebih tepat dibaca sebagai modeling/documentation artifact, bukan runtime enforcement. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/bpmn-coverage/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Artifact | Makna | Production Note |
|---|---|---|
| Data Object | Data/dokumen yang digunakan/dihasilkan | Bukan DB table |
| Data Store | Persistent repository secara konseptual | Detail schema tetap di design DB |
| Text Annotation | Catatan/penjelasan | Jangan simpan rule utama di annotation |
| Group | Pengelompokan visual | Bukan runtime scope |
| Association | Menghubungkan artifact ke element | Bukan sequence flow |

---

# 3. Camunda Runtime Mapping

| BPMN Element | Runtime Camunda | Java/Jersey/MyBatis Mapping |
|---|---|---|
| None Start | Start process instance | Jersey endpoint starts process |
| Service Task | Job | Java worker + MyBatis transaction |
| User Task | Task instance | Custom UI / Tasklist / completion API |
| Message Catch | Message subscription | Callback endpoint publishes/correlates message |
| Timer | Engine-managed wait | SLA/reminder/timeout path |
| Gateway | FEEL condition/event routing | Reads process variables |
| Error Boundary | Catches BPMN error | Worker throws BPMN error |
| Incident | Runtime failure | Operate/API/runbook recovery |
| End Event | Execution path ends | Domain status already persisted |

Incidents can be created when a job has no retries left, condition expression does not return boolean, timer expression has wrong type, decision cannot be evaluated, or BPMN error is thrown but not caught. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/incidents/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 4. Symbol Selection Decision Matrix

```text
Pekerjaan manusia?
  ├─ Need UI/task lifecycle      → User Task
  ├─ Offline only                → Manual Task
  └─ No
      ├─ Automated work          → Service Task
      ├─ Send message outward    → Send Task
      ├─ Wait inbound message    → Receive Task / Message Catch
      ├─ Evaluate decision       → Business Rule Task
      └─ Small transformation    → Script Task
```

```text
Routing?
  ├─ Exactly one path by data      → XOR
  ├─ All paths                     → AND
  ├─ One or more paths by data     → OR
  └─ First event wins              → Event-Based Gateway
```

```text
Exception?
  ├─ Expected negative business result     → Variable + Gateway
  ├─ Transient technical failure           → Retry
  ├─ Retry exhausted                       → Incident
  ├─ Business exception                    → BPMN Error
  ├─ Business attention                    → Escalation
  ├─ Undo completed action                 → Compensation
  └─ Stop all active work in scope         → Terminate
```

Camunda best practices distinguish expected problematic results from true exceptions: expected results can be modeled as variables/gateways, while BPMN error events are useful when a task cannot produce its expected result and the process needs a modeled reaction. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/best-practices/development/dealing-with-problems-and-exceptions/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. Production Modeling Patterns

## Pattern 1 - REST Starts Process

```text
POST /cases
  ↓
Validate API request
  ↓
Create domain case record
  ↓
Start process instance
  ↓
Return caseId + processInstanceKey
```

Checklist:

```text
[ ] idempotency key
[ ] domain record created first
[ ] process started with business key/caseId
[ ] start variables minimal
[ ] duplicate request safe
```

---

## Pattern 2 - Service Task Worker

```text
Service Task: Validate Case Data
jobType = validate-case-data
input   = caseId, caseType
output  = validationStatus, validationErrorCount
```

Worker rules:

```text
[ ] load domain state from DB
[ ] execute one clear business outcome
[ ] persist domain result
[ ] complete job with minimal variables
[ ] fail job for technical retry
[ ] throw BPMN error for business exception
[ ] idempotent on retry
```

---

## Pattern 3 - Async External Callback

```text
Send External Verification Request
  ↓
Event-Based Gateway
  ├── Message: VerificationCompleted
  └── Timer: PT48H
```

Message contract:

```text
messageName    = VerificationCompleted
correlationKey = caseId or verificationRequestId
payload        = status, referenceNo, checkedAt
```

Callback handling:

```text
1. authenticate/validate callback
2. persist callback idempotently
3. publish/correlate BPMN message
4. update correlation status
5. handle duplicate/late/unmatched callback
```

---

## Pattern 4 - User Task SLA

```text
User Task: Senior Officer Review
  ├── non-interrupting timer PT24H → Send Reminder
  ├── non-interrupting timer PT48H → Notify Supervisor
  └── interrupting timer PT72H     → Reassign/Escalate
```

Checklist:

```text
[ ] candidate group / assignee
[ ] authorization rule
[ ] form input/output
[ ] due date / SLA
[ ] reminder policy
[ ] final timeout behavior
[ ] decision persisted before task complete
```

---

## Pattern 5 - Business Error Recovery

```text
Service Task: Check Applicant Identity
  ├── success → Continue
  └── Error Boundary: IDENTITY_NOT_RESOLVABLE
          ↓
      Manual Identity Resolution
          ↓
      Gateway: resolved?
```

Use this when the task cannot produce the expected result because of a business exception. Do not use it for normal rejection or transient DB/API failures.

---

## Pattern 6 - Compensation

```text
Register Case
  └── Compensation Boundary → Undo Register Case

Later:
  Throw Compensation
    ↓
  Undo Register Case executed
```

Rules:

```text
[ ] compensation means business reversal, not DB rollback
[ ] handler must be idempotent
[ ] compensation action logged
[ ] external side effect reversal explicit
```

---

# 6. Variable / Data Contract Cheatsheet

## Process Variables

Keep small:

```text
caseId
caseType
validationStatus
duplicateFound
riskBand
reviewDecision
caseStatus
externalReferenceNo
```

Avoid:

```text
full document payload
raw external API response
access token
PII-heavy data
large JSON blob
debug trace
```

## Key Types

| Key | Purpose | Example |
|---|---|---|
| Business Key | Domain identity | `caseId` |
| Correlation Key | Match message to instance | `caseId`, `paymentInstructionId` |
| Idempotency Key | Prevent duplicate side effect | `notify:{caseId}:{template}` |
| External Reference | External system identity | `registryReferenceNo` |
| Process Instance Key | Engine runtime identity | Camunda-generated key |

## Contract Template

```text
Element:
Type:
BPMN id:
Input variables:
Output variables:
Business key:
Correlation key:
Idempotency key:
DB tables touched:
External dependency:
Retry policy:
Timeout:
Error behavior:
Audit evidence:
Owner:
```

---

# 7. PostgreSQL Support Tables

## Callback Log

```sql
CREATE TABLE external_callback_log (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    message_name VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    correlation_key VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    external_reference_no VARCHAR(128),
    payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    correlation_status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    correlated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    late_callback BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    error_message TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT uq_external_callback
        UNIQUE (message_name, correlation_key, external_reference_no)
);
```

## Outbox

```sql
CREATE TABLE integration_outbox (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    aggregate_type VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    event_type VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(256) NOT NULL,
    payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    published_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    error_message TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT uq_integration_outbox_idempotency
        UNIQUE (idempotency_key)
);
```

## Compensation Log

```sql
CREATE TABLE compensation_action_log (
    id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    case_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    original_action VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    compensation_action VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(256) NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    reason_code VARCHAR(128),
    requested_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    error_message TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT uq_compensation_idempotency UNIQUE (idempotency_key)
);
```

---

# 8. Production Review Checklist

## Process-Level

```text
[ ] Process purpose jelas
[ ] Process owner jelas
[ ] Start trigger jelas
[ ] End outcomes eksplisit
[ ] Business key jelas
[ ] Main happy path readable
[ ] Negative business outcomes terlihat
[ ] Exception path terlihat
[ ] External participants dipisahkan sebagai pool/message
[ ] Process tidak terlalu besar
```

## Task-Level

```text
[ ] Setiap service task punya jobType
[ ] Worker owner jelas
[ ] Input/output variable terdokumentasi
[ ] Retry policy jelas
[ ] Worker idempotent
[ ] DB transaction jelas
[ ] External dependency timeout jelas
[ ] Business error vs technical failure dibedakan
[ ] Output variable minimal
[ ] Observability field lengkap
```

## Gateway-Level

```text
[ ] Gateway bukan task
[ ] Gateway type tepat: XOR/AND/OR/Event-Based
[ ] Variable/event input jelas
[ ] XOR condition mutually exclusive
[ ] XOR/OR punya default path
[ ] AND split/join simetris
[ ] OR combinations sudah dites
[ ] Event-based gateway punya timeout
[ ] Unknown value masuk manual triage/safe fallback
```

## Event-Level

```text
[ ] Message name jelas
[ ] Correlation key stabil
[ ] Payload minimal
[ ] Callback persisted before correlation
[ ] Duplicate callback safe
[ ] Late callback policy jelas
[ ] Timer punya business meaning
[ ] Signal hanya untuk broadcast
[ ] Conditional event tidak menyembunyikan integration
[ ] End event mencerminkan business outcome
```

## User Task-Level

```text
[ ] Candidate group/assignee jelas
[ ] Authorization rule jelas
[ ] Form input/output jelas
[ ] SLA/due date jelas
[ ] Reminder/escalation jelas
[ ] Review/approval decision dipersist
[ ] Task completion tidak hanya mengandalkan frontend taskId
[ ] Cancellation/reassignment behavior jelas
```

## Data-Level

```text
[ ] Process variables kecil dan relevan
[ ] Sensitive/large payload tidak masuk variables
[ ] Domain DB adalah source of truth
[ ] Audit table untuk decision penting
[ ] Callback log tersedia
[ ] Outbox untuk outbound event penting
[ ] Idempotency key untuk retry/callback/publish
[ ] Variable/message contract versioned
```

## Operations-Level

```text
[ ] Incident runbook tersedia
[ ] Job backlog monitoring tersedia
[ ] User task aging dashboard tersedia
[ ] Callback pending/unmatched/late dashboard tersedia
[ ] Logs punya caseId + processInstanceKey + jobType
[ ] Metrics by process/version/element tersedia
[ ] Migration plan untuk running instances
[ ] Rollback/redeploy plan jelas
```

---

# 9. Anti-Pattern Catalog

| Anti-Pattern | Gejala | Risiko | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Big black-box task | `Process Case` | Logic tersembunyi | Pecah ke task/gateway/event |
| BPMN jadi code flow | Open DB connection, mapper call | Terlalu teknis | Naikkan ke orchestration level |
| Gateway sebagai task | `Gateway: Validate Data` | Semantic salah | Service task lalu gateway |
| No default gateway | No-match incident | Fragile | Default manual triage |
| Message tanpa timeout | Instance menggantung | Operational debt | Timer/event-based gateway |
| Signal untuk targeted callback | Banyak process trigger | Data corruption | Message + correlation key |
| User task tanpa SLA | Aging backlog | Stuck work | Timer boundary |
| Worker tidak idempotent | Retry duplicate side effect | Data corruption | Unique key/idempotency |
| Callback tidak dipersist | Correlation gagal → data hilang | No recovery | Callback log |
| Business rejection jadi incident | Operational noise | Wrong semantics | Normal gateway path |
| Process variable jadi DB | Payload besar/sensitif | Security/performance | DB + variable reference |
| Compensation tidak idempotent | Double reversal | Data corruption | Compensation log |
| Version mismatch | BPMN expects missing variable | Runtime failure | Compatible release set |

---

# 10. Minimal Production Test Matrix

| Area | Scenario | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| Start | Valid request | Case created + process started |
| Start | Duplicate request | No duplicate side effect |
| Service Task | Success | DB updated + variables emitted |
| Service Task | Transient failure | Retry |
| Service Task | Retry exhausted | Incident |
| Business Error | Known BPMN error | Boundary/event subprocess catches |
| Gateway | Known branches | Correct route |
| Gateway | Unknown enum/null | Default/manual triage |
| User Task | Authorized completion | Decision persisted + task completed |
| User Task | Unauthorized completion | Rejected, task remains |
| Message | Valid callback | Persisted + correlated |
| Message | Duplicate callback | Idempotent |
| Message | Late callback | Marked late/reconciled |
| Timer | Reminder | Side-flow, task remains |
| Timer | Hard timeout | Main activity cancelled/escalated |
| Compensation | Run twice | Safe/idempotent |
| Migration | Old running instance | Mapped safely or left on old version |

---

# 11. Final One-Page Mental Checklist

```text
BPMN production-ready if:

Readable:
  Business and engineering can understand it.

Executable:
  Symbols match Camunda runtime semantics.

Contracted:
  jobType, variables, message, form, DMN, call activity contracts exist.

Safe:
  retry, timeout, idempotency, compensation, incident handling are clear.

Consistent:
  PostgreSQL authoritative state aligns with process state.

Auditable:
  decisions, callbacks, escalations, errors, and outcomes are queryable.

Observable:
  caseId, processInstanceKey, elementId, jobType, messageName visible in logs/metrics.

Versionable:
  BPMN, DMN, form, worker, DB migration, and message contracts are compatible.
```

---

## Response:
7/16/2026, 10:06:40 AM

# Cheatsheet - Camunda 8 BPMN XML Properties + Modeler Properties Panel

Fokus cheatsheet ini: property yang paling sering kamu isi di **Camunda Modeler Properties Panel** dan bentuk XML-nya di BPMN Camunda 8. Baseline referensi di bawah mengikuti **Camunda 8.9 docs**; tetap cek versi runtime perusahaan karena beberapa property/fitur bisa berbeda antar minor version.

---

# 1. Namespace Minimal BPMN Camunda 8

Biasanya file BPMN Camunda 8 punya namespace seperti ini:

```xml
<bpmn:definitions
    xmlns:bpmn="http://www.omg.org/spec/BPMN/20100524/MODEL"
    xmlns:bpmndi="http://www.omg.org/spec/BPMN/20100524/DI"
    xmlns:dc="http://www.omg.org/spec/DD/20100524/DC"
    xmlns:di="http://www.omg.org/spec/DD/20100524/DI"
    xmlns:zeebe="http://camunda.org/schema/zeebe/1.0"
    xmlns:modeler="http://camunda.org/schema/modeler/1.0"
    id="Definitions_case_intake"
    targetNamespace="http://bpmn.io/schema/bpmn">

  <bpmn:process id="case-intake-process"
                name="Case Intake Process"
                isExecutable="true">
    ...
  </bpmn:process>
</bpmn:definitions>
```

Property penting:

| XML | Modeler Panel | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| `bpmn:process/@id` | Process ID | Stable contract; dipakai saat start process |
| `bpmn:process/@name` | Name | Human-readable |
| `bpmn:process/@isExecutable` | Executable | Harus `true` untuk executable process |
| `bpmn:*@id` | Element ID | Jangan sering rename kalau ada running instance/migration |
| `bpmn:*@name` | Name | Nama business-readable |

---

# 2. Service Task

Service task adalah automated work. Di Camunda 8, service task membutuhkan `zeebe:taskDefinition` dengan `type`; `type` adalah referensi job worker, dan `retries` menentukan retry saat worker melaporkan failure, default-nya 3. Service task juga mendukung task headers dan variable mappings. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/service-tasks/))

## Modeler Properties Panel

| Panel Field | XML | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Task definition / Type | `zeebe:taskDefinition/@type` | `validate-case-data` |
| Retries | `zeebe:taskDefinition/@retries` | `3` |
| Headers | `zeebe:taskHeaders/zeebe:header` | `operation=validate` |
| Inputs | `zeebe:ioMapping/zeebe:input` | `caseId` |
| Outputs | `zeebe:ioMapping/zeebe:output` | `validationStatus` |

## XML

```xml
<bpmn:serviceTask id="Activity_validate_case_data"
                  name="Validate Case Data">
  <bpmn:extensionElements>
    <zeebe:taskDefinition type="validate-case-data" retries="3" />

    <zeebe:taskHeaders>
      <zeebe:header key="operation" value="validate-case-data" />
      <zeebe:header key="owner" value="case-platform" />
    </zeebe:taskHeaders>

    <zeebe:ioMapping>
      <zeebe:input source="= caseId" target="caseId" />
      <zeebe:input source="= caseType" target="caseType" />
      <zeebe:output source="= validationStatus" target="validationStatus" />
      <zeebe:output source="= validationErrorCount" target="validationErrorCount" />
    </zeebe:ioMapping>
  </bpmn:extensionElements>
</bpmn:serviceTask>
```

## Production Notes

```text
job type = contract antara BPMN dan worker
input mapping = contract masuk worker
output mapping = contract keluar worker
headers = static metadata, bukan payload dinamis
```

Gunakan job type domain-specific:

```text
validate-case-data
check-duplicate-case
calculate-risk-score
register-case
send-case-notification
```

Hindari:

```text
task
worker
service
process
doStuff
```

---

# 3. Send Task / Message Throw Event

Send task di Camunda 8 didefinisikan seperti service task: ia butuh job type, worker yang subscribe, headers, dan variable mappings. Dokumentasi Camunda juga menyebut message throw/end event saat ini behave seperti service task/send task: event membuat job dan worker harus memprosesnya; Zeebe tidak otomatis “mengirim” message throw event ke message catch event. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/send-tasks/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Send Task XML

```xml
<bpmn:sendTask id="Activity_send_notification"
               name="Send Applicant Notification">
  <bpmn:extensionElements>
    <zeebe:taskDefinition type="send-notification" retries="3" />
    <zeebe:taskHeaders>
      <zeebe:header key="channel" value="email" />
      <zeebe:header key="template" value="case-submitted" />
    </zeebe:taskHeaders>
    <zeebe:ioMapping>
      <zeebe:input source="= caseId" target="caseId" />
      <zeebe:input source="= applicantEmail" target="recipient" />
      <zeebe:output source="= notificationRequestId" target="notificationRequestId" />
    </zeebe:ioMapping>
  </bpmn:extensionElements>
</bpmn:sendTask>
```

## Message Throw Event XML

```xml
<bpmn:intermediateThrowEvent id="Event_publish_case_registered"
                             name="Publish Case Registered">
  <bpmn:extensionElements>
    <zeebe:taskDefinition type="publish-case-event" retries="3" />
    <zeebe:taskHeaders>
      <zeebe:header key="eventType" value="CaseRegistered" />
      <zeebe:header key="topic" value="case.events" />
    </zeebe:taskHeaders>
  </bpmn:extensionElements>
  <bpmn:messageEventDefinition messageRef="Message_CaseRegistered" />
</bpmn:intermediateThrowEvent>
```

Production rule:

```text
Untuk event penting, worker sebaiknya insert outbox dulu, baru publish async.
```

---

# 4. User Task

Camunda user task memakai `zeebe:userTask` extension element. Ini adalah implementation type yang direkomendasikan sejak Camunda 8.6; alternatif job-worker-based user task dilakukan dengan menghapus `zeebe:userTask`, tetapi pendekatan itu deprecated dan kehilangan banyak fitur lifecycle/task management. User task mendukung assignment, scheduling, priority, variable mapping, forms, headers, dan task listeners. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/user-tasks/))

## Modeler Properties Panel

| Panel Field | XML | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Implementation | `zeebe:userTask` | Camunda user task |
| Assignee | `zeebe:assignmentDefinition/@assignee` | `= reviewerId` |
| Candidate users | `@candidateUsers` | `alice,bob` |
| Candidate groups | `@candidateGroups` | `senior-officer` |
| Due date | `zeebe:taskSchedule/@dueDate` | `= reviewDueDate` |
| Follow-up date | `@followUpDate` | `= now() + duration("P1D")` |
| Priority | `zeebe:priorityDefinition/@priority` | `80` |
| Form ID | `zeebe:formDefinition/@formId` | `case-review-form` |
| Form binding | `@bindingType` | `deployment`, `versionTag` |
| External form | `@externalReference` | `custom-case-review-ui` |
| Task listeners | `zeebe:taskListeners` | `assigning`, `completing` |

## XML - Camunda Form + Assignment + Schedule

```xml
<bpmn:userTask id="UserTask_senior_review"
               name="Senior Officer Review">
  <bpmn:extensionElements>
    <zeebe:formDefinition formId="case-review-form"
                          bindingType="deployment" />

    <zeebe:assignmentDefinition
        candidateGroups="senior-officer"
        assignee="= assignedReviewer" />

    <zeebe:taskSchedule
        dueDate="= reviewDueDate"
        followUpDate="= now() + duration(&#34;P1D&#34;)" />

    <zeebe:priorityDefinition priority="80" />

    <zeebe:ioMapping>
      <zeebe:input source="= caseId" target="caseId" />
      <zeebe:input source="= riskBand" target="riskBand" />
      <zeebe:output source="= reviewDecision" target="reviewDecision" />
      <zeebe:output source="= reviewComment" target="reviewComment" />
    </zeebe:ioMapping>

    <zeebe:userTask />
  </bpmn:extensionElements>
</bpmn:userTask>
```

## XML - Custom Form Reference

```xml
<bpmn:userTask id="UserTask_custom_review"
               name="Review Case in Custom UI">
  <bpmn:extensionElements>
    <zeebe:formDefinition externalReference="custom-case-review-ui" />
    <zeebe:assignmentDefinition candidateGroups="senior-officer" />
    <zeebe:userTask />
  </bpmn:extensionElements>
</bpmn:userTask>
```

## XML - User Task Listener

```xml
<bpmn:userTask id="UserTask_review" name="Review Case">
  <bpmn:extensionElements>
    <zeebe:taskListeners>
      <zeebe:taskListener eventType="assigning"
                          type="audit-user-task-assignment"
                          retries="3" />
      <zeebe:taskListener eventType="completing"
                          type="validate-review-completion"
                          retries="3" />
    </zeebe:taskListeners>
    <zeebe:userTask />
  </bpmn:extensionElements>
</bpmn:userTask>
```

Production notes:

```text
candidateGroups mulai Camunda 8.8 sebaiknya refer ke group ID, bukan group name.
assignee/candidateUsers/candidateGroups case-sensitive.
dueDate harus ISO 8601 datetime string dengan timezone jika static.
```

---

# 5. Business Rule Task / DMN

Business rule task biasanya mengevaluasi DMN decision. Property penting: `decisionId`, `resultVariable`, dan binding type. Binding type bisa `latest`, `deployment`, atau `versionTag`; default jika tidak ditentukan adalah `latest`. `resultVariable` adalah nama variable tempat hasil decision disimpan. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/business-rule-tasks/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Modeler Properties Panel

| Panel Field | XML | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Decision ID | `zeebe:calledDecision/@decisionId` | `determine-risk-band` |
| Result variable | `@resultVariable` | `riskDecisionResult` |
| Binding type | `@bindingType` | `deployment` |
| Version tag | `@versionTag` | `2026.07` |
| Inputs/Outputs | `zeebe:ioMapping` | map input/output |

## XML

```xml
<bpmn:businessRuleTask id="BusinessRule_determine_risk_band"
                       name="Determine Risk Band">
  <bpmn:extensionElements>
    <zeebe:calledDecision
        decisionId="determine-risk-band"
        resultVariable="riskDecisionResult"
        bindingType="deployment" />

    <zeebe:ioMapping>
      <zeebe:input source="= caseType" target="caseType" />
      <zeebe:input source="= duplicateFound" target="duplicateFound" />
      <zeebe:output source="= riskDecisionResult.riskBand" target="riskBand" />
      <zeebe:output source="= riskDecisionResult.riskScore" target="riskScore" />
    </zeebe:ioMapping>
  </bpmn:extensionElements>
</bpmn:businessRuleTask>
```

Production notes:

```text
latest      = praktis untuk dev, riskier untuk audit.
deployment  = process dan decision versi deploy yang sama.
versionTag  = controlled release by tag.
```

Untuk process regulatory/case management, prefer `deployment` atau `versionTag`.

---

# 6. Script Task

Script task bisa memakai FEEL expression atau job worker implementation. Untuk job-worker implementation, script task mendefinisikan job type sama seperti service task, headers bisa dipakai untuk static parameters, dan variable mappings sama seperti service task. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/script-tasks/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## XML - FEEL Expression Style

```xml
<bpmn:scriptTask id="Script_normalize_priority"
                 name="Normalize Priority">
  <bpmn:extensionElements>
    <zeebe:script expression="= if riskScore &gt;= 80 then &#34;HIGH&#34; else &#34;NORMAL&#34;"
                  resultVariable="priorityLabel" />
  </bpmn:extensionElements>
</bpmn:scriptTask>
```

## XML - Worker Style

```xml
<bpmn:scriptTask id="Script_transform_payload"
                 name="Transform Payload">
  <bpmn:extensionElements>
    <zeebe:taskDefinition type="transform-payload" retries="3" />
    <zeebe:taskHeaders>
      <zeebe:header key="scriptName" value="case-payload-normalizer" />
    </zeebe:taskHeaders>
  </bpmn:extensionElements>
</bpmn:scriptTask>
```

Production rule:

```text
Script task hanya untuk transformasi kecil.
Kalau logic butuh dependency, DB, API, unit test serius → service task.
```

---

# 7. Receive Task / Message Catch Event

Message correlation berbasis subscription dengan `message name` dan `correlation key`. Subscription dibuka saat process instance menunggu message; jika message name dan correlation key cocok, message dikorelasikan. Jika tidak ada subscription yang cocok, message bisa discarded; setelah subscription dibuka, correlation key tidak berubah walaupun variable referensi berubah. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/messages/))

## XML - Message Definition

```xml
<bpmn:message id="Message_RegistryCheckCompleted"
              name="RegistryCheckCompleted">
  <bpmn:extensionElements>
    <zeebe:subscription correlationKey="= caseId" />
  </bpmn:extensionElements>
</bpmn:message>
```

## XML - Intermediate Message Catch Event

```xml
<bpmn:intermediateCatchEvent id="Event_wait_registry_completed"
                             name="Registry Check Completed">
  <bpmn:messageEventDefinition messageRef="Message_RegistryCheckCompleted" />
</bpmn:intermediateCatchEvent>
```

## XML - Receive Task

```xml
<bpmn:receiveTask id="Receive_wait_registry_completed"
                  name="Wait Registry Check Completed">
  <bpmn:messageEventDefinition messageRef="Message_RegistryCheckCompleted" />
</bpmn:receiveTask>
```

## XML - Message Boundary Event

```xml
<bpmn:boundaryEvent id="Boundary_cancel_case"
                    name="Cancel Case Requested"
                    attachedToRef="Receive_wait_registry_completed">
  <bpmn:messageEventDefinition messageRef="Message_CancelCaseRequested" />
</bpmn:boundaryEvent>
```

Non-interrupting boundary:

```xml
<bpmn:boundaryEvent id="Boundary_additional_document"
                    name="Additional Document Submitted"
                    attachedToRef="UserTask_senior_review"
                    cancelActivity="false">
  <bpmn:messageEventDefinition messageRef="Message_AdditionalDocumentSubmitted" />
</bpmn:boundaryEvent>
```

Production checklist:

```text
messageName    = stable business event name
correlationKey = stable business key / interaction key
messageId      = use for idempotency when publishing
TTL            = use only when buffering is intended
callback log   = persist before correlation
```

---

# 8. Timer Events

Timer event bisa berupa date, duration, atau cycle. Timer start event menjadwalkan timer saat deployment; intermediate timer catch membuat process instance berhenti sampai timer triggered; interrupting timer boundary menghentikan activity saat timer triggered, sedangkan non-interrupting timer boundary menjalankan side-flow dan activity tetap aktif. Timer tidak dijamin trigger tepat di waktu configured; bisa lebih lambat tergantung load, tapi tidak lebih awal. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/timer-events/))

## XML - Timer Start Date

```xml
<bpmn:startEvent id="Start_sla_sweep" name="Daily SLA Sweep">
  <bpmn:timerEventDefinition>
    <bpmn:timeCycle>0 0 2 * * *</bpmn:timeCycle>
  </bpmn:timerEventDefinition>
</bpmn:startEvent>
```

## XML - Intermediate Duration

```xml
<bpmn:intermediateCatchEvent id="Timer_wait_24h"
                             name="Wait 24 Hours">
  <bpmn:timerEventDefinition>
    <bpmn:timeDuration>PT24H</bpmn:timeDuration>
  </bpmn:timerEventDefinition>
</bpmn:intermediateCatchEvent>
```

## XML - Interrupting Boundary Timer

```xml
<bpmn:boundaryEvent id="Boundary_review_timeout"
                    name="Review Timeout"
                    attachedToRef="UserTask_senior_review">
  <bpmn:timerEventDefinition>
    <bpmn:timeDuration>PT72H</bpmn:timeDuration>
  </bpmn:timerEventDefinition>
</bpmn:boundaryEvent>
```

## XML - Non-Interrupting Repeating Reminder

```xml
<bpmn:boundaryEvent id="Boundary_review_reminder"
                    name="Review Reminder"
                    attachedToRef="UserTask_senior_review"
                    cancelActivity="false">
  <bpmn:timerEventDefinition>
    <bpmn:timeCycle>R3/PT24H</bpmn:timeCycle>
  </bpmn:timerEventDefinition>
</bpmn:boundaryEvent>
```

Timer values:

```text
timeDate      = 2026-07-20T09:00:00+07:00
timeDuration  = PT24H, P3D, PT15M
timeCycle     = R3/PT24H, R/P1D, cron expression
```

---

# 9. Error Events

Error event memakai `errorCode`; thrown error dicocokkan ke error boundary event atau error event subprocess. Worker juga bisa throw BPMN error saat memproses job. Jika error tidak tertangkap, incident dibuat. Error catch event harus static errorCode; catch-all bisa omit error reference. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/error-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## XML - Error Definition

```xml
<bpmn:error id="Error_IdentityNotResolvable"
            name="Identity Not Resolvable"
            errorCode="IDENTITY_NOT_RESOLVABLE" />
```

## XML - Error Boundary

```xml
<bpmn:boundaryEvent id="Boundary_identity_not_resolvable"
                    name="Identity Not Resolvable"
                    attachedToRef="Activity_check_identity">
  <bpmn:errorEventDefinition errorRef="Error_IdentityNotResolvable" />
</bpmn:boundaryEvent>
```

## XML - Error End

```xml
<bpmn:endEvent id="End_invalid_identity"
               name="Invalid Identity">
  <bpmn:errorEventDefinition errorRef="Error_IdentityNotResolvable" />
</bpmn:endEvent>
```

## Java Worker Throw Error

```java
client.newThrowErrorCommand(job)
        .errorCode("IDENTITY_NOT_RESOLVABLE")
        .errorMessage("Applicant identity cannot be resolved")
        .variables(Map.of(
                "identityCheckStatus", "NOT_RESOLVABLE",
                "identityFailureReason", reasonCode
        ))
        .send()
        .join();
```

Decision rule:

```text
expected negative result → complete job + gateway
business exception       → throw BPMN error
technical transient      → fail job + retry
```

---

# 10. Escalation Events

Escalation is non-critical and communicates to a higher flow scope. It uses `escalationCode`; throw events can use static value/expression, catch events need static value or catch-all. Escalation can be thrown by intermediate throw or end event and caught by boundary event or event subprocess. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/escalation-events/))

## XML - Escalation Definition

```xml
<bpmn:escalation id="Escalation_ReviewSlaWarning"
                 name="Review SLA Warning"
                 escalationCode="REVIEW_SLA_WARNING" />
```

## XML - Intermediate Escalation Throw

```xml
<bpmn:intermediateThrowEvent id="Throw_review_sla_warning"
                             name="Review SLA Warning">
  <bpmn:escalationEventDefinition escalationRef="Escalation_ReviewSlaWarning" />
</bpmn:intermediateThrowEvent>
```

## XML - Escalation Boundary

```xml
<bpmn:boundaryEvent id="Boundary_review_escalation"
                    name="Review Escalation"
                    attachedToRef="Subprocess_manual_review"
                    cancelActivity="false">
  <bpmn:escalationEventDefinition escalationRef="Escalation_ReviewSlaWarning" />
</bpmn:boundaryEvent>
```

Production rule:

```text
Error       = normal path cannot continue.
Escalation  = process can continue, but attention/handler is needed.
```

---

# 11. Call Activity

Call activity memanggil externalized BPMN process. Property utama: `processId`, `bindingType`, `versionTag`, dan variable propagation. Binding type: `latest`, `deployment`, atau `versionTag`; default jika tidak diset adalah `latest`. Call activity membuat child process instance, parent menunggu sampai child selesai. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/call-activities/))

## Modeler Properties Panel

| Panel Field | XML | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Called process / Process ID | `zeebe:calledElement/@processId` | `risk-assessment-process` |
| Binding | `@bindingType` | `deployment`, `versionTag` |
| Version tag | `@versionTag` | `2026.07` |
| Propagate all parent variables | `@propagateAllParentVariables` | `false` |
| Propagate all child variables | `@propagateAllChildVariables` | `false` |
| Input/Output mappings | `zeebe:ioMapping` | explicit contract |

## XML - Recommended Contract-Based

```xml
<bpmn:callActivity id="Call_risk_assessment"
                   name="Run Risk Assessment">
  <bpmn:extensionElements>
    <zeebe:calledElement
        processId="risk-assessment-process"
        bindingType="versionTag"
        versionTag="2026.07"
        propagateAllParentVariables="false"
        propagateAllChildVariables="false" />

    <zeebe:ioMapping>
      <zeebe:input source="= caseId" target="caseId" />
      <zeebe:input source="= applicantId" target="applicantId" />
      <zeebe:input source="= caseType" target="caseType" />

      <zeebe:output source="= riskScore" target="riskScore" />
      <zeebe:output source="= riskBand" target="riskBand" />
      <zeebe:output source="= riskAssessmentId" target="riskAssessmentId" />
    </zeebe:ioMapping>
  </bpmn:extensionElements>
</bpmn:callActivity>
```

Production rule:

```text
Jangan propagate all variables secara default untuk process kritikal.
Treat call activity seperti API contract: explicit input/output.
```

---

# 12. Gateways & Sequence Flow Conditions

## Exclusive Gateway XML

```xml
<bpmn:exclusiveGateway id="Gateway_risk_band"
                       name="Risk Band?" />

<bpmn:sequenceFlow id="Flow_high_risk"
                   sourceRef="Gateway_risk_band"
                   targetRef="UserTask_senior_review"
                   name="HIGH">
  <bpmn:conditionExpression xsi:type="bpmn:tFormalExpression">
    = riskBand = "HIGH"
  </bpmn:conditionExpression>
</bpmn:sequenceFlow>

<bpmn:sequenceFlow id="Flow_default_risk"
                   sourceRef="Gateway_risk_band"
                   targetRef="UserTask_manual_triage"
                   name="default" />

<!-- gateway default attribute is normally on the gateway -->
<bpmn:exclusiveGateway id="Gateway_risk_band"
                       name="Risk Band?"
                       default="Flow_default_risk" />
```

Practical notes:

```text
condition expression harus boolean
default flow harus diset di gateway, bukan hanya sequence flow tanpa condition
XOR conditions harus mutually exclusive
```

---

# 13. Multi-Instance Marker

Useful untuk batch/list processing, misalnya review multiple documents.

## XML - Parallel Multi-Instance

```xml
<bpmn:serviceTask id="Activity_validate_document"
                  name="Validate Document">
  <bpmn:extensionElements>
    <zeebe:taskDefinition type="validate-document" retries="3" />
  </bpmn:extensionElements>

  <bpmn:multiInstanceLoopCharacteristics isSequential="false">
    <bpmn:extensionElements>
      <zeebe:loopCharacteristics
          inputCollection="= documents"
          inputElement="document"
          outputCollection="validatedDocuments"
          outputElement="= validationResult" />
    </bpmn:extensionElements>
  </bpmn:multiInstanceLoopCharacteristics>
</bpmn:serviceTask>
```

Production notes:

```text
parallel multi-instance → hati-hati race condition variable
gunakan local variables dan output collection
worker harus idempotent per item
```

---

# 14. Execution Listener

Execution listeners bisa dikonfigurasi pada BPMN element untuk start/end event lifecycle. Camunda docs menyebut listener bisa diset pada tasks, events, gateways, process, dan subprocess; listener punya event type `start` atau `end`, `type`, dan retries. ([Camunda 8 Docs](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/execution-listeners/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## XML

```xml
<bpmn:serviceTask id="Activity_register_case"
                  name="Register Case">
  <bpmn:extensionElements>
    <zeebe:taskDefinition type="register-case" retries="3" />

    <zeebe:executionListeners>
      <zeebe:executionListener eventType="start"
                               type="audit-element-start"
                               retries="3" />
      <zeebe:executionListener eventType="end"
                               type="audit-element-end"
                               retries="3" />
    </zeebe:executionListeners>
  </bpmn:extensionElements>
</bpmn:serviceTask>
```

Production warning:

```text
Jangan jadikan listener sebagai tempat business logic utama.
Gunakan untuk audit ringan, precondition, cleanup, atau instrumentation.
```

---

# 15. End-to-End XML Mini Example

```xml
<bpmn:process id="case-intake-process"
              name="Case Intake Process"
              isExecutable="true">

  <bpmn:startEvent id="Start_case_submitted"
                   name="Case Submitted" />

  <bpmn:serviceTask id="Activity_validate_case"
                    name="Validate Case Data">
    <bpmn:extensionElements>
      <zeebe:taskDefinition type="validate-case-data" retries="3" />
      <zeebe:ioMapping>
        <zeebe:input source="= caseId" target="caseId" />
        <zeebe:input source="= caseType" target="caseType" />
        <zeebe:output source="= validationStatus" target="validationStatus" />
      </zeebe:ioMapping>
    </bpmn:extensionElements>
  </bpmn:serviceTask>

  <bpmn:exclusiveGateway id="Gateway_case_valid"
                         name="Case Valid?"
                         default="Flow_invalid_default" />

  <bpmn:sequenceFlow id="Flow_valid"
                     sourceRef="Gateway_case_valid"
                     targetRef="Activity_determine_risk"
                     name="valid">
    <bpmn:conditionExpression xsi:type="bpmn:tFormalExpression">
      = validationStatus = "VALID"
    </bpmn:conditionExpression>
  </bpmn:sequenceFlow>

  <bpmn:sequenceFlow id="Flow_invalid_default"
                     sourceRef="Gateway_case_valid"
                     targetRef="Activity_reject_case"
                     name="invalid/default" />

  <bpmn:businessRuleTask id="Activity_determine_risk"
                         name="Determine Risk Band">
    <bpmn:extensionElements>
      <zeebe:calledDecision decisionId="determine-risk-band"
                            resultVariable="riskResult"
                            bindingType="deployment" />
      <zeebe:ioMapping>
        <zeebe:output source="= riskResult.riskBand" target="riskBand" />
      </zeebe:ioMapping>
    </bpmn:extensionElements>
  </bpmn:businessRuleTask>

  <bpmn:userTask id="UserTask_senior_review"
                 name="Senior Officer Review">
    <bpmn:extensionElements>
      <zeebe:formDefinition formId="case-review-form"
                            bindingType="deployment" />
      <zeebe:assignmentDefinition candidateGroups="senior-officer" />
      <zeebe:taskSchedule dueDate="= reviewDueDate" />
      <zeebe:userTask />
    </bpmn:extensionElements>
  </bpmn:userTask>

  <bpmn:endEvent id="End_case_registered"
                 name="Case Registered" />
</bpmn:process>
```

Catatan: snippet di atas fokus property extension. Diagram lengkap tetap butuh `sequenceFlow` lengkap dan BPMN DI (`bpmndi`) jika dibuka rapi di modeler.

---

# 16. Properties Panel Review Checklist

Saat buka BPMN di Modeler, cek ini satu per satu.

## Process

```text
[ ] Process ID stable dan domain-specific
[ ] Name human-readable
[ ] isExecutable = true
[ ] Version/tag strategy jelas
```

## Service/Send/Script Worker Task

```text
[ ] taskDefinition.type ada
[ ] retries diset sesuai risk
[ ] headers tidak menyimpan secret
[ ] input mapping minimal
[ ] output mapping eksplisit
[ ] job type punya worker owner
```

## User Task

```text
[ ] zeebe:userTask ada untuk Camunda user task
[ ] assignee/candidateGroups/candidateUsers benar
[ ] group ID sesuai Identity
[ ] dueDate/followUpDate sesuai SLA
[ ] formDefinition bindingType tidak asal latest
[ ] completion variables dipersist ke DB di application layer
```

## Message

```text
[ ] message name business-readable
[ ] correlationKey expression benar
[ ] correlation key tersedia sebelum subscription aktif
[ ] message start idempotency strategy jelas
[ ] publish/correlate endpoint choice jelas
```

## Timer

```text
[ ] timeDate/timeDuration/timeCycle valid
[ ] timezone jelas untuk timeDate
[ ] interrupting vs non-interrupting benar
[ ] repeating timer punya batas atau policy
[ ] timeout branch update domain state
```

## Error/Escalation

```text
[ ] errorCode/escalationCode spesifik
[ ] error untuk business exception, bukan normal rejection
[ ] escalation untuk attention, bukan technical retry
[ ] catch handler ada dan jelas
```

## Call Activity

```text
[ ] processId benar
[ ] bindingType production-safe: deployment/versionTag
[ ] propagateAllParentVariables=false jika perlu isolation
[ ] propagateAllChildVariables=false jika perlu output contract
[ ] input/output mapping eksplisit
```

---

# 17. Anti-Pattern XML/Properties

| Anti-Pattern | Contoh | Risiko | Perbaikan |
|---|---|---|---|
| Job type generik | `type="service"` | Worker ambiguity | `validate-case-data` |
| Retry default tanpa sadar | omit retries | Retry policy tidak intentional | Set explicit retries |
| Header berisi secret | `apiKey=...` | Secret leak | Secret manager/env |
| No output mapping | worker output semua variables | variable bloat/leak | Explicit output |
| User task tanpa `zeebe:userTask` | fallback job-worker implementation | kehilangan user task lifecycle | Tambahkan `zeebe:userTask` |
| Candidate group by display name | `Senior Officer` | mismatch identity | group ID |
| Message correlation key mutable | `= email` | wrong correlation | `= caseId` |
| Timer date tanpa timezone | ambiguous | SLA salah | ISO datetime dengan offset/Z |
| Call activity latest default | omitted binding | child version berubah tidak terkontrol | `deployment`/`versionTag` |
| Propagate all variables | default all variables | collision/security issue | explicit mappings |

---

# 18. Production Defaults yang Saya Rekomendasikan

```text
Service task:
  type       = domain-specific kebab-case
  retries    = explicit
  input      = minimal
  output     = explicit

User task:
  implementation = Camunda user task
  candidateGroups = group ID
  form binding = deployment/versionTag
  dueDate/followUpDate = expression from domain SLA

Message:
  name = past-tense business event
  correlationKey = immutable business key
  messageId = externalReference/idempotency key if available

Timer:
  duration/date/cycle = explicit
  hard timeout = interrupting
  reminder = non-interrupting with limit

Business rule:
  bindingType = deployment/versionTag
  resultVariable = explicit
  output mapping = stable variables consumed by gateway

Call activity:
  bindingType = deployment/versionTag
  propagate all = false for critical reusable workflows
  input/output = explicit contract

Error:
  errorCode = uppercase domain code
  catch path = manual recovery/reject/compensate

Escalation:
  escalationCode = uppercase business attention code
  non-interrupting unless takeover is required
```
